#+TITLE: Honey Service Lane: Telegram Claude Bots as agent-claude
#+DESCRIPTION: Service lane for Honey's (beelink) tanuki bots, run as agent-claude from a root-owned system checkout: honey-opus/sonnet/haiku launchers, Telegram preflight, OpenRC service body and installer, boundary law
#+AUTHOR: the Honey operator (the Honey operator), for Mètsàtron's DotCortex
#+LANGUAGE: en
#+PROPERTY: header-args :mkdirp yes :results silent
#+PROPERTY: header-args:bash :comments no
#+PROPERTY: header-args:python :comments no
#+PROPERTY: header-args:text :comments no

* Why

Honey (hostname =beelink=) is the operator's machine and runs her clinical stack. Mètsàtron wants three Telegram Claude bots there that work like the kikin ones: one append-only =claude-warm= session each, compacted in idle windows, so the prompt cache survives between turns. His ruling (2026-09-25, "Option A"): they run as a Unix user other than =gille=, so the clinical boundary is real file permissions (the bot user cannot enter =/home/gille= or read the Secret Vault), and they log in with the operator's Claude account.

*Revised 2026-09-26 (his ruling): the bots run as =agent-claude=*, the canonical Claude agent user (uid 2002, [[file:agents-users.org][agents-users.org]]), mirroring the agent-user pattern rather than his own =metsatron= account. =agent-claude= is provisioned like the other agent users: node from its own Guix profile (=agent-guix-apply=, Guix bootstrapped on Honey by =guix-foreign-bootstrap=, [[file:package-guix.org][package-guix.org]]), =claude= and =bun= in its own =~/.npm-global= (=agent-cli-install=), its own anonymous DotCortex checkout at =~/DotCortex= (until the service-lane switchover below, the OpenRC services run the launchers from there, since an agent account is not stowed), and a copy of the operator's Claude credential (his choice, 2026-09-26, over a separate =setup-token=; the known cost is that both copies share one refresh token). The =metsatron= account work below (stow, npm lane, guard bindings) stays for his own account on Honey.

The kikin launchers (=HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/claude-{fable,opus,haiku}=) are bound to HelmCortex paths Honey does not have, so this module ports their behaviour into a *service lane* ([[*Service lane: a root-owned system checkout][below]]):

| Persona   | Service / launcher | Telegram            | Model (default)             | Env override         |
|-----------+--------------------+---------------------+-----------------------------+----------------------|
| Bunta     | =honey-opus=       | =@honey_opus_bot=   | =claude-opus-4-8[1m]=       | =HONEY_OPUS_MODEL=   |
| Sasuke    | =honey-sonnet=     | =@honey_sonnet_bot= | =claude-sonnet-5-5=         | =HONEY_SONNET_MODEL= |
| Shoukichi | =honey-haiku=      | =@honey_haiku_bot=  | =claude-haiku-5-5= | =HONEY_HAIKU_MODEL=  |

Mètsàtron named the three (2026-09-26); Sasuke rode Sonnet 4.6 until Mètsàtron moved him to Sonnet 5.5 (2026-09-30). On kikin a bot carries its persona in two places: its brain's =CLAUDE.md= (=# CLAUDE.md - <Name>=, =## Identity=, =## Telegram=) and a consort row in =all/.config/agent-session/scope-gazetteer.json=. The gazetteer is sealed and each row carries a partner chosen by ceremony, so this module does *not* add rows; the three rows wait for Mètsàtron. The identity lives in each bot's working-directory =CLAUDE.md= (below).

Everything lives in one lane directory, =all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/= (=<lane>= below), which =all/.stow-local-ignore= keeps out of every home: nothing of it is stowed, on Honey or anywhere else.

- =<lane>/bin/honey-{opus,sonnet,haiku}=: the launchers (plus the voice helpers).
- =<lane>/lib/=: the shared launcher library and the Telegram preflight (a port of kikin's =claude-telegram-preflight.bash=).
- =<lane>/openrc/honey-bot=: the one OpenRC body the three services share, and =<lane>/openrc/honey-claude-install=, the root installer.
- =<lane>/SPARSE=: the paths the host's sparse system checkout carries (below).
- =<lane>/CLAUDE.{opus,sonnet,haiku}.md=: each bot's identity plus the Honey boundary law, copied into its working directory =~/honey-work/<bot>/CLAUDE.md=.
- =<lane>/lib/preservation.bash=: the operator's usage guard (below).

Until 2026-09-28 the launchers sat in the =honey/= host overlay, so stowing any Honey account put =honey-opus= and friends on its PATH although only =agent-claude= runs them. The =honey= row in =hosts.ssv= is =active= for =metsatron= only ([[file:layers.org][layers.org]]); the operator's row stays =planned=. With the lane moved out, =honey/= holds only its scope marker ([[file:package-host-excludes.org][package-host-excludes.org]]). =claude= and =bun= come from metsatron's own npm lane ([[file:package-npm.org][package-npm.org]], "Account lane: metsatron on Honey"). That lane needs Node >= 22, which in this fleet comes from Guix, and Honey has no Guix yet: the proposed route is in [[file:package-host-gates.org][package-host-gates.org]] ("Honey's provisioning lanes"). The account is stowed with =STOW_CONFLICTS=adopt= ([[file:layers.org][layers.org]], "Adopting an existing account"), so its =/etc/skel= defaults and identical files are recorded and linked, and anything else stops the run.

* What differs from kikin

- *Always the Telegram bot.* The launchers keep kikin's =--telegram= switch, so a plain run is an interactive warm session in the same directory, but the services always pass =--telegram=.
- *the operator's usage guard, same mechanism.* The launchers bind =claude-warm='s preservation guard exactly as kikin's =claude-preservation.bash= does, but the pool is the operator's account (see "Usage guard" below).
- *Herdr agent names are machine-prefixed and distinct from Honey's existing warm consort.* =/etc/init.d/claude-warm= (as =gille=) already registers as =honey-opus=, so the bots register as =honey-opus-bot=, =honey-sonnet-bot= and =honey-haiku-bot=.
- *A pid file.* Three =claude-warm= supervisors run under one account, so the OpenRC service cannot find "its" supervisor by command line. The launcher writes =$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/<name>.pid= just before it =exec='s =claude-warm= (same pid), and each service owns its own =XDG_RUNTIME_DIR= (=/run/honey-<bot>=).

* Launcher library

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/lib/telegram-preflight.bash
# all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/lib/telegram-preflight.bash (generated by agents-bots-honey.org)
# Port of kikin's FORGE/bin/lib/claude-telegram-preflight.bash for Honey.
#
# The official Telegram integration is not a daemon: the plugin's .mcp.json
# starts server.ts through Claude Code's MCP stdio client. This preflight
# validates that inline server and reserves the per-token state directory;
# it never daemonizes server.ts or starts an unrelated Telegram service.
# The token itself never lands in a path, a log or the environment.

honey_telegram_preflight() {
    local wrapper_name="${1:-}"
    local state_dir="${2:-}"
    # The lane's own settings (passed with --settings) enable the plugin, so the
    # agent account needs no hand-kept ~/.claude/settings.json.
    local settings="${CLAUDE_SETTINGS_FILE:-${LIB_DIR:+$LIB_DIR/../claude-settings.json}}"
    settings="${settings:-$HOME/.claude/settings.json}"
    local plugin_cache="$HOME/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/telegram"
    local plugin_root="" candidate plugin_command pid pid_command pid_state_dir
    local runtime_root lock_path token_fingerprint

    if [[ ! "$wrapper_name" =~ ^honey-(opus|sonnet|haiku)$ ]]; then
        printf '%s\n' "honey Telegram launch refused: invalid wrapper identity" >&2
        return 1
    fi
    if [[ ! "$state_dir" =~ ^$HOME/\.claude/channels/[a-z0-9-]+$ ]]; then
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: invalid channel state path" >&2
        return 1
    fi
    command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: jq is required for plugin preflight" >&2
        return 1
    }
    [[ -f "$settings" ]] || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: settings are missing at $settings" >&2
        return 1
    }
    jq -e '.enabledPlugins["telegram@claude-plugins-official"] == true' "$settings" >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: Telegram plugin is not enabled in $settings" >&2
        return 1
    }
    [[ -d "$plugin_cache" ]] || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: official Telegram plugin cache is missing" >&2
        return 1
    }
    while IFS= read -r candidate; do
        plugin_root="$candidate"
    done < <(find "$plugin_cache" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d -print 2>/dev/null | sort -V)
    [[ -n "$plugin_root" && -f "$plugin_root/.mcp.json" && -f "$plugin_root/server.ts" ]] || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: no complete official plugin server was found" >&2
        return 1
    }
    plugin_command="$(jq -r '.mcpServers.telegram.command // empty' "$plugin_root/.mcp.json" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    # Plugin 0.0.6 declares a bare "bun"; on Honey bun lives in metsatron's npm prefix.
    if [[ "$plugin_command" != /* ]]; then
        plugin_command="$(command -v -- "$plugin_command" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    fi
    [[ "$plugin_command" = /* && -x "$plugin_command" ]] || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: plugin MCP command is unavailable" >&2
        return 1
    }
    [[ -f "$state_dir/.env" ]] && grep -Eq '^TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=[^[:space:]].*$' "$state_dir/.env" || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: bot token state is missing at $state_dir/.env" >&2
        return 1
    }
    command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: sha256sum is required for token locking" >&2
        return 1
    }
    token_fingerprint="$(grep '^TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=' "$state_dir/.env" | sha256sum | awk '{print substr($1,1,16)}')"
    if [[ ! "$token_fingerprint" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{16}$ ]]; then
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: bot token state could not be fingerprinted" >&2
        return 1
    fi

    # One getUpdates consumer per token: hold a per-token lock across the exec.
    runtime_root="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/run/user/$(id -u)}/claude-idle-compaction"
    mkdir -p "$runtime_root"
    lock_path="$runtime_root/telegram-token-${token_fingerprint}.launch.lock"
    exec 9>"$lock_path"
    if ! flock -n 9; then
        printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: another session owns this bot token" >&2
        return 1
    fi

    # Never kill or replace a live plugin server; a stale pid file is the plugin's to recover.
    if [[ -f "$state_dir/bot.pid" ]]; then
        pid="$(tr -d '[:space:]' < "$state_dir/bot.pid" 2>/dev/null || true)"
        if [[ "$pid" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ && -r "/proc/$pid/cmdline" ]]; then
            pid_command="$(tr '\0' ' ' < "/proc/$pid/cmdline" 2>/dev/null || true)"
            pid_state_dir="$(tr '\0' '\n' < "/proc/$pid/environ" 2>/dev/null \
                | sed -n 's/^TELEGRAM_STATE_DIR=//p' | head -1 || true)"
            if [[ "$pid_state_dir" == "$state_dir" && "$pid_command" == *server.ts* ]]; then
                printf '%s\n' "$wrapper_name: Telegram launch refused: an active inline plugin server already owns $state_dir" >&2
                return 1
            fi
        fi
    fi
    return 0
}
#+END_SRC

The shared launch body. Each launcher only names its bot, its model variable and its default model.

Mail-woken Telegram turns use =MAILCORTEX_WAKE_REPLY_CHAT=. Each Telegram launcher sources the private, machine-local =$HOME/.config/mailcortex/wake-reply-<bot>.env= (opus, sonnet or haiku) before exporting that binding to its supervisor; the file is read in the pane, so an existing tmux server cannot discard the service environment. Copy the template from =services-mailcortex-claude.org= into that private path as the bot user with mode 0600 and provision the actual chat outside this repository. An inherited binding is also accepted; the public fallback =YOUR_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID= is diagnosed as an unprovisioned placeholder by the Stop guard.

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/lib/launch.bash
# all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/lib/launch.bash (generated by agents-bots-honey.org)
# Shared body of honey-opus / honey-sonnet / honey-haiku.

honey_claude_path_append() {
    local dir
    for dir in "$@"; do
        [[ -d "$dir" ]] || continue
        case ":$PATH:" in *":$dir:"*) ;; *) PATH="${PATH:+$PATH:}$dir" ;; esac
    done
    export PATH
}

# honey_claude_idle_hooks_preflight <name> <settings>
# The bots are unstowed, so --settings is their only hook source.  Without
# claude-hook-idle-event on SessionStart and Stop, claude-warm never learns a
# session or a turn ended and never compacts (2026-09-30: never once, for days).
honey_claude_idle_hooks_preflight() {
    local name="$1" settings="${2:-$HOME/.claude/settings.json}" event
    for event in SessionStart Stop; do
        jq -e --arg e "$event" \
            '[.hooks[$e][]?.hooks[]?.command | split(" ")[0]] | index("claude-hook-idle-event") != null' \
            "$settings" >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
            printf '%s\n' "$name: refused: $settings does not register claude-hook-idle-event on $event (idle compaction would never run)" >&2
            return 1
        }
    done
    command -v claude-hook-idle-event >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
        printf '%s\n' "$name: refused: claude-hook-idle-event is not on PATH (is it in the lane's SPARSE?)" >&2
        return 1
    }
    # A warning, not a refusal: without the snapshot writer the seat still runs, but MailCortex wakes queue as busy (2026-11-13).
    { jq -e '.statusLine.command == "claude-statusline"' "$settings" >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v claude-statusline >/dev/null 2>&1; } || \
        printf '%s\n' "$name: warning: no claude-statusline snapshot writer (is it in the lane's SPARSE and settings?); MailCortex wakes will queue as busy" >&2
    return 0
}

# honey_claude_launch <bot> <model> [--telegram] [claude args...]
honey_claude_launch() {
    local bot="$1" model="$2"
    shift 2
    local name="honey-$bot"
    local agent="honey-$bot-bot"
    local state_dir="$HOME/.claude/channels/telegram-$bot"
    local work_dir="${HONEY_CLAUDE_WORK_ROOT:-$HOME/honey-work}/$bot"
    local telegram=0 arg
    local passthrough=() resume_id=""

    # Resume pass-through (2026-10-17, Mètsàtron's word: resume Bunta, never
    # restart him): --resume <session-id> travels to claude-warm and on to the
    # claude CLI. The id is validated and a malformed one is refused loudly —
    # a relaunch that quietly started fresh would lose the live conversation.
    while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
        arg="$1"
        case "$arg" in
            --telegram) telegram=1 ;;
            --resume)
                if [[ -z "${2:-}" || "${2:-}" == -* ]]; then
                    printf '%s\n' "$name: refused: --resume needs a session id after it" >&2
                    return 1
                fi
                resume_id="$2"
                shift
                ;;
            --resume=*)
                resume_id="${arg#--resume=}"
                ;;
            *) passthrough+=("$arg") ;;
        esac
        shift
    done
    if [[ -n "$resume_id" ]]; then
        if [[ ! "${resume_id,,}" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$ ]]; then
            printf '%s\n' "$name: refused: --resume id '$resume_id' is not a session id (UUID)" >&2
            return 1
        fi
        passthrough+=(--resume "$resume_id")
    fi

    # The bot user's own npm prefix (claude, bun), agent Guix profile (node), stowed
    # bin, and the lane checkout's all/.local/bin (claude-warm and the fleet hooks;
    # the lane is all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude, four levels below .local);
    # appended, never replacing PATH.
    local repo_bin=""
    [[ -n "${LIB_DIR:-}" ]] && repo_bin="$(cd -- "$LIB_DIR/../../../../bin" 2>/dev/null && pwd || true)"
    honey_claude_path_append "$HOME/.local/bin" "${NPM_CONFIG_PREFIX:-$HOME/.npm-global}/bin" \
        "$HOME/.guix-extra-profiles/agent/agent/bin" ${repo_bin:+"$repo_bin"} \
        ${LIB_DIR:+"$LIB_DIR/../bin"}

    [[ -d "$work_dir" ]] || {
        printf '%s\n' "$name: refused: working directory $work_dir is missing (see agents-bots-honey.org)" >&2
        return 1
    }
    [[ -f "$work_dir/CLAUDE.md" ]] || {
        printf '%s\n' "$name: refused: $work_dir/CLAUDE.md (the Honey boundary law) is missing" >&2
        return 1
    }
    cd "$work_dir" || return 1

    if [[ "$telegram" -eq 1 ]]; then
        honey_telegram_preflight "$name" "$state_dir" || return 1
        honey_claude_idle_hooks_preflight "$name" \
            "${CLAUDE_SETTINGS_FILE:-${LIB_DIR:+$LIB_DIR/../claude-settings.json}}" || return 1
    fi
    # the operator's usage guard (preservation.bash); inert with a reason if it cannot bind.
    honey_claude_preservation_bindings "$name"
    # The OpenRC service finds this supervisor by pid: exec keeps the pid.
    if [[ -n "${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-}" && -d "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR" ]]; then
        printf '%s\n' "$$" > "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/$name.pid"
    fi
    if [[ "$telegram" -eq 1 ]]; then
        # Voice lane (see "Voice lane"): each bot's Kitten seat, the Honey whisper
        # subset for inbound notes, and the hooks in claude-settings.json.
        local voice_var="HONEY_${bot^^}_VOICE" voice
        case "$bot" in opus) voice=Jasper ;; sonnet) voice=Luna ;; haiku) voice=Kiki ;; esac
        export HONEY_VOICE="${!voice_var:-$voice}"
        [[ -n "${LIB_DIR:-}" ]] && export WHISPER_TRANSCRIBE="$LIB_DIR/../bin/honey-whisper-transcribe"
        # Mail wake (services-mailcortex-claude.org): seats listed in
        # HONEY_CLAUDE_MAIL_WAKE_BOTS (default: all three; Bunta, Sasuke and
        # Shoukichi each have a Kikin wake registry entry, applied by hand on Honey
        # 2026-10-30 and encoded here 2026-10-31) launch through
        # mailcortex-claude-warm with the opt-in set; it execs claude-warm
        # unchanged unless the wake is enabled.
        local warm=claude-warm wake=0
        case " ${HONEY_CLAUDE_MAIL_WAKE_BOTS-opus sonnet haiku} " in *" $bot "*) warm=mailcortex-claude-warm wake=1 ;; esac
        local wake_reply_env="$HOME/.config/mailcortex/wake-reply-$bot.env"
        [[ ! -f "$wake_reply_env" ]] || source "$wake_reply_env"
        # acceptEdits (auto) mode: plan mode blocks unattended bot work (as on kikin).
        exec env CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_AGENT="$agent" TELEGRAM_STATE_DIR="$state_dir" MAILCORTEX_CLAUDE_WAKE="$wake" \
            MAILCORTEX_WAKE_REPLY_CHAT="${MAILCORTEX_WAKE_REPLY_CHAT:-YOUR_TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID}" \
            "$warm" --model "$model" --permission-mode acceptEdits \
            --allowedTools=mcp__plugin_telegram_telegram \
            ${LIB_DIR:+--settings="$LIB_DIR/../claude-settings.json"} \
            --channels plugin:telegram@claude-plugins-official "${passthrough[@]+"${passthrough[@]}"}"
    fi
    exec env CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_AGENT="$agent" \
        claude-warm --model "$model" "${passthrough[@]+"${passthrough[@]}"}"
}
#+END_SRC

* Usage guard (the operator's account, kikin-central telemetry)

=claude-warm= carries the fleet's preservation guard: it reserves quota headroom with a central authority before it compacts, so no session burns a pool dry. The authority and its telemetry live on kikin (=HelmCortex/FORGE/scripts/fleet_preservation=, "Kikin is the single shared-pool authority"; it moves to tamamuri later). A caller binds it with the fourteen =CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_*= variables; if any is missing or invalid the guard stays inert with a reason and the session still runs. kikin's =claude-preservation.bash= binds Mètsàtron's pool; this library binds *the operator's* pool with the same mechanism:

| Binding             | kikin launchers                           | Honey bots                                                      |
|---------------------+-------------------------------------------+-----------------------------------------------------------------|
| bridge (caller)     | =~/HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/fleet-preservation= | same file from the HelmCortex skills mirror (=helmcortex-skills-mirror.org= carries it) |
| authority host      | =kikin= (=kikin-kushi= on kikin itself)   | =kikin= (override: =CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_HOST=)    |
| authority executable | =/home/metsatron/HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/fleet-preservation= on kikin | same                                            |
| ledger root         | =.../fleet-preservation/claude=            | =.../fleet-preservation/claude-gillean= (one telemetry cache per pool) |
| telemetry sensor    | =t480s= (reads Mètsàtron's account)       | =honey-metsatron= (=ai-usage= as metsatron, logged in as the operator) |
| account hash        | sha256 of the =~/.claude.json= oauth identity | same rule, on metsatron's =~/.claude.json= (the operator's login) |
| pool, estimates     | =shared-subscription=, 5 / 1 / 0.5 / 0.5, protection on, nudge off | same                                  |

The hash is computed at launch and never stored; no account id, token or address is in this repo.

*Before the guard can go live (kikin side, Mètsàtron's):* (1) metsatron on Honey needs an SSH key and a =kikin= alias that kikin accepts for =fleet-preservation authority=; (2) kikin needs a =honey-metsatron= alias and key that may run =ai-usage --json --no-remote --providers claude= on Honey; (3) as the fleet-preservation README requires, kikin must return fresh =state=ok= telemetry from that sensor (=fleet-preservation observe --root .../claude-gillean --expected-authority-hostname kikin-kushi --telemetry-host honey-metsatron --provider claude --account-hash <hash> --model-pool shared-subscription=), and a disposable owner-poll canary must prove the installed generation. Until then the bindings are exported but every poll fails closed (no reservation, no compaction directive) and the bots still answer.

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/lib/preservation.bash
# all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/lib/preservation.bash (generated by agents-bots-honey.org)
# the operator's usage guard: kikin's claude-preservation.bash bindings, pointed at her pool.

honey_claude_preservation_bindings() {
    local name="${1:-honey-claude}"
    local account_file="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ACCOUNT_FILE:-$HOME/.claude.json}"
    local mirror="${HONEY_HELMCORTEX_MIRROR:-$HOME/.local/share/helmcortex/skills-mirror}"
    local account_identity account_digest

    if [[ -n "${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ACCOUNT_HASH:-}" ]]; then
        account_digest="$CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ACCOUNT_HASH"
    else
        if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || ! command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            printf '%s\n' "$name: usage guard inert: jq and sha256sum are required" >&2
            return 0
        fi
        account_identity="$({ jq -er \
            '.oauthAccount.organizationUuid // .oauthAccount.accountUuid // .oauthAccount.userID // empty' \
            "$account_file" 2>/dev/null || true; } | tr -d '\n')"
        if [[ -z "$account_identity" ]]; then
            printf '%s\n' "$name: usage guard inert: no Claude account identity in $account_file (run claude /login)" >&2
            return 0
        fi
        account_digest="sha256:$(printf '%s' "$account_identity" | sha256sum | awk '{print $1}')"
        unset account_identity
    fi

    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_BRIDGE="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_BRIDGE:-$mirror/FORGE/bin/fleet-preservation}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_BRIDGE_ADAPTER="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_BRIDGE_ADAPTER:-federated_preservation.SshAuthorityTransport}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_HOST="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_HOST:-kikin}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_IDENTITY="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_IDENTITY:-kikin-kushi}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_EXECUTABLE="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_EXECUTABLE:-/home/metsatron/HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/fleet-preservation}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_LEDGER_ROOT="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_LEDGER_ROOT:-/home/metsatron/.local/state/fleet-preservation/claude-gillean}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_TELEMETRY_SOURCE="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_TELEMETRY_SOURCE:-authority:${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_AUTHORITY_HOST}}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_TELEMETRY_HOST="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_TELEMETRY_HOST:-honey-metsatron}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_TRANSPORT="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_TRANSPORT:-ssh}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_PROVIDER="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_PROVIDER:-claude}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ACCOUNT_HASH="$account_digest"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_MODEL_POOL="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_MODEL_POOL:-shared-subscription}"
    # Same calibration as kikin: 5 percent per 500k input tokens.
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ESTIMATE_PERCENT="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ESTIMATE_PERCENT:-5}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ESTIMATE_UNCERTAINTY_PERCENT="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_ESTIMATE_UNCERTAINTY_PERCENT:-1}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_IN_FLIGHT_RESERVE_PERCENT="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_IN_FLIGHT_RESERVE_PERCENT:-0.5}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_CONTROL_RESERVE_PERCENT="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_CONTROL_RESERVE_PERCENT:-0.5}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_PROTECTION_ENABLED="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_PROTECTION_ENABLED:-1}"
    export CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_NUDGE_ENABLED="${CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_NUDGE_ENABLED:-0}"
}

* Launchers

Model ids per Mètsàtron's rulings of 2026-09-26, 2026-09-30, 2026-10-19 and 2026-10-27 (Opus 4.8[1m] — Opus 5.5 left the menu fleet-wide on 2026-10-19 —, Sonnet 5.5, Haiku 5.5 — Shoukichi moved home to Honey on Haiku 5.5, 2026-10-27); each can be overridden per service without an edit (=HONEY_OPUS_MODEL=, =HONEY_SONNET_MODEL=, =HONEY_HAIKU_MODEL=).

#+NAME: honey-launcher-head
#+BEGIN_SRC bash
set -euo pipefail

LIB_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "$(readlink -f -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")")/../lib" && pwd)"
source "$LIB_DIR/telegram-preflight.bash"
source "$LIB_DIR/preservation.bash"
source "$LIB_DIR/launch.bash"

case "${1-}" in
  -h|--help)
    printf '%s\n' "Usage: $(basename -- "$0") [--telegram] [--resume SESSION_ID] [claude args...]" \
      "Launch this Honey Claude bot through claude-warm in ~/honey-work/<bot> (agents-bots-honey.org)." \
      "  --telegram  attach the Telegram channel; runs in acceptEdits (auto) mode." \
      "              Token: ~/.claude/channels/telegram-<bot>/.env (never printed)." \
      "  --resume SESSION_ID  continue that conversation (UUID; passed to claude-warm" \
      "              and on to claude). The canonical door is nurse-joy revive, from Kikin."
    exit 0 ;;
esac
#+END_SRC

* Voice lane

Mètsàtron's ask (2026-09-26): the tanuki bots hear his voice notes and answer with voice notes too, in the Kitten seats pvox gives their kikin counterparts: Bunta Jasper (the Opus seat), Sasuke Luna (Fable's), Shoukichi Kiki (Haiku's), each at speed 1.2 on =kitten-tts-nano-0.8-int8=. On kikin pvox plays replies aloud on the speaker host; here each reply is also sent back into the chat as a playable voice note, because Honey has no HelmCortex and no speaker lane.

- *Inbound:* the fleet's own =claude-hook-voice-transcribe= ([[file:agents-hooks.org][agents-hooks.org]]) runs as the =UserPromptSubmit= hook. It honours =$WHISPER_TRANSCRIBE=, which the launcher points at =honey-whisper-transcribe=, the Honey subset of HelmCortex's =whisper-transcribe= (same call shape, model source, cache and SHA-256 pin; no resident daemon, loop detection or escalation). =whisper-cli= comes from agent-claude's Guix profile, =ffmpeg= from the host.
- *Outbound:* =honey-claude-voice-reply= runs as the =PostToolUse= hook on the reply tool. It sends the raw reply and its declared format through the fleet's shared =voice-text-normalize= boundary before the spoken-character cap, detaches at once, renders the clean text with the agent's =~/kitten-venv= (DotCortex =kitten-apply=), encodes Opus, and posts it with =sendVoice= to the same chat. The token is read from the bot's state dir, never argv or logs; the log (=~/.cache/honey-voice-reply.log=) carries no text or synthesizer stderr. =HONEY_VOICE_OFF=1= silences it, =HONEY_<BOT>_VOICE= overrides a seat.
- The hooks reach Claude through =--settings=, so an unstowed agent account needs no hand-edited =settings.json=. The same file enables the Telegram plugin and names its marketplace (Claude Code honours =enabledPlugins= from =--settings=, checked with =claude plugin list= on 2.1.284), and the preflight checks it there, so agent-claude's own =~/.claude/settings.json= carries nothing the bots depend on.

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/bin/honey-claude-voice-reply :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env python3" :tangle-mode (identity #o755)
"""PostToolUse hook (Telegram reply tool): send the reply back as a voice note.

Renders the reply text with Kitten TTS in this bot's voice, encodes Opus with
ffmpeg, and posts it to the same chat with the Bot API's sendVoice, so every
answer also arrives as a playable file. The hook detaches at once and never
blocks or fails the reply; problems go to ~/.cache/honey-voice-reply.log
(never the text). The token is read from $TELEGRAM_STATE_DIR/.env and never
appears in argv or the log.
"""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.request
import uuid

LOG = os.path.expanduser("~/.cache/honey-voice-reply.log")
KITTEN_PY = os.path.expanduser("~/kitten-venv/bin/python")
MODEL = os.environ.get("HONEY_VOICE_MODEL", "KittenML/kitten-tts-nano-0.8-int8")
VOICE = os.environ.get("HONEY_VOICE", "")
SPEED = os.environ.get("HONEY_VOICE_SPEED", "1.2")
MAX_CHARS = int(os.environ.get("HONEY_VOICE_MAX_CHARS", "0") or 0)  # 0 = speak the whole reply (2026-10-07)
NORMALIZER = (
    os.environ.get("VOICE_TEXT_NORMALIZER")
    or shutil.which("voice-text-normalize")
    or str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[5] / "all/.local/bin/voice-text-normalize")
)
SYNTH = r"""
import sys
import numpy as np
from kittentts import KittenTTS
model_id, voice, speed, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], float(sys.argv[3]), sys.argv[4]
audio = KittenTTS(model_id).generate(sys.stdin.read(), voice=voice, speed=speed)
open(out, "wb").write((np.clip(audio, -1.0, 1.0) * 32767).astype("<i2").tobytes())
"""


def log(msg):
    try:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(LOG), exist_ok=True)
        with open(LOG, "a") as f:
            f.write("%s %s\n" % (time.strftime("%F %T"), msg))
    except OSError:
        pass


def token():
    state = os.environ.get("TELEGRAM_STATE_DIR") or os.path.expanduser("~/.claude/channels/telegram")
    try:
        for line in open(os.path.join(state, ".env")):
            if line.startswith("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN="):
                return line.split("=", 1)[1].strip().strip('"')
    except OSError:
        return None
    return None


def send_voice(tok, chat_id, ogg, reply_to):
    boundary = uuid.uuid4().hex
    parts = []
    fields = {"chat_id": str(chat_id)}
    if reply_to:
        fields["reply_to_message_id"] = str(reply_to)
    for k, v in fields.items():
        parts.append(('--%s\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name="%s"\r\n\r\n%s\r\n' % (boundary, k, v)).encode())
    parts.append(('--%s\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name="voice"; filename="reply.ogg"\r\n'
                  'Content-Type: audio/ogg\r\n\r\n' % boundary).encode())
    parts.append(open(ogg, "rb").read())
    parts.append(("\r\n--%s--\r\n" % boundary).encode())
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        "https://api.telegram.org/bot%s/sendVoice" % tok, data=b"".join(parts),
        headers={"Content-Type": "multipart/form-data; boundary=%s" % boundary})
    return json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60)).get("ok")


def normalize_reply(text, format_name):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [NORMALIZER, "--format", format_name], input=text,
            text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=10,
        )
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
        raise RuntimeError(error.__class__.__name__) from error
    if result.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError("exit-%d" % result.returncode)
    return result.stdout.strip()


def render_and_send(text, chat_id, reply_to):
    tok = token()
    if not tok:
        log("SKIP no-token")
        return
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="honey-voice-") as tmp:
        pcm, ogg = os.path.join(tmp, "reply.pcm"), os.path.join(tmp, "reply.ogg")
        t0 = time.time()
        r = subprocess.run([KITTEN_PY, "-c", SYNTH, MODEL, VOICE, SPEED, pcm],
                           input=text.encode(), capture_output=True, timeout=600)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            log("FAIL synth rc=%d" % r.returncode)
            return
        r = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-y", "-f", "s16le", "-ar", "24000", "-ac", "1",
                            "-i", pcm, "-c:a", "libopus", "-b:a", "32k", ogg], capture_output=True, timeout=120)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            log("FAIL ffmpeg rc=%d" % r.returncode)
            return
        try:
            ok = send_voice(tok, chat_id, ogg, reply_to)
        except Exception as e:
            log("FAIL send %s" % e.__class__.__name__)
            return
        log("OK voice=%s chars=%d secs=%.1f sent=%s" % (VOICE, len(text), time.time() - t0, ok))


def main(detach=True):
    if os.environ.get("HONEY_VOICE_OFF") or not VOICE or not os.path.exists(KITTEN_PY):
        return
    try:
        data = json.loads(sys.stdin.read() or "{}")
    except ValueError:
        return
    ti = data.get("tool_input") or {}
    raw, format_name = ti.get("text") or "", ti.get("format") or "markdown"
    chat_id = ti.get("chat_id")
    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not isinstance(format_name, str) or not raw.strip() or not chat_id:
        return
    try:
        text = normalize_reply(raw, format_name)
    except RuntimeError as error:
        log("SKIP normalize-failed %s" % error)
        return
    if not text:
        return
    if MAX_CHARS and len(text) > MAX_CHARS:
        text = text[:MAX_CHARS]
    # Detach: the reply has already gone out; the voice note follows it.
    if detach:
        if os.fork() > 0:
            return
        os.setsid()
        if os.fork() > 0:
            os._exit(0)
        devnull = os.open(os.devnull, os.O_RDWR)
        for fd in (0, 1, 2):
            os.dup2(devnull, fd)
    try:
        render_and_send(text, chat_id, ti.get("reply_to"))
    finally:
        if detach:
            os._exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/bin/honey-whisper-transcribe :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env python3" :tangle-mode (identity #o755)
"""The Honey subset of HelmCortex's whisper-transcribe, for hosts without HelmCortex.

Same call shape as the canonical FORGE/bin/whisper-transcribe that
claude-hook-voice-transcribe runs (--model TIER --out PATH AUDIO), so the hook
reaches it through $WHISPER_TRANSCRIBE. It keeps the canonical model source,
cache path and pinned SHA-256, the ffmpeg 16 kHz decode and the whisper-cli
call with its CPU retry; it leaves out the resident daemon, loop detection and
tier escalation. Output: a transcript file with the same YAML front matter.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import urllib.request
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

MODEL_CACHE_DIR = Path.home() / ".cache" / "whisper-cpp" / "models"
MODEL_BASE_URL = "https://huggingface.co/ggerganov/whisper.cpp/resolve/main"
# Pins copied from the canonical whisper-transcribe (verified 2026-07-03).
MODEL_SHA256 = {"small": "1be3a9b2063867b937e64e2ec7483364a79917e157fa98c5d94b5c1fffea987b"}
MODEL_FILENAMES = {"small": "ggml-small.bin"}


def model_path(tier):
    name = MODEL_FILENAMES[tier]
    path = MODEL_CACHE_DIR / name
    if path.exists():
        return path
    MODEL_CACHE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = path.with_suffix(".part")
    urllib.request.urlretrieve("%s/%s" % (MODEL_BASE_URL, name), tmp)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(tmp.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    if digest != MODEL_SHA256[tier]:
        tmp.unlink()
        sys.exit("model %s failed its SHA-256 pin (got %s)" % (name, digest))
    tmp.rename(path)
    return path


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("audio")
    ap.add_argument("--model", default="small", choices=sorted(MODEL_FILENAMES))
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=470)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    cli = shutil.which("whisper-cli")
    if cli is None or shutil.which("ffmpeg") is None:
        sys.exit("whisper-cli or ffmpeg missing (agent Guix profile / system ffmpeg)")
    model = model_path(args.model)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="honey-whisper-") as tmp:
        wav = Path(tmp) / "in.wav"
        subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-y", "-i", args.audio, "-ar", "16000", "-ac", "1",
                        "-c:a", "pcm_s16le", str(wav)], check=True, timeout=120)
        stem = Path(tmp) / "out"
        cmd = [cli, "-m", str(model), "-f", str(wav), "-otxt", "-of", str(stem), "-nt"]
        try:
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, timeout=args.timeout, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as err:
            if err.returncode >= 0:
                raise
            subprocess.run(cmd + ["-ng"], check=True, timeout=args.timeout,
                           stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        text = (stem.with_suffix(".txt")).read_text().strip()
    front = "---\nsource: %s\nmodel: %s\ngenerated_at: %s\n---\n" % (
        os.path.basename(args.audio), args.model, datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"))
    Path(args.out).write_text(front + text + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
#+END_SRC

=honey-claude-wakeup-guard= is the Honey seats' copy of the fleet's wakeup cache guard (HelmCortex userspace hook =wakeup-cache-guard=, 2026-10-18). A loop wakeup scheduled at or past the one-hour prompt-cache lifetime lands just after expiry and re-writes the seat's whole context to cache. Eleven such idle ticks burned half a weekly quota overnight on Kikin. The Honey seats do not load the HelmCortex userspace hooks, so the same rule rides here: any =ScheduleWakeup= over 3300 seconds is refused before it runs, and stop requests always pass.

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/bin/honey-claude-wakeup-guard :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env python3" :tangle-mode (identity #o755)
"""PreToolUse guard: refuse ScheduleWakeup delays past the prompt-cache window (exit 2, reason on stderr)."""
import json
import sys

LIMIT = 3300

try:
    tool_input = json.load(sys.stdin).get("tool_input") or {}
except Exception:
    sys.exit(0)
if tool_input.get("stop") is True:
    sys.exit(0)
try:
    delay = float(tool_input.get("delaySeconds", 0))
except (TypeError, ValueError):
    sys.exit(0)
if delay > LIMIT:
    sys.stderr.write(
        f"WAKEUP CACHE GUARD: ScheduleWakeup blocked (delaySeconds={int(delay)} > {LIMIT}).\n"
        "The prompt cache lives one hour; a wake at or just past it re-writes the whole\n"
        "context to cache at full price. If nothing is actionable, STOP the loop\n"
        "(ScheduleWakeup stop:true) and wake only on the Admiral's message or a worker's\n"
        f"completion notice. If a wake is truly needed, keep it <= {LIMIT}s.\n")
    sys.exit(2)
sys.exit(0)
#+END_SRC

=promptSuggestionEnabled: false= keeps the prompt box truly empty between turns. Claude Code otherwise prints a dimmed suggested next message there, which the mail wake's screen check reads as typed input, so the seat reports busy forever and mail never wakes it (found 2026-10-12 on Bunta's first wake).

* OpenRC services (one body, installed by root)

Modelled on Honey's =/etc/init.d/claude-warm=, with the lessons it already paid for:

- =use net dns= / =after=, never =need net=: Honey has no hard =net= service and =need net= fails dependency resolution.
- A dedicated =XDG_RUNTIME_DIR= under =/run= per bot, created in =start_pre=: =/run/user/<uid>= does not exist at a headless boot.
- The Claude UI must render in the tmux pane, or attaching shows an empty pane and nobody can pair or operate the bot (found 2026-09-26: the launcher's output was redirected into the log). The log is a copy taken with =tmux pipe-pane=.
- =claude-warm= ignores SIGHUP and survives its tmux pane, so =stop= signals the supervisor itself (by the launcher's pid file), then removes the tmux session.
- =PATH= is only ever *appended* to (the login shell's PATH plus the bot user's npm prefix, agent Guix profile and bin), never overwritten.
- The launcher comes from the root-owned system checkout, and =start_pre= refuses to start if anything in the lane or the fleet =bin= it appends is not owned by root or is group/other-writable: the bot must never run code it could have edited itself.

One script body serves all three services: the bot comes from =RC_SVCNAME= (=honey-opus= to =opus=). =honey-claude-install= (below) installs it as =/etc/init.d/honey-{opus,sonnet,haiku}=. =/etc/conf.d/honey-<bot>= may set =bot_user=, =honey_claude_checkout= or =resume==<session-id>: with =resume= set, =start= launches the bot on that conversation instead of a fresh one.

**Resume semantics (2026-10-17): the service and nurse-joy never fight over the session.** =rc-service honey-<bot> restart= remains forbidden as a resume tool (=stop= kills the tmux session and the live context with it). Two doors resume: (1) *nurse-joy* (Kikin, =HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/nurse-joy=) revives Bunta over ssh as =agent-claude= — graceful =/exit=, relaunch through this lane's launcher with the truthful session id — inside the *live* tmux session, never touching the service; the launcher rewrites =$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/honey-<bot>.pid= on every launch, so the service's =status= stays truthful. (2) *the service*, with =resume= set in =/etc/conf.d/honey-<bot>=, for a boot or deliberate =stop= where the operator already knows the session id; =start_pre= refuses a malformed id.

#+NAME: honey-openrc
#+BEGIN_SRC bash
#!/sbin/openrc-run
# Honey Telegram Claude bot (generated by agents-bots-honey.org; source of truth: DotCortex).
# Runs honey-<bot> --telegram inside its own detached tmux session as agent-claude.
# Observe: su -l agent-claude -c 'tmux attach -t honey-<bot>' (Ctrl-b d detaches).
# Control: XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/run/honey-<bot> claude-warmctl list (as agent-claude).

bot="${RC_SVCNAME#honey-}"
name="${RC_SVCNAME}"
# The bots run as agent-claude (ruling 2026-09-26); /etc/conf.d/honey-<bot> may override.
: "${bot_user:=agent-claude}"
description="Honey ${bot} Telegram Claude bot (claude-warm as ${bot_user})"

bot_home="$(getent passwd "${bot_user}" | cut -d: -f6)"
bot_session="${RC_SVCNAME}"
bot_cwd="${bot_home}/honey-work/${bot}"
# The service lane: launchers run from the host's root-owned system checkout, never
# from a checkout the bot user can write (agents-bots-honey.org, "Service lane").
: "${honey_claude_checkout:=/usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex}"
# /etc/conf.d/honey-<bot> may set resume=<session-id>: start on that conversation
# (resume semantics: nurse-joy revive is the live-seat door; this is the boot door).
resume="${resume:-}"
bot_lane="${honey_claude_checkout}/all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude"
bot_launcher="${bot_lane}/bin/${RC_SVCNAME}"
bot_log="/var/log/${RC_SVCNAME}.log"
xdg="/run/${RC_SVCNAME}"
bot_pidfile="${xdg}/${RC_SVCNAME}.pid"
bot_path_add="${bot_home}/.local/bin:${bot_home}/.npm-global/bin:${bot_home}/.guix-extra-profiles/agent/agent/bin"

depend() {
    use net dns
    after net elogind dbus
}

start_pre() {
    case "${bot}" in
        opus|sonnet|haiku) ;;
        *) eerror "unknown Honey bot service name: ${RC_SVCNAME}"; return 1 ;;
    esac
    [ -x "${bot_launcher}" ] || { eerror "missing launcher ${bot_launcher} (system checkout)"; return 1; }
    # Everything the bot executes from the checkout must be root's and unwritable by others.
    if [ -n "$(find "${bot_lane}" "${honey_claude_checkout}/all/.local/bin" \
            \( ! -user root -o \( ! -type l -perm /022 \) \) -print -quit 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
        eerror "${honey_claude_checkout} is not a root-owned, read-only system checkout; refusing"
        return 1
    fi
    checkpath -d -m 0700 -o "${bot_user}:${bot_user}" "${xdg}"
    checkpath -f -m 0640 -o "${bot_user}:${bot_user}" "${bot_log}"
    if [ -n "${resume}" ] && ! printf '%s' "${resume}" | grep -Eq '^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$'; then
        eerror "resume must be a session id (UUID): ${resume}"
        return 1
    fi
}

bot_pid() {
    local pid
    [ -r "${bot_pidfile}" ] || return 1
    pid="$(tr -dc '0-9' < "${bot_pidfile}")"
    [ -n "${pid}" ] || return 1
    [ "$(stat -c %U "/proc/${pid}" 2>/dev/null)" = "${bot_user}" ] || return 1
    tr '\0' ' ' < "/proc/${pid}/cmdline" 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'claude-warm' || return 1
    echo "${pid}"
}

start() {
    local bot_args="--telegram"
    [ -n "${resume}" ] && bot_args="${bot_args} --resume ${resume}"
    ebegin "Starting ${description} (tmux: ${bot_session})"
    su -l "${bot_user}" -c "export XDG_RUNTIME_DIR='${xdg}' PATH=\"\${PATH}:${bot_path_add}\"; \
        tmux new-session -d -s '${bot_session}' -c '${bot_cwd}' \
        -e XDG_RUNTIME_DIR='${xdg}' -e PATH=\"\${PATH}\" \
        'exec ${bot_launcher} ${bot_args}' \
        && tmux pipe-pane -o -t '${bot_session}' 'cat >> ${bot_log}'"
    eend $? "Failed to start ${RC_SVCNAME}"
}

stop() {
    local pid i=0
    ebegin "Stopping ${description}"
    pid="$(bot_pid)"
    if [ -n "${pid}" ]; then
        kill -TERM "${pid}" 2>/dev/null
        while kill -0 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null && [ $i -lt 10 ]; do
            sleep 1; i=$((i+1))
        done
        kill -0 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null && kill -KILL "${pid}" 2>/dev/null
    fi
    rm -f "${bot_pidfile}"
    su -l "${bot_user}" -c "tmux kill-session -t '${bot_session}'" 2>/dev/null
    eend 0
}

status() {
    if bot_pid >/dev/null; then
        einfo "${RC_SVCNAME} supervisor is running (pid $(bot_pid))"
        return 0
    fi
    einfo "${RC_SVCNAME} is not running"
    return 3
}
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/openrc/honey-bot :noweb yes :tangle-mode (identity #o644)
<<honey-openrc>>
#+END_SRC

* Service lane: a root-owned system checkout

The bots are a host service, not part of anyone's dotfiles, so they run from a *service lane*: one root-owned DotCortex checkout per host at =/usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex=, next to the Ductor seams that already live under =/usr/local/share/dotcortex= ([[file:agents-ductor-seams.org][agents-ductor-seams.org]]). Why:

- *Nobody's PATH.* The lane is never stowed, so =metsatron= (or the operator, later) no longer gets three bot launchers on PATH from the =honey/= overlay.
- *The bot cannot edit what it runs.* Today the services run launchers from =agent-claude='s own checkout, which =agent-claude= can write; the body now refuses a checkout that is not root's and read-only (=start_pre=).
- *One checkout per host, and a light one.* It is updated only by root, on Mètsàtron's word, and an update changes nothing running until the service is restarted.

** The checkout is sparse and partial
A full DotCortex checkout is about 218k files. The lane needs a handful, so the system checkout reuses the HelmCortex skills mirror's shape ([[file:helmcortex-skills-mirror.org][helmcortex-skills-mirror.org]]): a *partial* clone (=--filter=blob:none=: only the blobs of the checked-out paths are fetched) with a *non-cone sparse* checkout of exactly the paths below. No submodules are initialised.

The lane declares its own path list, =<lane>/SPARSE=, traced from what the launchers actually run:

- the lane itself (launchers, library, voice helpers, settings, OpenRC body, identity files);
- =claude-warm=, which every launcher =exec='s (standard library only);
- =claude-warmctl=, the control command the OpenRC body's header names (=claude-warm --control=);
- =claude-hook-voice-transcribe=, the =UserPromptSubmit= hook in =claude-settings.json= (standard library only; it runs =$WHISPER_TRANSCRIBE=, which the launcher points at the lane's own =honey-whisper-transcribe=).
- =claude-hook-telegram-delivery-guard=, the =Stop= hook in =claude-settings.json= (bash and =python3= only): a bot whose turn ends without calling the Telegram reply tool gets one more turn, so an answer never stays in a transcript nobody reads. Its first gate is =CLAUDE_IDLE_CHANNEL=, which =claude-warm= exports to the session.
- =claude-hook-idle-event=, the lifecycle adapter every =claude-warm= session needs (bash, =jq= and =python3= only). It is registered on every event where the fleet's own settings register it ([[file:agents-hooks.org][agents-hooks.org]], =all/.claude/settings.shared.json=); without it =claude-warm= never sees a session start or a turn end, so its idle timer never arms and the bots never compact.

*Incident, 2026-09-30.* The lane shipped without this hook: =SPARSE= did not carry it and =claude-settings.json= did not register it. On a stowed host the hook reaches Claude through =~/.claude/settings.json=; the bots are unstowed, their =~/.claude/settings.json= is empty, and =--settings= is their only hook source. All three supervisors sat at =status: starting= from their first launch (2026-09-26) and never compacted once. Three guards now stop a repeat: the tests require every idle-event registration of the fleet's settings in the lane's settings; the launcher refuses to start a Telegram bot whose settings lack the hook or whose =PATH= cannot find it; and =claude-warm= itself records =lifecycle-hooks-missing= when a supervised session sends no lifecycle event after launch ([[file:agents-claude-idle-supervisor.org][agents-claude-idle-supervisor.org]]).

- =claude-statusline=, the status-line command in =claude-settings.json= (bash and =jq= only; the =HelmCortex= creature lookup it tries is skipped when absent). Besides drawing the line, it is the *snapshot writer*: it writes =status.json= (channel, session id, model, input-context tokens) into the seat's =claude-idle-compaction/<channel>/= runtime directory, found through =$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR= and the live session's =state.json=. =claude-warm='s admission gate reads that file before it will say a seat is idle, and answers =status-snapshot-missing= (reported to MailCortex as =busy=) when it is absent.

*Incident, 2026-11-13.* The lane carried no =statusLine=, so on Honey nothing ever wrote the snapshot: every MailCortex wake to a Honey Claude seat was admitted once after a launch and then queued as "busy" for good (Shoukichi's two mails sat queued for an hour while his pane was idle; the =claude-warm= state said =last_gate_rejection_reason: status-snapshot-missing=). Hermes traced the string to the one place that produces it; the fix is this registration plus the =SPARSE= entry. It takes effect when the system checkout syncs and each seat is relaunched (the launcher passes the settings with =--settings=), after which =status.json= appears in the seat's runtime directory.

- =codex-warm=, =codex-warmctl= and =codex-hook-idle-event= (standard library, bash, =jq= and =python3= only), added 2026-10-05 for the approved warm switchover (=agents-codex-idle-supervisor.org=, "Switchover to the agent accounts"): =codex-warm.agent-codex= resolves its binary from this checkout, never from one =agent-codex= can write. No bot runs them.

Everything else the bots call comes from the host or =agent-claude='s own profiles, never from DotCortex: =jq=, =sha256sum=, =ffmpeg=, =whisper-cli=, =~/kitten-venv=, =claude= and =bun= (=~/.npm-global=), node (agent Guix profile), and the preservation bridge from the HelmCortex skills mirror. The tests fail if a lane file names an =all/.local/bin= command or an =all/= path that =SPARSE= does not carry, so widening the lane means widening this list.

#+NAME: honey-claude-sparse

/all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/

** Installer
=honey-claude-install= has three verbs, all with =--dry-run=:

- =sync= clones the system checkout on first run, then updates it: it refuses a checkout with local edits, on another branch, with another remote, or whose history the remote rewrote; it fetches, *re-applies the sparse set from the fetched =SPARSE=* (so a widened list arrives with the change that needs it), and fast-forwards. That is =git pull --ff-only= with the sparse set re-read first. The clone sets =index.version 4=: a non-cone sparse checkout still lists every tracked path in its index (the sparse index needs cone mode, and cone mode would pull in the whole fleet =bin=), and version 4 prefix-compresses those paths, about 40% smaller. It runs with =umask 022=, so the files come out root-owned and unwritable by others even under =sudo= from a =002= shell. The first clone may be run from any checkout (its own =SPARSE= seeds the patterns); after that, run it from the system checkout.
- =install= (the default) puts the body in place as =/etc/init.d/honey-{opus,sonnet,haiku}= and backs up what it replaces, following the Ductor installer. It refuses any checkout but the root-owned system one.
- =workdirs= (added 2026-10-31) copies each lane identity file =CLAUDE.<bot>.md= to =~agent-claude/honey-work/<bot>/CLAUDE.md=, root-owned and read-only to the bot, backing up a changed file as =<file>.bak-<timestamp>= and leaving an identical one alone (=HONEY_CLAUDE_WORK_ROOT= overrides the destination for rehearsals). Until then the copy was a manual "reviewed apply step" that nothing owned: after the 2026-10-19 sync the system checkout carried a corrected Bunta file while his working directory kept the 2026-09-30 copy that still said Opus 5.5, for a month. It refuses any checkout but the root-owned system one, like =install=.

None of the verbs starts, restarts or adds a service to a runlevel (the three are already in =default=). =HONEY_CLAUDE_CHECKOUT=, =HONEY_CLAUDE_REMOTE= and =HONEY_CLAUDE_BRANCH= override the destination, remote and branch of =sync=, for rehearsals in a scratch directory; only the real destination needs root.

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/openrc/honey-claude-install :tangle-mode (identity #o755)
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# honey-claude-install (generated by agents-bots-honey.org): keep the host's root-owned
# sparse system checkout current, and install Honey's tanuki bot services from it.
set -euo pipefail
umask 022

SYSTEM_CHECKOUT=/usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex
LANE_REL=all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude
BOTS=(opus sonnet haiku)

usage() {
    cat <<'USAGE'
Usage: honey-claude-install [--dry-run] [install]
       honey-claude-install [--dry-run] sync
       honey-claude-install [--dry-run] workdirs
       honey-claude-install -h|--help

sync     clone or update the sparse, blobless system checkout
         (/usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex) from <lane>/SPARSE; refuses local
         edits, another branch or remote, and non-fast-forward history
install  install the shared OpenRC body as /etc/init.d/honey-{opus,sonnet,haiku}
         from the root-owned system checkout (default verb); existing scripts
         are backed up as <file>.bak-<timestamp>
workdirs copy each bot's identity file (<lane>/CLAUDE.<bot>.md) from the
         root-owned system checkout to ~agent-claude/honey-work/<bot>/CLAUDE.md,
         root-owned and read-only to the bot; a changed file is backed up as
         <file>.bak-<timestamp>, an identical one is left alone

Run as root. Nothing is started, restarted or added to a runlevel.
  --dry-run   print what would change; change nothing (any user, any checkout)
Env (sync rehearsals): HONEY_CLAUDE_CHECKOUT, HONEY_CLAUDE_REMOTE, HONEY_CLAUDE_BRANCH
Env (workdirs rehearsals): HONEY_CLAUDE_WORK_ROOT (default: ~agent-claude/honey-work)
USAGE
}

DRY=0 VERB=install
for arg in "$@"; do
    case "$arg" in
        -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
        --dry-run) DRY=1 ;;
        sync|install|workdirs) VERB="$arg" ;;
        *) usage >&2; exit 2 ;;
    esac
done

lane="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "$(readlink -f -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")")/.." && pwd)"
checkout="$(cd -- "$lane/../../../../.." && pwd)"
run() { if [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ]; then echo "would: $*"; else "$@"; fi; }
fail() { echo "honey-claude-install: $*" >&2; exit 1; }

do_sync() {
    local dir="${HONEY_CLAUDE_CHECKOUT:-$SYSTEM_CHECKOUT}"
    local remote="${HONEY_CLAUDE_REMOTE:-https://gitlab.com/metsatron/dotfiles.git}"
    local branch="${HONEY_CLAUDE_BRANCH:-master}"
    command -v git >/dev/null 2>&1 || fail "git is not installed"
    if [ "$dir" = "$SYSTEM_CHECKOUT" ] && [ "$DRY" -eq 0 ] && [ "$(id -u)" -ne 0 ]; then
        fail "run as root to sync $SYSTEM_CHECKOUT"
    fi
    if [ ! -d "$dir/.git" ]; then
        [ ! -e "$dir" ] || [ -z "$(ls -A "$dir" 2>/dev/null)" ] || fail "refusing: $dir exists, is not empty and is not a checkout"
        local tmp="$dir.partial.$$"
        run git clone --quiet --filter=blob:none --no-checkout --branch "$branch" "$remote" "$tmp"
        # The sparse set must come from the branch being cloned, never from the lane on
        # disk: on a first clone of a moved checkout the lane is the OLD copy, so its
        # SPARSE predates the files this clone is for (2026-11-13: claude-statusline
        # was in the branch and absent from the fresh checkout until a second sync).
        local sparse_source="$branch:$LANE_REL/SPARSE"
        run git -C "$tmp" show "$sparse_source" >/dev/null 2>&1 || fail "missing $sparse_source in $remote $branch"
        run git -C "$tmp" sparse-checkout init --no-cone
        # Non-cone mode keeps every path in the index (no sparse index); v4 prefix-compresses it.
        run git -C "$tmp" config index.version 4
        if [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ]; then
            echo "would: set the sparse patterns from $sparse_source:"; git -C "$tmp" show "$sparse_source" | sed 's/^/  /'
        else
            git -C "$tmp" show "$sparse_source" | git -C "$tmp" sparse-checkout set --no-cone --stdin
        fi
        run git -C "$tmp" checkout --quiet "$branch"
        run mv "$tmp" "$dir"
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || echo "cloned $(git -C "$dir" rev-parse --short HEAD) into $dir (sparse, blobless)"
        return 0
    fi
    [ "$(git -C "$dir" remote get-url origin)" = "$remote" ] || fail "refusing: $dir origin is not $remote"
    [ "$(git -C "$dir" symbolic-ref --short HEAD 2>/dev/null || true)" = "$branch" ] || fail "refusing: $dir is not on $branch"
    [ -z "$(git -C "$dir" status --porcelain --untracked-files=no)" ] || fail "refusing: $dir has local edits; it is read-only by contract"
    run git -C "$dir" fetch --quiet --filter=blob:none origin "$branch"
    if [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ]; then
        echo "would: re-apply the sparse set from FETCH_HEAD:$LANE_REL/SPARSE, then merge --ff-only FETCH_HEAD"
        return 0
    fi
    local before after
    before="$(git -C "$dir" rev-parse HEAD)"
    git -C "$dir" show "FETCH_HEAD:$LANE_REL/SPARSE" | git -C "$dir" sparse-checkout set --no-cone --stdin
    git -C "$dir" merge --quiet --ff-only FETCH_HEAD || fail "refusing: $branch is not a fast-forward of $dir; re-clone deliberately"
    after="$(git -C "$dir" rev-parse HEAD)"
    [ "$before" = "$after" ] && echo "current ${after:0:10}" || echo "updated ${before:0:10}..${after:0:10} (restart the bots to use it)"
}

do_install() {
    local body="$lane/openrc/honey-bot" ts problems=() unsafe bot dst
    ts="$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
    [ "$checkout" = "$SYSTEM_CHECKOUT" ] || problems+=("not the system checkout: $checkout (expected $SYSTEM_CHECKOUT)")
    unsafe="$(find "$lane" "$checkout/all/.local/bin" \( ! -user root -o \( ! -type l -perm /022 \) \) -print -quit 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [ -z "$unsafe" ] || problems+=("not root-owned and read-only: $unsafe (and maybe more)")
    for bot in "${BOTS[@]}"; do
        [ -x "$lane/bin/honey-$bot" ] || problems+=("missing launcher $lane/bin/honey-$bot")
    done
    [ -r "$body" ] || problems+=("missing body $body")
    if [ "${#problems[@]}" -gt 0 ]; then
        printf 'honey-claude-install: %s\n' "${problems[@]}" >&2
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || exit 1
        echo "honey-claude-install: dry-run continues; a real run would refuse" >&2
    fi
    [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ] || fail "run as root"
    for bot in "${BOTS[@]}"; do
        dst="/etc/init.d/honey-$bot"
        if [ -e "$dst" ] && cmp -s "$body" "$dst"; then echo "unchanged $dst"; continue; fi
        [ -e "$dst" ] && run cp -p "$dst" "$dst.bak-$ts"
        run install -m 0755 -o root -g root "$body" "$dst"
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || echo "installed $dst"
    done
    echo "launchers: $lane/bin/honey-{opus,sonnet,haiku} (restart each bot to switch)"
}

do_workdirs() {
    local user=agent-claude work_root ts problems=() unsafe bot src dst group
    ts="$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
    work_root="${HONEY_CLAUDE_WORK_ROOT:-$(getent passwd "$user" | cut -d: -f6)/honey-work}"
    group="$(id -gn "$user" 2>/dev/null || echo root)"
    [ "$checkout" = "$SYSTEM_CHECKOUT" ] || problems+=("not the system checkout: $checkout (expected $SYSTEM_CHECKOUT)")
    unsafe="$(find "$lane" \( ! -user root -o \( ! -type l -perm /022 \) \) -print -quit 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [ -z "$unsafe" ] || problems+=("not root-owned and read-only: $unsafe (and maybe more)")
    for bot in "${BOTS[@]}"; do
        [ -r "$lane/CLAUDE.$bot.md" ] || problems+=("missing identity file $lane/CLAUDE.$bot.md")
        [ -d "$work_root/$bot" ] || problems+=("missing working directory $work_root/$bot")
    done
    if [ "${#problems[@]}" -gt 0 ]; then
        printf 'honey-claude-install: %s\n' "${problems[@]}" >&2
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || exit 1
        echo "honey-claude-install: dry-run continues; a real run would refuse" >&2
    fi
    [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ] || fail "run as root"
    for bot in "${BOTS[@]}"; do
        src="$lane/CLAUDE.$bot.md"
        dst="$work_root/$bot/CLAUDE.md"
        if [ -e "$dst" ] && cmp -s "$src" "$dst"; then echo "unchanged $dst"; continue; fi
        [ -e "$dst" ] && run cp -p "$dst" "$dst.bak-$ts"
        run install -m 0644 -o root -g "$group" "$src" "$dst"
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || echo "installed $dst"
    done
    echo "identity files: restart a bot (nurse-joy, Mètsàtron's call) for a changed CLAUDE.md to load"
}

case "$VERB" in
    sync) do_sync ;;
    install) do_install ;;
    workdirs) do_workdirs ;;
esac
#+END_SRC

* FCA sterile DSH worker lane (SOURCE candidate; not Telegram)

This is the bounded source candidate for the Honey FCA synthetic worker. It is a new non-Telegram lane and does not alter the existing Kikin DeepSeek Telegram bot, its OpenCode Go route, its home, or its identity. The sanctioned final owner is =agent-deepseek= at uid/gid 2012 from [[file:agents-users.org][agents-users.org]]. The lane stays offline until a separate API-only egress gate is approved.

The worker uses the standard generated agent toolchain layout under its own home, with a fixed runtime staging directory containing only the reviewed Node executable and DSH package dependency closure. Its DSH home, config, workspace, logs, and future private env path are distinct from the Telegram bot and from the operator's home. No whole DSH installation or home is mounted.

The future private target is =/home/agent-deepseek/.config/deepseek-fca-dsh/private/fca-synthetic.env=. It is not created, populated, transferred, mounted, or read by this candidate. After real identity, closure, effective-config, and network boundaries are accepted, Hermes may securely copy the dedicated NeuralWatt env directly from Kikin to Honey. No key may travel through mail, chat, argv, stdout, or logs; no Aura or unrelated environment is included.

#+BEGIN_SRC text :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/SOURCE.md :comments no
# Generated by agents-bots-honey.org. SOURCE candidate only; not provisioned.
owner: agent-deepseek
uid_gid: 2012:2012
kind: sterile-non-telegram-synthetic-worker
runtime_root: $HOME/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime
node_bind: $HOME/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime/node/bin/node -> /opt/node/bin/node
dsh_bind: $HOME/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime/dsh/node_modules -> /opt/dsh/node_modules
dsh_home: /tmp/dsh-home
workspace: /workspace/fca-synthetic
future_private_env: $HOME/.config/deepseek-fca-dsh/private/fca-synthetic.env
network: none
inherited_environment: cleared
config: disable model, web, shell, filesystem, jobs, workflow, tools, and subagents
telemetry: disabled twice (overlay row session-telemetry-otel and DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED in the launcher environment)
boot: honey-fca-dsh boot-check boots the composed profile (offline) and exits; dsh-config alone proves row composition only
telegram: forbidden
clinical_or_aura_paths: forbidden

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/bin/honey-fca-dsh :shebang "#!/usr/bin/bash -p" :comments no
# Generated by agents-bots-honey.org. Fixed offline candidate; no Telegram path.
# Startup trust (revision 4): the interpreter line is absolute and carries -p, so bash neither runs BASH_ENV/ENV nor imports
# exported functions (BASH_FUNC_*), and it ignores SHELLOPTS, BASHOPTS, CDPATH and GLOBIGNORE. Nothing written inside a script can
# undo code that ran before its first line, so a start without -p (for example "bash honey-fca-dsh") is refused, not trusted.
# Every helper below is an absolute path. Dynamic-loader variables (LD_PRELOAD) act before bash starts; callers must scrub them (sudo env_reset).
case $- in *p*) ;; *) echo 'refused: start through the executable (bash -p), not as an argument to bash' >&2; exit 2 ;; esac
set -euo pipefail
readonly TRUSTED_PATH=/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin
PATH="$TRUSTED_PATH"; readonly PATH; export PATH

ACTION="${1:-help}"
[ "$#" -le 1 ] || { echo 'refused: arbitrary arguments are disabled' >&2; exit 2; }
[ "$(/usr/bin/id -u)" = 2012 ] || { echo 'refused: must run as agent-deepseek uid 2012' >&2; exit 2; }
[ "$(/usr/bin/id -g)" = 2012 ] || { echo 'refused: primary gid must be 2012' >&2; exit 2; }
[ "$(/usr/bin/getent passwd 2012 | /usr/bin/cut -d: -f1)" = agent-deepseek ] || { echo 'refused: uid 2012 owner mismatch' >&2; exit 2; }
PASSWD_HOME="$(/usr/bin/getent passwd agent-deepseek | /usr/bin/cut -d: -f6)"
[ -n "$PASSWD_HOME" ] || { echo 'refused: passwd home is missing' >&2; exit 2; }
[ -n "${HOME:-}" ] && [ "$HOME" = "$PASSWD_HOME" ] || { echo 'refused: inherited HOME mismatch' >&2; exit 2; }
[ "$(/usr/bin/readlink -f -- "$HOME")" = "$HOME" ] || { echo 'refused: inherited HOME is non-canonical' >&2; exit 2; }
case "$ACTION" in probe|dsh-config|boot-check|help) ;; *) echo 'usage: honey-fca-dsh {probe|dsh-config|boot-check|help}' >&2; exit 2 ;; esac

ROOT="$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(/usr/bin/dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd -P)"
STAGE="$HOME/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime"
NODE="$STAGE/node/bin/node"
DSH_MODULES="$STAGE/dsh/node_modules"
CLI="$DSH_MODULES/@deepseek-ai/dsh/lib/bin.js"
FIXTURE="$ROOT/fca-synthetic-fixture"
reject_path() {
  local path="$1" resolved
  case "$path" in
    *"/../"*|../*|*/..|..|*"$HOME/.config"*|*"$HOME/.hermes"*|*"$HOME/Secret Vault"*|/home/gille|/home/gille/*|*state.db*|*aura-hermes.env*)
      echo "refused: forbidden path: $path" >&2; exit 3 ;;
  esac
  resolved="$(/usr/bin/readlink -f -- "$path")"
  [ "$resolved" = "$path" ] || { echo "refused: non-canonical path: $path" >&2; exit 3; }
}
[ "$STAGE" = "$HOME/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime" ] || { echo 'refused: runtime root mismatch' >&2; exit 3; }
[ "$FIXTURE" = "$ROOT/fca-synthetic-fixture" ] || { echo 'refused: fixture root mismatch' >&2; exit 3; }
reject_path "$STAGE"; reject_path "$NODE"; reject_path "$DSH_MODULES"; reject_path "$CLI"; reject_path "$FIXTURE"
[ -x /usr/bin/bwrap ] && [ -x "$NODE" ] && [ -f "$CLI" ] && [ -d "$FIXTURE" ] || {
  echo 'refused: reviewed runtime staging or fixture is incomplete' >&2; exit 3;
}
if [ -n "$(/usr/bin/find "$FIXTURE" -type l -print -quit)" ]; then
  echo 'refused: fixture contains symlinked payload' >&2; exit 3
fi
REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST="$STAGE/dsh/REVIEWED-CLOSURE.sha256"
REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256=REVIEW_REQUIRED
[ "$REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256" != REVIEW_REQUIRED ] || {
  echo 'refused: reviewed runtime closure identity is not supplied' >&2; exit 3;
}
[ -r "$REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST" ] || { echo 'refused: reviewed closure manifest is missing' >&2; exit 3; }
[ "$(/usr/bin/sha256sum "$REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST" | /usr/bin/cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256" ] || {
  echo 'refused: reviewed closure manifest identity mismatch' >&2; exit 3;
}
manifest_paths="$(/usr/bin/mktemp -p /tmp)"; actual_paths="$(/usr/bin/mktemp -p /tmp)"
trap '/usr/bin/rm -f "$manifest_paths" "$actual_paths" "$manifest_paths.sorted"' EXIT
while read -r digest rel extra; do
  [ -n "$digest" ] && [ -n "$rel" ] && [ -z "$extra" ] || { echo 'refused: malformed closure manifest' >&2; exit 3; }
  [[ "$digest" =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$ ]] || { echo 'refused: malformed closure digest' >&2; exit 3; }
  case "$rel" in /*|*"/../"*|../*|*/..|..|*[^A-Za-z0-9_@./-]*) echo 'refused: unsafe closure manifest path' >&2; exit 3 ;; esac
  printf '%s\n' "$rel" >> "$manifest_paths"
done < "$REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST"
/usr/bin/sort -u "$manifest_paths" > "$manifest_paths.sorted"; /usr/bin/mv "$manifest_paths.sorted" "$manifest_paths"
if [ -n "$(cd "$STAGE" && /usr/bin/find node/bin/node dsh/node_modules -type l -print -quit)" ]; then
  echo 'refused: closure symlinks require explicit reviewed handling' >&2; exit 3
fi
(cd "$STAGE" && /usr/bin/find node/bin/node dsh/node_modules -type f -print) | /usr/bin/sort -u > "$actual_paths"
/usr/bin/cmp -s "$manifest_paths" "$actual_paths" || { echo 'refused: closure inventory mismatch' >&2; exit 3; }
(cd "$STAGE" && /usr/bin/sha256sum -c --quiet "$REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST") || {
  echo 'refused: reviewed closure content mismatch' >&2; exit 3;
}
/usr/bin/grep -Fqx node/bin/node "$manifest_paths" || { echo 'refused: Node is absent from reviewed closure' >&2; exit 3; }

BWRAP=/usr/bin/bwrap

common=(
  --unshare-user --unshare-pid --unshare-net --unshare-ipc --unshare-uts
  --unshare-cgroup --uid 2012 --gid 2012
  --ro-bind /usr /usr --ro-bind /bin /bin --ro-bind /lib /lib --ro-bind /lib64 /lib64
  --dir /opt/node --dir /opt/node/bin
  --ro-bind "$NODE" /opt/node/bin/node
  --dir /opt/dsh --dir /opt/dsh/node_modules
  --ro-bind "$DSH_MODULES" /opt/dsh/node_modules
  --ro-bind "$FIXTURE" /workspace/fca-synthetic
  --tmpfs /tmp --proc /proc --dev /dev --chdir /workspace/fca-synthetic
)

# Fixed worker environment (the caller's is never inherited: env -i). DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED makes dsh itself disable the
# session-telemetry-otel row at boot (any non-empty value); the overlay disables the same row, and the namespace has no network behind
# either layer. HOME, DSH_HOME and /tmp are a fresh tmpfs per start: no real credential or settings file can be present in them.
WORKER_ENV=(
  PATH=/opt/node/bin:/usr/bin:/bin HOME=/tmp/worker-home DSH_HOME=/tmp/dsh-home DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1
  LC_CTYPE=C NEURALWATT_BASE_URL=synthetic://disabled NEURALWATT_MODEL=synthetic-only
)

if [ "$ACTION" = help ]; then /usr/bin/sed -n '1,120p' "$ROOT/SOURCE.md"; exit 0; fi
if [ "$ACTION" = probe ]; then
  exec "$BWRAP" "${common[@]}" /usr/bin/env -i "${WORKER_ENV[@]}" \
    /usr/bin/python3 /workspace/fca-synthetic/offline_fixture.py
fi
if [ "$ACTION" = boot-check ]; then
  # Real boot of the composed profile (no --dump-config), then a clean shutdown; prints names only. Exit 0 = booted.
  exec "$BWRAP" "${common[@]}" /usr/bin/env -i "${WORKER_ENV[@]}" \
    /opt/node/bin/node /workspace/fca-synthetic/boot_check.mjs
fi
exec "$BWRAP" "${common[@]}" /usr/bin/env -i "${WORKER_ENV[@]}" \
  /opt/node/bin/node /opt/dsh/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/dsh/lib/bin.js \
  --from-default-profile headless --patch /workspace/fca-synthetic/cordis.patch.yml \
  --profile fca-synthetic --dump-config
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC yaml :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/fca-synthetic-fixture/cordis.patch.yml :comments no
# Candidate-only disable overlay (revision 7). Plugin ids were verified against the REAL headless profile in the sandbox as uid 2012 on
# Node 22.23.1. Every model, tool, web, subagent, workflow, subprocess/sandbox, headless and gateway plugin is disabled; only
# session/storage data-plane plugins remain. Any future capability needs a separate reviewed overlay.

#  - session-telemetry-otel was ENABLED: it needs only `sessions`, builds an OTLP/HTTP exporter to the vendor endpoint and uploads session

# This is the minimum honest bootable model-disabled profile: a data-plane skeleton that idles. It cannot run a task; a model-enabled
