** Real boot proof and telemetry off (revision 7, SOURCE candidate; not provisioned)

Independent review found that =dsh --dump-config= composes rows without importing plugins, so the revision-5 overlay proved row composition only. A real boot of that exact composition fails: nine enabled entries wait for services the overlay disables and boot rejects pending entries. The overlay now disables those nine rows as well (the minimum honest bootable model-disabled profile: a data-plane skeleton that idles and cannot run a task). The enabled =session-telemetry-otel= row (OTLP/HTTP exporter to the vendor endpoint, uploads session history on feedback commit) is disabled in the overlay, and the launcher sets =DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED= for every sandbox entry, so telemetry has two independent hard-off layers behind a namespace with no network. The new =boot-check= launcher mode boots the composed profile offline and shuts it down, printing names only. The tests run the real launcher as the real worker uid 2012 and mutate each layer; nothing here provisions, pins or activates anything.

#+BEGIN_SRC javascript :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/fca-synthetic-fixture/boot_check.mjs :comments no
// Offline boot proof for the sterile FCA DSH worker (SOURCE candidate). Boots the real headless profile with the reviewed overlay inside
// the launcher's sandbox (no network), then reports names only: the boot verdict, any entry that did not activate, and the paths
// (never contents) the boot created under the sterile home. It performs no model, tool or network action. Exit 0 = booted, 1 = failed.
import { readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { runCli } from '/opt/dsh/node_modules/@deepseek-ai/dsh/lib/bin.js';

const argv = ['--from-default-profile', 'headless', '--patch', '/workspace/fca-synthetic/cordis.patch.yml', '--profile', 'fca-synthetic'];
process.argv = [process.argv[0], process.argv[1], ...argv];

function walk(root, rel = '') {
  let names = [];
  for (const entry of readdirSync(join(root, rel), { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const path = join(rel, entry.name);
    names.push(entry.isDirectory() ? `${path}/` : path);
    if (entry.isDirectory() && entry.name !== 'node_modules') names = names.concat(walk(root, path)); // the module farm is links only
  }
  return names.sort();
}
const files = (root) => { try { return walk(root); } catch { return []; } };
const report = (verdict, extra) => process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ boot: verdict, ...extra })}\n`);

const deadline = setTimeout(() => { report('failed', { reason: 'boot deadline exceeded' }); process.exit(1); }, 30000);
try {
  await runCli();
} catch (error) {
  const detail = String(error?.message ?? error).split('\n').filter((line) => !line.startsWith('    at ')).slice(0, 40);
  report('failed', { detail });
  process.exit(1);
}
clearTimeout(deadline);
report('ok', { dsh_home: files(process.env.DSH_HOME), home: files(process.env.HOME) });
process.kill(process.pid, 'SIGTERM'); // the launcher's own signal handler disposes the tree and exits 0
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/boot-proof/test_boot_profile.py :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3" :comments no
"""Real-boot and telemetry-off tests for the sterile FCA DSH worker lane (revision 7, SOURCE candidate; nothing is provisioned).
Runs the REAL launcher as the real worker uid 2012 (NO supplementary groups) in the real bwrap sandbox, on a throwaway copy of the lane
under /tmp whose closure pin is filled in; the production launcher keeps REVIEW_REQUIRED and is never executed. No network, no model,
no credential. Needs passwordless sudo for setpriv and the staged runtime under /home/agent-deepseek. Usage: test_boot_profile.py [LANE_DIR]
LANE_DIR is a tangled lane tree (bin/, fca-synthetic-fixture/); by default the tree is assembled from the files next to this one, with the
two unchanged fixture files taken from DotCortex at the frozen preimage 7fe4080df8."""
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
PREIMAGE = '7fe4080df8'
DOT = '/home/gille/DotCortex'
LANE_REL = 'all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/'
STAGE = '/home/agent-deepseek/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime'
WORKER_HOME = '/home/agent-deepseek'
WORK = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-boot-')
os.chmod(WORK, 0o755)

TELEMETRY_ROW = 'session-telemetry-otel'
BOOT_CLOSURE = {  # row id -> package name that reports "pending" when the row is left enabled
    'session-title-llm': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-session-title-first-prompt-llm',
    'plugin-package-inventory-deepseek': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-plugin-package-inventory-deepseek',
    'permission': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-permission-presets',
    'plan-mode': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-plan-mode',
    'compaction-basic': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-compaction-basic',
    'command-compact': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-command-compact',
    'timeout-policy': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-tool-call-timeout-policy',
    'spill-policy': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-spill-policy',
    'session-checkpoint-policy': '@deepseek-ai/dsh-session-checkpoint-policy',
}
# Everything a boot may create under the sterile DSH_HOME (the module farm under node_modules is links only and is not listed).
EXPECTED_DSH_HOME = {
    'profiles/', 'profiles/fca-synthetic/', 'profiles/fca-synthetic/.dsh-module-fallback/',
    'profiles/fca-synthetic/.dsh-module-fallback/node_modules/', 'profiles/fca-synthetic/cordis.patch.yml',
    'profiles/fca-synthetic/cordis.yml', 'profiles/fca-synthetic/node_modules/', 'profiles/fca-synthetic/package.json',
    'profiles/fca-synthetic/pnpm-workspace.yaml', 'profiles/node_modules/', 'storages/',
}
SECRETISH = re.compile(r'credential|setting|spill|token|secret|\.env|key|auth', re.I)
HAVE_RUNTIME = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'test', '-x', STAGE + '/node/bin/node']).returncode == 0


def read(path):
    with open(path) as handle:
        return handle.read()


def write(path, text):
    with open(path, 'w') as handle:
        handle.write(text)


def git_show(path):
    return subprocess.run(['git', '-C', DOT, 'show', '%s:%s%s' % (PREIMAGE, LANE_REL, path)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout


def assemble(dest):
    """Copy a lane tree to dest (bin/, fca-synthetic-fixture/, SOURCE.md)."""
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and os.path.isdir(sys.argv[1]):
        shutil.copytree(sys.argv[1], dest, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns('__pycache__', 'egress', 'boot-proof'))
    else:
        os.makedirs(dest + '/bin')
        os.makedirs(dest + '/fca-synthetic-fixture/fixtures')
        for src, dst in (('honey-fca-dsh', 'bin/honey-fca-dsh'), ('cordis.patch.yml', 'fca-synthetic-fixture/cordis.patch.yml'),
                         ('boot_check.mjs', 'fca-synthetic-fixture/boot_check.mjs'), ('SOURCE.md', 'SOURCE.md')):
            shutil.copy(os.path.join(HERE, src), os.path.join(dest, dst))
        for rel in ('fca-synthetic-fixture/offline_fixture.py', 'fca-synthetic-fixture/fixtures/offline.json'):
            write(os.path.join(dest, rel), git_show(rel))
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(dest):
        os.chmod(root, 0o755)
        for name in files:
            full = os.path.join(root, name)
            os.chmod(full, 0o755 if name == 'honey-fca-dsh' else 0o644)


def pin(lane):
    manifest = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'sha256sum', STAGE + '/dsh/REVIEWED-CLOSURE.sha256'], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    digest = manifest.stdout.split()[0]
    path = lane + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh'
    text = read(path)
    assert 'REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256=REVIEW_REQUIRED' in text
    write(path, text.replace('REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256=REVIEW_REQUIRED', 'REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256=' + digest))


def make_lane(name, overlay=None, launcher=None, boot_check=None):
    """A pinned throwaway lane; overlay/launcher/boot_check are optional functions text -> text (sabotage or the old preimage)."""
    lane = '%s/%s' % (WORK, name)
    assemble(lane)
    for rel, edit in (('fca-synthetic-fixture/cordis.patch.yml', overlay), ('bin/honey-fca-dsh', launcher),
                      ('fca-synthetic-fixture/boot_check.mjs', boot_check)):
        if edit is not None:
            text = read(os.path.join(lane, rel))
            new = edit(text)
            assert new != text, 'edit did not change %s for %s' % (rel, name)
            write(os.path.join(lane, rel), new)
    pin(lane)
    return lane


def as_worker(argv, env=(), timeout=120):
    cmd = ['sudo', '-n', 'setpriv', '--reuid=2012', '--regid=2012', '--clear-groups', '/usr/bin/env', '-i',
           'HOME=' + WORKER_HOME, 'PATH=/usr/bin:/bin'] + list(env) + list(argv)
    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)


def boot(lane, env=()):
    result = as_worker([lane + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh', 'boot-check'], env)
    report = None
    for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
        if line.startswith('{'):
            report = json.loads(line)
    return result, report


def overlay_rows(text):
    rows, current = [], None
    for line in text.splitlines():
        match = re.match(r'- id: (\S+)\s*$', line)
        if match:
            current = {'id': match.group(1), 'disabled': False}
            rows.append(current)
        elif current is not None and re.match(r'  disabled: true\s*$', line):
            current['disabled'] = True
    return rows


def dump_rows(lane):
    result = as_worker([lane + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh', 'dsh-config'])
    assert result.returncode == 0 and not result.stderr, (result.returncode, result.stderr[:300])
    return {row['id']: row['disabled'] for row in overlay_rows(result.stdout)}


def drop_row(row_id):
    def edit(text):
        return re.sub(r'- id: %s\n  disabled: true\n' % re.escape(row_id), '', text)
    return edit


def drop_telemetry_env(text):
    return text.replace(' DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1', '')


@unittest.skipUnless(HAVE_RUNTIME, 'staged worker runtime or sudo not available')
class BootProfile(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.good = make_lane('good')

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        shutil.rmtree(WORK, ignore_errors=True)

    # ---- static: the two telemetry layers and the closure rows must be present (these fail if either layer is missing) ----
    def test_01_overlay_disables_telemetry_row_and_never_enables_anything(self):
        rows = overlay_rows(read(self.good + '/fca-synthetic-fixture/cordis.patch.yml'))
        by_id = {row['id']: row['disabled'] for row in rows}
        self.assertEqual(by_id.get(TELEMETRY_ROW), True, 'session-telemetry-otel must be disabled in the overlay')
        for row_id in BOOT_CLOSURE:
            self.assertEqual(by_id.get(row_id), True, row_id)
        self.assertEqual([row['id'] for row in rows if not row['disabled']], [], 'the overlay must only disable')
        self.assertEqual(len(by_id), len(rows), 'duplicate row ids')

    def test_02_launcher_sets_telemetry_env_for_every_mode_and_has_no_other_environment(self):
        text = read(self.good + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh')
        block = re.search(r'^WORKER_ENV=\((.*?)^\)', text, re.S | re.M)
        self.assertIsNotNone(block)
        self.assertIn('DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1', block.group(1).split())
        execs = [line for line in text.splitlines() if 'exec "$BWRAP"' in line]
        self.assertEqual(len(execs), 3, 'probe, boot-check and dsh-config are the only sandbox entries')
        for line in execs:
            self.assertIn('/usr/bin/env -i "${WORKER_ENV[@]}"', line)
        self.assertEqual(len(re.findall(r'/usr/bin/env -i', text)), 3, 'every sandbox entry must use the one fixed environment')

    # ---- real boot ----
    def test_03_real_boot_succeeds_offline_with_clean_shutdown_and_no_stderr(self):
        result, report = boot(self.good)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, (result.stdout[-400:], result.stderr[-400:]))
        self.assertEqual(report['boot'], 'ok')
        self.assertEqual(result.stderr, '')

    def test_04_boot_writes_only_the_expected_sterile_paths_and_no_telemetry_identity(self):
        _, report = boot(self.good)
        self.assertEqual(report['home'], [], 'nothing may be written to HOME')
        self.assertEqual(set(report['dsh_home']), EXPECTED_DSH_HOME)
        self.assertNotIn('.anonymous-user-id', report['dsh_home'])
        self.assertEqual([name for name in report['dsh_home'] if SECRETISH.search(name)], [], 'credential/settings/spill file appeared')

    def test_05_the_witness_is_real_when_both_telemetry_layers_are_missing(self):
        lane = make_lane('both-missing', overlay=drop_row(TELEMETRY_ROW), launcher=drop_telemetry_env)
        result, report = boot(lane)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout[-300:])
        self.assertIn('.anonymous-user-id', report['dsh_home'], 'telemetry constructs its exporter and identity when nothing disables it')

    def test_06_each_telemetry_layer_alone_is_sufficient(self):
        for name, kwargs in (('row-only', {'launcher': drop_telemetry_env}), ('env-only', {'overlay': drop_row(TELEMETRY_ROW)})):
            with self.subTest(name):
                result, report = boot(make_lane(name, **kwargs))
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout[-300:])
                self.assertNotIn('.anonymous-user-id', report['dsh_home'])

    def test_07_caller_environment_cannot_turn_telemetry_on_or_move_the_home(self):
        hostile = ['DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=', 'DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE=FEEDBACK_ONLY', 'DSH_TELEMETRY_OTLP_URL=https://telemetry.invalid/v1/logs',
                   'OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT=https://telemetry.invalid', 'DSH_HOME=/tmp/not-the-sandbox', 'NEURALWATT_API_KEY=x']
        result, report = boot(self.good, env=hostile)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout[-300:])
        self.assertNotIn('.anonymous-user-id', report['dsh_home'])
        self.assertEqual(set(report['dsh_home']), EXPECTED_DSH_HOME)

    # ---- the finding: the revision-5 composition does not boot, and boot_check can fail ----
    def test_08_the_revision5_overlay_fails_boot_naming_exactly_the_nine_pending_entries(self):
        old = git_show('fca-synthetic-fixture/cordis.patch.yml')
        lane = make_lane('rev5', overlay=lambda _text: old)
        result, report = boot(lane)
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1)
        self.assertEqual(report['boot'], 'failed')
        pending = [line.split(': pending')[0] for line in report['detail'] if ': pending' in line]
        self.assertEqual(pending[0], '@deepseek-ai/dsh-session-title-first-prompt-llm', 'first rejected entry')
        self.assertEqual(sorted(pending), sorted(BOOT_CLOSURE.values()))

    def test_09_every_boot_closure_row_is_necessary(self):
        for row_id, package in BOOT_CLOSURE.items():
            with self.subTest(row_id):
                result, report = boot(make_lane('no-' + row_id, overlay=drop_row(row_id)))
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, row_id)
                self.assertIn(package + ': pending', '\n'.join(report['detail']))

    # ---- offline modes: unchanged behaviour except the ten rows ----
    def test_10_dsh_config_differs_from_revision5_by_exactly_the_ten_disabled_rows(self):
        old = make_lane('rev5-cfg', overlay=lambda _text: git_show('fca-synthetic-fixture/cordis.patch.yml'))
        before, after = dump_rows(old), dump_rows(self.good)
        self.assertEqual(set(before), set(after))
        flipped = {row for row in after if before[row] != after[row]}
        self.assertEqual(flipped, set(BOOT_CLOSURE) | {TELEMETRY_ROW})
        self.assertTrue(all(after[row] for row in flipped), 'a row went from disabled to enabled')
        self.assertTrue(after[TELEMETRY_ROW])

    def test_11_probe_is_unchanged_and_boot_check_keeps_the_launcher_refusals(self):
        probe = as_worker([self.good + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh', 'probe'])
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(probe.stdout), {'fixture_read': True, 'network_policy': 'none', 'worker_uid': 2012})
        extra = as_worker([self.good + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh', 'boot-check', '--profile', 'x'])
        self.assertEqual(extra.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn('arbitrary arguments are disabled', extra.stderr)
        operator = subprocess.run([self.good + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh', 'boot-check'], capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(operator.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn('must run as agent-deepseek', operator.stderr)

    def test_12_boot_check_run_through_bash_dash_p_refusal_is_kept(self):
        result = as_worker(['/usr/bin/bash', self.good + '/bin/honey-fca-dsh', 'boot-check'])
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2)
        self.assertIn('start through the executable', result.stderr)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    args = [sys.argv[0]] + [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not os.path.isdir(a)]
    unittest.main(argv=args, verbosity=2)

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/boot-proof/mutation-check.sh :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash" :comments no
# Mutation proof for the revision-7 boot/telemetry tests: each sabotaged lane MUST fail test_boot_profile.py. Disposable space only.
set -uo pipefail
HERE="$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)"
TMP="$(mktemp -d)"; trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
fail=0
mutate() { # name file perl-expression (-0pi: whole-file, multi-line capable); the file is a standalone product next to this script
  local name="$1" file="$2" expr="$3" d="$TMP/$1"
  mkdir -p "$d"; cp "$HERE"/honey-fca-dsh "$HERE"/cordis.patch.yml "$HERE"/boot_check.mjs "$HERE"/SOURCE.md "$HERE"/test_boot_profile.py "$d"/
  perl -0pi -e "$expr" "$d/$file"
  if cmp -s "$HERE/$file" "$d/$file"; then echo "MUTATION NOT APPLIED: $name"; fail=1; return; fi
  if python3 -B "$d/test_boot_profile.py" >"$d/out" 2>&1; then echo "SURVIVED (tests too weak): $name"; fail=1
  else echo "killed: $name ($(grep -c '^FAIL\|^ERROR' "$d/out") failing)"; fi
}
mutate telemetry-row-removed       cordis.patch.yml 's/- id: session-telemetry-otel\n  disabled: true\n//'
mutate telemetry-row-enabled       cordis.patch.yml 's/(- id: session-telemetry-otel\n  disabled: )true/${1}false/'
mutate telemetry-env-removed       honey-fca-dsh    's/ DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1//'
mutate telemetry-env-empty         honey-fca-dsh    's/DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1/DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=/'
mutate boot-row-removed            cordis.patch.yml 's/- id: spill-policy\n  disabled: true\n//'
mutate probe-uses-own-env          honey-fca-dsh    's/(if \[ "\$ACTION" = probe \]; then\n  exec "\$BWRAP" "\$\{common\[@\]\}" \/usr\/bin\/env -i )"\$\{WORKER_ENV\[@\]\}"/${1}PATH=\/usr\/bin HOME=\/tmp\/worker-home DSH_HOME=\/tmp\/dsh-home/'
mutate boot-check-inherits-env     honey-fca-dsh    's/(= boot-check \]; then\n.*\n  exec "\$BWRAP" "\$\{common\[@\]\}" \/usr\/bin\/env) -i/${1}/'
mutate boot-check-never-fails      boot_check.mjs   's/(report\(.failed., \{ detail \}\);\n  process\.exit\()1/${1}0/'
mutate boot-check-reports-ok-unbooted boot_check.mjs 's/await runCli\(\);/await Promise.resolve();/'
mutate listing-hides-writes        boot_check.mjs   's/dsh_home: files\(process\.env\.DSH_HOME\)/dsh_home: []/'
exit $fail

** Fixed-upstream egress for the worker (revision 6, SOURCE candidate; not provisioned)

Hermes chose option 2 on 2026-11-06: the worker keeps =--unshare-net= and gets one restricted Unix-socket path to a host relay that can reach =api.neuralwatt.com:443= and nothing else. The relay runs as its own unprivileged identity and passes TLS through untouched (the client verifies the provider certificate and hostname itself); a shim inside the namespace bridges =127.0.0.1:443= to the socket. The existing offline =probe= and =dsh-config= launcher modes are unchanged and gain no network. Nothing below is provisioned, started or activated; the design, the policy, the identity and socket specification, and every open stop condition are in the generated =egress/EGRESS.md=. The tests run synthetic local TLS only: the injected resolver and connector exist in tests, never in production code.

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3 -I"
"""Fixed-upstream TLS passthrough relay for the sterile FCA DSH worker (SOURCE candidate; not provisioned).

Runs on the HOST as a dedicated unprivileged identity (no Aura/service identity, no cortex membership, no supplementary groups).
Listens ONLY on a filesystem Unix socket (no TCP listener). The worker's network namespace has no network at all; a shim inside it
(fca_egress_shim.py) copies bytes from 127.0.0.1:443 to this socket. This relay is the enforcement point.

What it does, in order, per connection:
  1. SO_PEERCRED must be the worker uid (defence in depth over the socket ACL).
  2. Read exactly ONE bounded TLS record, which must be a complete ClientHello; the server_name must be exactly UPSTREAM_HOST.
     No other upstream can be named: there is no CONNECT, SOCKS, URL, config, environment or argv selector. Not even a flag.
  3. Resolve UPSTREAM_HOST itself; every answer must be a public address (private/loopback/link-local/tailnet/CGNAT/ULA/mapped/
     NAT64/6to4/Teredo are refused) and the connection goes to a validated literal address on port 443 only.
  4. Replay the ClientHello and copy opaque bytes both ways. TLS is NEVER terminated: the client verifies the provider certificate
     and hostname itself. This module never loads a TLS library and never holds key material or plaintext.
Fail closed everywhere: malformed or fractured input, resolver or connect failure, upstream close, any deadline or byte bound.
Logs carry fixed reason codes and counters only (never SNI, addresses, headers, bodies, ClientHello bytes or key material).
"""
import ipaddress
import os
import select
import socket
import stat
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

UPSTREAM_HOST = 'api.neuralwatt.com'
UPSTREAM_PORT = 443
SOCKET_DIR = '/run/fca-dsh-egress'
SOCKET_PATH = SOCKET_DIR + '/relay.sock'
WORKER_UID = 2012
FORBIDDEN_IDS = frozenset({0, 1000, 2000, 2012})  # root, gille, cortex, worker: the relay must be none of these
HELLO_DEADLINE = 5.0
CONNECT_DEADLINE = 5.0
IDLE_DEADLINE = 60.0
TOTAL_DEADLINE = 900.0
MAX_CONCURRENT = 4
MAX_DIRECTION_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024
CHUNK = 16384
MAX_RECORD = 16384
BANNED_EXTENSIONS = frozenset({0xfe0d, 0xfe0a, 0xfe09, 0xfe08, 0xff03, 0xffce})  # ECH / ESNI: would hide the real name
DENY_NETWORKS = tuple(ipaddress.ip_network(n) for n in (
    '100.64.0.0/10', '192.0.0.0/24', '198.18.0.0/15', '240.0.0.0/4', '64:ff9b::/96', '64:ff9b:1::/48', '2002::/16',
    '2001::/32', 'fc00::/7', 'fe80::/10', '100::/64', '2001:db8::/32', '::ffff:0:0/96'))
REASONS = frozenset({'peer', 'busy', 'hello', 'sni', 'dns', 'dnsaddr', 'connect', 'peername', 'idle', 'total', 'bytes', 'closed', 'ok'})


class Reject(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code):
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code


class _Reader:
    def __init__(self, data):
        self.d, self.i = data, 0

    def take(self, n):
        if n < 0 or self.i + n > len(self.d):
            raise Reject('hello')
        out = self.d[self.i:self.i + n]
        self.i += n
        return out

    def u8(self):
        return self.take(1)[0]

    def u16(self):
        return struct.unpack('>H', self.take(2))[0]

    def u24(self):
        return struct.unpack('>I', b'\0' + self.take(3))[0]

    def done(self):
        return self.i == len(self.d)


def parse_client_hello_sni(record):
    """Validate one complete TLS record holding a complete ClientHello; return the single server_name (lower-case ASCII)."""
    if len(record) < 5 or record[0] != 0x16 or record[1] != 3 or record[2] not in (1, 2, 3):
        raise Reject('hello')
    length = struct.unpack('>H', record[3:5])[0]
    if not 4 <= length <= MAX_RECORD or len(record) != 5 + length:
        raise Reject('hello')  # includes a ClientHello fractured over several records, and trailing bytes
    r = _Reader(record[5:])
    if r.u8() != 1 or r.u24() != length - 4:
        raise Reject('hello')
    r.take(2)
    r.take(32)
    sid = r.u8()
    if sid > 32:
        raise Reject('hello')
    r.take(sid)
    suites = r.u16()
    if suites < 2 or suites % 2:
        raise Reject('hello')
    r.take(suites)
    comp = r.u8()
    if comp < 1:
        raise Reject('hello')
    r.take(comp)
    ext_len = r.u16()
    ext = _Reader(r.take(ext_len))
    if not r.done():
        raise Reject('hello')
    seen, names = set(), []
    while not ext.done():
        etype, elen = ext.u16(), ext.u16()
        body = ext.take(elen)
        if etype in seen or etype in BANNED_EXTENSIONS:
            raise Reject('sni')
        seen.add(etype)
        if etype == 0:
            lst = _Reader(body)
            inner = _Reader(lst.take(lst.u16()))
            if not lst.done():
                raise Reject('sni')
            while not inner.done():
                if inner.u8() != 0:
                    raise Reject('sni')
                names.append(inner.take(inner.u16()))
    if len(names) != 1:
        raise Reject('sni')
    name = names[0]
    if not 1 <= len(name) <= 253 or any(c not in b'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789-.' for c in name):
        raise Reject('sni')
    if name.startswith(b'.') or name.endswith(b'.') or b'..' in name:
        raise Reject('sni')
    return name.decode('ascii').lower()


def address_allowed(text):
    """True only for a plain public unicast IPv4/IPv6 literal (no scope id, no mapped/translated/tunnel forms)."""
    if not isinstance(text, str) or '%' in text:
        return False
    try:
        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(text)
    except ValueError:
        return False
    if ip.version == 6 and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:
        return False
    if ip.is_multicast or not ip.is_global:
        return False
    return not any(ip in net for net in DENY_NETWORKS)


def default_resolver(host, port):
    return [ai[4][0] for ai in socket.getaddrinfo(host, port, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)]


def default_connector(address, port, timeout):
    family = socket.AF_INET6 if ':' in address else socket.AF_INET
    s = socket.socket(family, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        s.connect((address, port))
        return s
    except BaseException:
        s.close()
        raise


class Relay:
    """Production code builds Relay(SOCKET_PATH). Tests may inject resolver/connector/peer_uid and shorter bounds; nothing is
    selectable at run time (no argv, environment or file configuration is read anywhere in this module)."""

    def __init__(self, path=SOCKET_PATH, peer_uid=WORKER_UID, resolver=default_resolver, connector=default_connector,
                 hello_deadline=HELLO_DEADLINE, connect_deadline=CONNECT_DEADLINE, idle_deadline=IDLE_DEADLINE,
                 total_deadline=TOTAL_DEADLINE, max_concurrent=MAX_CONCURRENT, max_bytes=MAX_DIRECTION_BYTES, log=None):
        self.path, self.peer_uid, self.resolver, self.connector = path, peer_uid, resolver, connector
        self.hello_deadline, self.connect_deadline = hello_deadline, connect_deadline
        self.idle_deadline, self.total_deadline, self.max_bytes = idle_deadline, total_deadline, max_bytes
        self.slots = threading.BoundedSemaphore(max_concurrent)
        self.resolving = threading.Lock()
        self.counters = {code: 0 for code in REASONS}
        self.log = log or (lambda line: print(line, file=sys.stderr, flush=True))
        self.listener = None
        self.stopping = False

    def note(self, code):
        assert code in REASONS
        self.counters[code] += 1
        self.log('event=%s total=%d' % (code, self.counters[code]))

    # -- listener -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def bind(self):
        directory = os.path.dirname(self.path)
        st = os.lstat(directory)
        if not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.geteuid() or st.st_mode & 0o027:
            raise SystemExit('refused: socket directory must be a real directory owned by the relay identity, no other access')
        try:
            st = os.lstat(self.path)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            st = None
        if st is not None:
            if not stat.S_ISSOCK(st.st_mode):
                raise SystemExit('refused: socket path exists and is not a socket')
            probe = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
            try:
                probe.settimeout(1)
                probe.connect(self.path)
            except OSError:
                os.unlink(self.path)
            else:
                raise SystemExit('refused: a relay is already listening')
            finally:
                probe.close()
        old = os.umask(0o177)
        try:
            self.listener = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            self.listener.bind(self.path)
        finally:
            os.umask(old)
        os.chmod(self.path, 0o600)
        if self.peer_uid is not None and os.geteuid() != self.peer_uid:
            subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/setfacl', '-m', 'u:%d:rw-' % self.peer_uid, self.path], check=True, env={},
                           stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=10)
        self.listener.listen(8)

    def serve_forever(self):
        while not self.stopping:
            try:
                conn, _ = self.listener.accept()
            except OSError:
                return
            threading.Thread(target=self.handle, args=(conn,), daemon=True).start()

    def stop(self):
        self.stopping = True
        try:
            self.listener.close()
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            os.unlink(self.path)
        except OSError:
            pass

    # -- one connection -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _read_exact(self, conn, n, deadline):
        out = b''
        while len(out) < n:
            left = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if left <= 0:
                raise Reject('hello')
            conn.settimeout(left)
            try:
                chunk = conn.recv(n - len(out))  # plain recv: ancillary data (fds) is discarded by the kernel, never accepted
            except (socket.timeout, OSError):
                raise Reject('hello')
            if not chunk:
                raise Reject('hello')
            out += chunk
        return out

    def _resolve(self):
        if not self.resolving.acquire(timeout=self.connect_deadline):  # wait for a lookup already in flight; refuse only if it outlasts the deadline
            raise Reject('dns')  # one lookup at a time; a stuck resolver cannot pile up threads
        box = {}

        def work():
            try:
                box['v'] = list(self.resolver(UPSTREAM_HOST, UPSTREAM_PORT))
            except BaseException:
                box['e'] = True
            finally:
                self.resolving.release()

        t = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True)
        t.start()
        t.join(self.connect_deadline)
        if 'v' not in box or not box['v']:
            raise Reject('dns')
        out = []
        for a in box['v']:
            if not address_allowed(a):
                raise Reject('dnsaddr')  # one bad answer poisons the whole set (rebinding / split-horizon)
            if a not in out:
                out.append(a)
        return out

    def _connect(self, addresses):
        end = time.monotonic() + self.connect_deadline
        for address in addresses:
            left = end - time.monotonic()
            if left <= 0:
                break
            try:
                up = self.connector(address, UPSTREAM_PORT, left)
            except (OSError, socket.timeout):
                continue
            try:
                peer = up.getpeername()[0]
                if ipaddress.ip_address(peer) != ipaddress.ip_address(address):
                    raise Reject('peername')
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                up.close()
                raise Reject('peername')
            except Reject:
                up.close()
                raise
            return up
        raise Reject('connect')

    def handle(self, conn):
        if not self.slots.acquire(blocking=False):
            self.note('busy')
            conn.close()
            return
        up = None
        try:
            cred = conn.getsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_PEERCRED, struct.calcsize('3i'))
            if self.peer_uid is not None and struct.unpack('3i', cred)[1] != self.peer_uid:
                raise Reject('peer')
            deadline = time.monotonic() + self.hello_deadline
            header = self._read_exact(conn, 5, deadline)
            if header[0] != 0x16:
                raise Reject('hello')
            n = struct.unpack('>H', header[3:5])[0]
            if not 4 <= n <= MAX_RECORD:
                raise Reject('hello')
            record = header + self._read_exact(conn, n, deadline)
            if parse_client_hello_sni(record) != UPSTREAM_HOST:
                raise Reject('sni')
            up = self._connect(self._resolve())
            up.settimeout(self.connect_deadline)
            up.sendall(record)
            self.note(self._pump(conn, up))
        except Reject as r:
            self.note(r.code)
        except OSError:
            self.note('closed')
        finally:
            for s in (up, conn):
                if s is not None:
                    try:
                        s.close()
                    except OSError:
                        pass
            self.slots.release()

    def _pump(self, a, b):
        start = time.monotonic()
        sent = {a: 0, b: 0}
        open_ = {a, b}
        peer = {a: b, b: a}
        for s in (a, b):
            s.setblocking(True)
        while open_:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - start >= self.total_deadline:
                return 'total'
            ready, _, _ = select.select(list(open_), [], [], min(self.idle_deadline, self.total_deadline - (now - start)))
            if not ready:
                return 'idle' if time.monotonic() - start < self.total_deadline else 'total'
            for s in ready:
                try:
                    s.settimeout(self.idle_deadline)
                    chunk = s.recv(CHUNK)
                except (socket.timeout, OSError):
                    return 'closed'
                if not chunk:
                    open_.discard(s)
                    try:
                        peer[s].shutdown(socket.SHUT_WR)
                    except OSError:
                        pass
                    continue
                sent[s] += len(chunk)
                if sent[s] > self.max_bytes:
                    return 'bytes'
                try:
                    peer[s].settimeout(self.idle_deadline)
                    peer[s].sendall(chunk)
                except (socket.timeout, OSError):
                    return 'closed'
        return 'ok'


def _harden():
    if os.geteuid() in FORBIDDEN_IDS or os.getegid() in FORBIDDEN_IDS or os.getuid() != os.geteuid() or os.getgid() != os.getegid():
        raise SystemExit('refused: relay must run as its own dedicated unprivileged identity')
    if set(os.getgroups()) - {os.getegid()}:
        raise SystemExit('refused: relay must have no supplementary groups')
    import ctypes
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    if libc.prctl(38, 1, 0, 0, 0) != 0 or libc.prctl(4, 0, 0, 0, 0) != 0:  # PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS, PR_SET_DUMPABLE=0
        raise SystemExit('refused: could not set no_new_privs/undumpable')


def main(argv):
    if len(argv) != 1:
        print('refused: this relay takes no arguments and reads no configuration', file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    _harden()
    relay = Relay()
    relay.bind()
    relay.serve_forever()
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv))
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_shim.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3 -I"
"""Inside-namespace loopback shim for the sterile FCA DSH worker (SOURCE candidate; not provisioned).

Runs INSIDE the bwrap network namespace (which has no network but a loopback). It listens on 127.0.0.1:443, where /etc/hosts maps
api.neuralwatt.com, and copies opaque bytes to the one bound Unix socket of the host relay. It parses nothing and enforces nothing:
the relay is the enforcement point and the namespace stays offline. Usage: fca_egress_shim.py -- COMMAND [ARG...]; it binds the port
(the sandbox grants CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE for exactly this), drops every capability, then runs COMMAND with the worker's environment.
Fail closed: if the relay socket is absent or the relay dies the client connection is closed at once. No fallback path exists."""
import ctypes
import os
import platform
import select
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time

LISTEN = ('127.0.0.1', 443)
RELAY_SOCKET = '/run/fca-egress/relay.sock'
MAX_CONCURRENT = 8
IDLE_DEADLINE = 60.0
TOTAL_DEADLINE = 900.0
CHUNK = 16384


class _CapData(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('effective', ctypes.c_uint32), ('permitted', ctypes.c_uint32), ('inheritable', ctypes.c_uint32)]


def drop_all_capabilities():
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    libc.prctl(47, 4, 0, 0, 0)  # PR_CAP_AMBIENT, PR_CAP_AMBIENT_CLEAR_ALL
    header = (ctypes.c_uint32 * 2)(0x20080522, 0)  # _LINUX_CAPABILITY_VERSION_3, this thread
    data = (_CapData * 2)()
    nr = {'x86_64': 126, 'aarch64': 91}[platform.machine()]  # SYS_capset
    if libc.syscall(nr, ctypes.byref(header), ctypes.byref(data)) != 0:  # capset(zeroed sets)
        raise SystemExit('refused: could not drop capabilities')
    if libc.prctl(38, 1, 0, 0, 0) != 0:  # PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS
        raise SystemExit('refused: could not set no_new_privs')


def pump(a, b):
    start, open_ = time.monotonic(), {a, b}
    peer = {a: b, b: a}
    while open_:
        left = TOTAL_DEADLINE - (time.monotonic() - start)
        if left <= 0:
            return
        ready, _, _ = select.select(list(open_), [], [], min(IDLE_DEADLINE, left))
        if not ready:
            return
        for s in ready:
            try:
                s.settimeout(IDLE_DEADLINE)
                chunk = s.recv(CHUNK)
                if not chunk:
                    open_.discard(s)
                    peer[s].shutdown(socket.SHUT_WR)
                    continue
                peer[s].settimeout(IDLE_DEADLINE)
                peer[s].sendall(chunk)
            except (OSError, socket.timeout):
                return


def handle(client, slots):
    relay = None
    try:
        relay = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        relay.settimeout(5)
        relay.connect(RELAY_SOCKET)  # absent socket or dead relay: ENOENT/ECONNREFUSED, the client is closed below
        pump(client, relay)
    except OSError:
        pass
    finally:
        for s in (relay, client):
            if s is not None:
                try:
                    s.close()
                except OSError:
                    pass
        slots.release()


def serve(listener):
    slots = threading.BoundedSemaphore(MAX_CONCURRENT)
    while True:
        try:
            client, _ = listener.accept()
        except OSError:
            return
        if not slots.acquire(blocking=False):
            client.close()
            continue
        threading.Thread(target=handle, args=(client, slots), daemon=True).start()


def main(argv):
    if len(argv) < 3 or argv[1] != '--':
        print('usage: fca_egress_shim.py -- COMMAND [ARG...]', file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    listener = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    listener.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    listener.bind(LISTEN)
    listener.listen(16)
    drop_all_capabilities()  # per-thread: done before any thread starts, so the accept thread and the child inherit none
    threading.Thread(target=serve, args=(listener,), daemon=True).start()
    return subprocess.run(argv[2:], check=False).returncode


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv))
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC text :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/EGRESS.md :comments no
# Generated by agents-bots-honey.org. SOURCE candidate only; nothing here is provisioned, started or activated.
# FCA DSH worker: fixed-upstream egress (revision 6)

Decision (Hermes, 2026-11-06): keep `--unshare-net`; give the worker ONE restricted Unix-socket path to a host relay that can reach
`api.neuralwatt.com:443` and nothing else. No host-network access, no broad skuid/CDN-IP rule.

    worker (bwrap, uid 2012, no network)                         host (separate unprivileged identity)
    node/DSH --TLS--> 127.0.0.1:443 --shim--> /run/fca-egress/relay.sock ==> fca_egress_relay.py --TCP:443--> api.neuralwatt.com

The relay is the enforcement point; the shim parses nothing. TLS is passthrough: the client verifies the provider certificate and
hostname itself (Node's bundled roots), so the relay never holds a certificate, key or plaintext.

## Files (all tangled under honey-fca-dsh/egress/)
- `fca_egress_relay.py` host relay. No argv, no environment, no config file, no TCP listener, no TLS library. Constants only.
- `fca_egress_shim.py` runs inside the sandbox: binds 127.0.0.1:443, drops every capability, then runs the worker command.
- `hosts` maps `api.neuralwatt.com` to 127.0.0.1 inside the namespace (there is no resolver, so nothing else resolves).
- `test_egress_relay.py`, `test_egress_sandbox.py`, `sandbox_*.py`, `relay_runner.py`, `mutation-check.sh`, `test-identity-nocortex.sh`: adversarial
  suites and mutation proof (synthetic local TLS only; injected resolver/connector exist in tests only).
- `cortex-removal.sh` the one-membership removal/rollback candidate (dry run by default).

## Relay policy (what is refused, fail closed)
Peer uid must be 2012 (SO_PEERCRED, on top of the socket ACL). Exactly one TLS record that is a whole ClientHello with exactly one
server_name equal to `api.neuralwatt.com` (case-insensitive); IP-literal, missing, wrong, look-alike, trailing-dot, duplicate or multiple
names, ECH/ESNI extensions, fractured or oversized records and trailing bytes are refused. The relay resolves the name itself and refuses
the whole answer set if ANY address is non-public (private, loopback, link-local, tailnet/CGNAT, ULA, mapped, NAT64, 6to4, Teredo,
multicast); it connects only to a validated literal on port 443 and checks the peer address afterwards. Bounds: ClientHello 5 s,
connect 5 s, idle 60 s, total 900 s, 4 concurrent, 64 MiB per direction, 16 KiB chunks. Logs are `event=<code> total=<n>` only.

## Close and truncation semantics (what the worker can and cannot see)
Question (Hermes, rev6b review): when the upstream dies mid-response, what does the worker see? Answer, measured and tested (revision 6c):
- After the ClientHello the relay is a byte pump. On an upstream FIN it forwards every byte it received, then half-closes toward the worker (FIN);
  on an upstream error, reset or timeout, or on its own idle, total or byte limit, it closes both sides. It never adds, drops or repairs a byte
  (`test_relay_forwards_exactly_the_upstream_bytes_then_a_fin_even_when_the_last_record_is_incomplete`).
- The relay cannot tell an orderly close from a truncation that ends on a TLS record boundary: TLS 1.3 encrypts alerts, so `close_notify` is
  opaque to it, and an upstream FIN with and without `close_notify` look the same on the wire it can read. It also cannot make a close louder:
  the worker side is a Unix stream socket and then a loopback TCP socket inside the namespace, and a Unix stream has no RST. Injecting garbage to
  force an error would break every legitimate close too, so this is deliberately not done (option (b) of the review, not (a)).
- What a client therefore sees: a LENIENT TLS client (Python's default, OpenSSL ignoring unexpected EOF) sees a clean empty end of stream for a
  truncated response, with no invented bytes (`test_truncation_without_close_notify_...`). A STRICT client sees the missing `close_notify` as an
  error through the relay, because TLS is end to end (`test_a_strict_client_sees_the_same_truncation_as_an_error_...`). An upstream reset is counted
  as `closed` and delivers nothing extra.
- Measured with the real DSH Node 22 through shim, socket and relay (`test_06b`, Honey only): a response whose body is shorter than its
  `Content-Length`, with or without `close_notify`, and a chunked body without its terminating chunk, are all reported INCOMPLETE (ECONNRESET).
  A close-delimited body (no `Content-Length`, no chunking, `Connection: close`) is reported COMPLETE even when truncated, and nothing at this layer can
  change that.
- RULE for every consumer of this path: truncation is detected by HTTP framing only. Treat a short `Content-Length` body or a missing chunk
  terminator as a failure, never rely on the TLS close, and never accept a close-delimited response for anything that matters.

## Close reasons (the `event=<code>` in the relay log; every connection ends with exactly one)
| code | fires when |
|---|---|
| `ok` | both directions ended normally (each side sent its FIN) |
| `peer` | the connecting uid is not the worker's (SO_PEERCRED) |
| `busy` | all 4 slots are in use; refused at once |
| `hello` | the first record is not one whole, well-formed ClientHello within 5 s (fractured, oversized, trailing bytes, silent) |
| `sni` | the server_name is not exactly `api.neuralwatt.com` (IP literal, missing, look-alike, duplicate, multiple, ECH/ESNI) |
| `dns` | the name lookup failed or returned nothing, OR the bounded wait for a lookup already in flight (at most the connect deadline, 5 s) expired. It does NOT mean that another connection merely happened to be resolving at that moment (it did before rev6d) |
| `dnsaddr` | any address in the answer set is not public |
| `connect` | no validated address accepted a connection within the connect deadline (5 s) |
| `peername` | the connected peer's address differs from the validated literal |
| `idle` | no traffic in either direction for 60 s |
| `total` | the connection reached 900 s |
| `bytes` | more than 64 MiB in one direction |
| `closed` | an I/O error, reset or timeout while reading the hello or forwarding (including an upstream reset) |

## Name lookups are serialized, and a second connection WAITS (revision 6d)
The relay resolves the name itself, one lookup at a time, so a stuck resolver cannot leak a thread per connection (a new lookup starts only when the previous
lookup thread has finished). rev6 to rev6c refused a connection IMMEDIATELY with `dns` if another lookup was in flight: two worker connections that started inside one
`getaddrinfo()` window lost one of them to a reset (found by CPU-starvation stress; `test_two_simultaneous_connections_with_a_slow_lookup_are_both_served`). Now the
second connection waits for the lock for at most the connect deadline (5 s) and is refused with `dns` only if the lookup outlasts it; waiters are bounded by the
4 concurrent slots (`test_a_stuck_resolver_cannot_pile_up_threads_and_waiters_are_bounded`). Clients should still retry a refused connection once.

## Identity and socket (spec for the later, human-released provisioning step; not run)
    useradd --system --no-create-home --shell /usr/sbin/nologin --user-group fca-egress     # no supplementary groups, not cortex
    install -d -o fca-egress -g fca-egress -m 0700 /run/fca-dsh-egress
    setfacl -m u:2012:--x /run/fca-dsh-egress                                               # traverse only
    # the relay itself creates relay.sock (umask 0177) and runs: setfacl -m u:2012:rw- relay.sock   (u:2012 only)
The relay refuses to start as root, uid 1000/2000/2012, or with ANY supplementary group.

## Service unit (`../openrc/`; human-released, not installed by this candidate)
`openrc/honey-fca-egress` is the OpenRC body: `command=/usr/bin/python3`, `command_args="-I /opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py"`,
`command_user=fca-egress:fca-egress`, no other argument. `start_pre` refuses a missing or uid 0 identity, any supplementary group, a worker that
is not uid 2012, and a relay directory that is not root-owned and read-only, then runs the `checkpath`/`setfacl` above (`/run` is tmpfs, so
every start recreates the directory; running it again changes nothing and corrects a loosened mode). OpenRC drops to `fca-egress` itself
(`command_user`, via start-stop-daemon); the relay is never run as root. `start_post` fails the start if `relay.sock` does not appear within 10 s, so a
start is never half-alive. The relay needs no tailnet: it listens on a Unix socket only and REFUSES tailnet addresses as destinations; the unit depends on
`net`/`dns` and a network fault shows per connection as `dns`/`connect`. The unit defines its own `stop`: it signals by pidfile and the `fca-egress` uid ONLY and never passes `--exec`, because the default OpenRC stop does, `command` is `/usr/bin/python3`, and with no pidfile `start-stop-daemon` then signals every OpenRC-started python3 process on the host. `stop_post` removes the socket only, never the code or the directory.
`openrc/honey-fca-egress-install` places ONLY the relay and the unit, and only when the relay's sha256 equals the `--expect-relay-sha256` you
pass (the hash recorded in the review). It starts nothing. `enable` links `/etc/rc2.d/S04honey-fca-egress`: this host boots sysvinit, so the
symlink is the evidence of boot durability, not `rc-update`. Order, each a separate human-released step: the `useradd` above, `install`,
`rc-service honey-fca-egress start`, `enable`.

## Sandbox fragment for the later model-enabled overlay (offline probe/dsh-config launcher modes are unchanged)
Add to the bwrap argv only for that separately reviewed mode:
    --cap-add CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE --ro-bind $LANE/egress/hosts /etc/hosts --ro-bind /run/fca-dsh-egress /run/fca-egress
    --ro-bind $LANE/egress/fca_egress_shim.py /opt/egress/shim.py     and run: /usr/bin/python3 -I /opt/egress/shim.py -- <command>
Do NOT use `--dev /dev` in that mode: reproduced 2026-11-06 (bwrap 0.11.0, kernel 7.2.0-rc2) that with it the in-namespace bind of
443 is denied although CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE is held. Bind `/dev/null`, `/dev/zero`, `/dev/urandom`, `/dev/random` with `--dev-bind`.
The model-enabled DSH overlay, its base URL (`https://api.neuralwatt.com/...`, default port) and the env file are not part of this candidate.

## Deployment path
Run the deployed lane from a worker-readable system path (for example a root-owned `/opt/honey-fca-dsh` checkout), never from
`/home/gille`: without cortex the sealed operator home is unreachable by uid 2012 (`test-identity-nocortex.sh`).

## cortex removal (separate, human-released, one membership only)
`cortex-removal.sh` (dry run by default) runs `gpasswd -d agent-deepseek cortex`; rollback `gpasswd -a agent-deepseek cortex`; it checks
that only that membership changed. NOTE: agents-users.org `agent-users-bootstrap` runs `usermod -aG cortex` for every agent on every
run, so a later bootstrap re-adds it unless canon is changed (host-conditional, because the Kikin Telegram deepseek bot shares the uid).

## Stop conditions still open (not done by this candidate)
No relay provisioning, no service start, no network or firewall change, no key or env file, no model request, no closure pin in the
production launcher, no activation of the relay or of any model-enabled mode.
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/cortex-removal.sh :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/bash -p"
# Least-privilege change for the sterile FCA worker: remove ONLY agent-deepseek's supplementary cortex membership. Touches no other
# user or group. Default is a dry run that prints the plan; --apply and --rollback need root and a human release (not run by this
# candidate). The worker owns its runtime/fixture (test-identity-nocortex.sh proves the lane runs without cortex); cortex would
# otherwise give it traverse access to the sealed operator home. A re-run of agent-users-bootstrap re-adds cortex (see ROLLOUT).
set -euo pipefail
PATH=/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin; export PATH
USER_NAME=agent-deepseek; GROUP_NAME=cortex; WANT_UID=2012
MODE="${1:-dry-run}"; [ "$#" -le 1 ] || { echo 'refused: one optional argument' >&2; exit 2; }
case "$MODE" in dry-run|--apply|--rollback) ;; *) echo 'usage: cortex-removal.sh [--apply|--rollback]' >&2; exit 2 ;; esac
[ "$(/usr/bin/id -u "$USER_NAME")" = "$WANT_UID" ] || { echo "refused: $USER_NAME is not uid $WANT_UID" >&2; exit 3; }
members() { /usr/bin/getent group "$GROUP_NAME" | /usr/bin/cut -d: -f4; }
groups_of() { /usr/bin/id -nG "$USER_NAME" | /usr/bin/tr ' ' '\n' | /usr/bin/sort | /usr/bin/paste -sd,; }
echo "before: $USER_NAME groups=$(groups_of); $GROUP_NAME members=$(members)"
before_members="$(members)"
case "$MODE" in
  dry-run)  echo "plan: /usr/bin/gpasswd -d $USER_NAME $GROUP_NAME    (rollback: /usr/bin/gpasswd -a $USER_NAME $GROUP_NAME)"; exit 0 ;;
  --apply)    [ "$(/usr/bin/id -u)" = 0 ] || { echo 'refused: run as root' >&2; exit 3; }
              /usr/bin/gpasswd -d "$USER_NAME" "$GROUP_NAME"
              want="$(printf '%s' "$before_members" | /usr/bin/tr ',' '\n' | /usr/bin/grep -vx "$USER_NAME" | /usr/bin/paste -sd,)" ;;
  --rollback) [ "$(/usr/bin/id -u)" = 0 ] || { echo 'refused: run as root' >&2; exit 3; }
              /usr/bin/gpasswd -a "$USER_NAME" "$GROUP_NAME"
              want="$before_members,$USER_NAME"; want="${want#,}" ;;
esac
after="$(members)"
echo "after:  $USER_NAME groups=$(groups_of); $GROUP_NAME members=$after"
# Every other member must be untouched (order-insensitive); the target is the only difference.
[ "$(printf '%s' "$after" | /usr/bin/tr ',' '\n' | /usr/bin/sort | /usr/bin/paste -sd,)" = "$(printf '%s' "$want" | /usr/bin/tr ',' '\n' | /usr/bin/sort | /usr/bin/paste -sd,)" ] || { echo 'FAILED: membership differs from the expected set' >&2; exit 4; }
[ "$MODE" = --rollback ] || ! /usr/bin/id -nG "$USER_NAME" | /usr/bin/tr ' ' '\n' | /usr/bin/grep -qx "$GROUP_NAME" || { echo 'FAILED: still a member' >&2; exit 4; }
echo "ok"
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/test_egress_relay.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3"
"""Adversarial tests for fca_egress_relay.py. Synthetic local TLS fixtures and injected resolver/connector are used IN TESTS ONLY;
no provider credential, no live model request, no outbound network. Usage: test_egress_relay.py [DIR_WITH_RELAY_MODULE]"""
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import os
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import socket
import ssl
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
import warnings

warnings.simplefilter('ignore', ResourceWarning)
import faulthandler
faulthandler.dump_traceback_later(int(os.environ.get('FCA_TEST_WATCHDOG', '180')), exit=True)  # a stall names itself instead of hanging
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
MODDIR = sys.argv.pop(1) if len(sys.argv) > 1 and os.path.isdir(sys.argv[1]) else HERE
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('fca_egress_relay', os.path.join(MODDIR, 'fca_egress_relay.py'))
R = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(R)
HOST = R.UPSTREAM_HOST
PUBLIC = '93.184.216.34'
TMP = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-egress-test-')


def sh(*argv):
    subprocess.run(argv, check=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)


def make_pki():
    sh('openssl', 'req', '-x509', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048', '-nodes', '-keyout', TMP + '/ca.key', '-out', TMP + '/ca.pem',
       '-subj', '/CN=fca-synthetic-test-ca', '-days', '2', '-addext', 'basicConstraints=critical,CA:TRUE', '-addext', 'keyUsage=critical,keyCertSign,cRLSign')
    for tag, name in (('good', HOST), ('wrongname', 'other.example.test')):
        sh('openssl', 'req', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048', '-nodes', '-keyout', TMP + '/%s.key' % tag, '-out', TMP + '/%s.csr' % tag,
           '-subj', '/CN=' + name)
        with open(TMP + '/%s.ext' % tag, 'w') as f:
            f.write('subjectAltName=DNS:%s\n' % name)
        sh('openssl', 'x509', '-req', '-in', TMP + '/%s.csr' % tag, '-CA', TMP + '/ca.pem', '-CAkey', TMP + '/ca.key',
           '-CAcreateserial', '-out', TMP + '/%s.pem' % tag, '-days', '2', '-extfile', TMP + '/%s.ext' % tag)
    sh('openssl', 'req', '-x509', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048', '-nodes', '-keyout', TMP + '/ca2.key', '-out', TMP + '/ca2.pem',
       '-subj', '/CN=unrelated-ca', '-days', '2')


# ---- ClientHello builders --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def real_hello(name=HOST, verify=True):
    ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
    ctx.check_hostname = False
    ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE
    inb, outb = ssl.MemoryBIO(), ssl.MemoryBIO()
    obj = ctx.wrap_bio(inb, outb, server_hostname=name)
    try:
        obj.do_handshake()
    except ssl.SSLWantReadError:
        pass
    return outb.read()


def sni_ext(*names):
    entries = b''.join(b'\0' + struct.pack('>H', len(n)) + n for n in names)
    lst = struct.pack('>H', len(entries)) + entries
    return struct.pack('>HH', 0, len(lst)) + lst


def build_hello(exts=None, name=HOST.encode(), sid=b'', trailing=b'', hs_type=1, rec_type=0x16, split_at=None):
    exts = [sni_ext(name)] if exts is None else exts
    body = b'\x03\x03' + os.urandom(32) + bytes([len(sid)]) + sid + b'\0\x02\x13\x01' + b'\1\0'
    e = b''.join(exts)
    body += struct.pack('>H', len(e)) + e
    hs = bytes([hs_type]) + struct.pack('>I', len(body))[1:] + body + trailing
    if split_at:
        a, b = hs[:split_at], hs[split_at:]
        return (bytes([rec_type]) + b'\3\1' + struct.pack('>H', len(a)) + a +
                bytes([rec_type]) + b'\3\1' + struct.pack('>H', len(b)) + b)
    return bytes([rec_type]) + b'\3\1' + struct.pack('>H', len(hs)) + hs


# ---- mock upstream and relay harness -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
class MockUpstream:
    def __init__(self, certtag='good', mode='echo'):
        self.mode, self.sock = mode, socket.socket()
        self.sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
        self.sock.listen(16)
        self.port = self.sock.getsockname()[1]
        self.ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_SERVER)
        self.ctx.load_cert_chain(TMP + '/%s.pem' % certtag, TMP + '/%s.key' % certtag)
        self.held = []
        self.requests = []
        threading.Thread(target=self.loop, daemon=True).start()

    def loop(self):
        while True:
            try:
                c, _ = self.sock.accept()
            except OSError:
                return
            threading.Thread(target=self.one, args=(c,), daemon=True).start()

    def one(self, c):
        try:
            if self.mode == 'silent':
                self.held.append(c)
                return
            t = self.ctx.wrap_socket(c, server_side=True)
            buf = b''
            while b'\r\n\r\n' not in buf:
                d = t.recv(4096)
                if not d:
                    return
                buf += d
            self.requests.append(buf)
            if self.mode == 'close-after-request':
                t.close()  # TCP FIN with NO TLS close_notify: a truncation as the relay sees it. The request was read first, so this can never be a RST.
                return
            if self.mode == 'reset-after-request':
                t.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_LINGER, struct.pack('ii', 1, 0))
                t.close()  # TCP RST
                return
            if self.mode == 'firehose':
                while True:
                    t.sendall(b'x' * 8192)
            t.sendall(b'mock-ok ' + hashlib.sha256(buf).hexdigest().encode())
            t.close()
        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
            pass

    def close(self):
        self.sock.close()
        for c in self.held:
            c.close()


class RawUpstream:
    """Not TLS: reads exactly the forwarded ClientHello record, then sends `payload` and ends the connection with a FIN or a RST.
    Deterministic: the close happens only after the hello was read and nothing else is pending."""
    def __init__(self, payload, how='fin'):
        self.payload, self.how, self.sock = payload, how, socket.socket()
        self.sock.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
        self.sock.listen(16)
        self.port = self.sock.getsockname()[1]
        threading.Thread(target=self.loop, daemon=True).start()

    def loop(self):
        while True:
            try:
                c, _ = self.sock.accept()
            except OSError:
                return
            threading.Thread(target=self.one, args=(c,), daemon=True).start()

    def one(self, c):
        try:
            head = b''
            while len(head) < 5:
                head += c.recv(5 - len(head))
            need = struct.unpack('>H', head[3:5])[0]
            while need:
                need -= len(c.recv(need))
            c.sendall(self.payload)
            if self.how == 'rst':
                c.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_LINGER, struct.pack('ii', 1, 0))
            else:
                c.shutdown(socket.SHUT_WR)
            c.close()
        except OSError:
            pass

    def close(self):
        self.sock.close()


class Up:
    """Socket proxy: the connector is injected in tests only; getpeername reports the validated address."""
    def __init__(self, s, address):
        self._s, self._a = s, address

    def getpeername(self):
        return (self._a, 443)

    def fileno(self):
        return self._s.fileno()

    def __getattr__(self, n):
        return getattr(self._s, n)


class Harness:
    def __init__(self, upstream=None, resolver=None, connector=None, **kw):
        self.dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='sock', dir=TMP)
        os.chmod(self.dir, 0o700)
        self.path = self.dir + '/relay.sock'
        self.logs, self.calls, self.upstream = [], [], upstream
        self.resolver = resolver or (lambda h, p: [PUBLIC])

        def connector_default(addr, port, timeout):
            self.calls.append((addr, port))
            if upstream is None:
                raise OSError('no upstream')
            return Up(socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1', upstream.port), timeout), addr)
        self.relay = R.Relay(self.path, peer_uid=os.getuid(), resolver=self.resolver, connector=connector or connector_default,
                             log=self.logs.append, **kw)
        self.relay.bind()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.relay.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()

    def tls(self, name=HOST, cafile='ca.pem', request=b'GET /canary HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\n\r\n', timeout=10):
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        s.connect(self.path)
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=TMP + '/' + cafile)
        t = ctx.wrap_socket(s, server_hostname=name)
        t.sendall(request)
        out = b''
        while True:
            d = t.recv(4096)
            if not d:
                break
            out += d
        return out

    def raw(self, data=b'', pause=0):
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
        s.settimeout(10)
        s.connect(self.path)
        if data:
            try:
                s.sendall(data)
            except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                pass  # the relay refused (and closed) before all our bytes were written: that IS the outcome under test, callers check it with closed_within
        return s

    def closed_within(self, s, secs):
        s.settimeout(secs)
        try:
            return s.recv(1) == b''
        except socket.timeout:
            return False
        except OSError:
            return True

    def close(self):
        self.relay.stop()
        if self.upstream:
            self.upstream.close()


class Base(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.h = []

    def harness(self, **kw):
        h = Harness(**kw)
        self.h.append(h)
        return h

    def tearDown(self):
        for h in self.h:
            h.close()

    @staticmethod
    def wait_for(predicate, secs=15):
        """Event-driven waiting: poll a condition instead of sleeping for a guessed time (a loaded host is slow, never wrong)."""
        end = time.monotonic() + secs
        while time.monotonic() < end:
            if predicate():
                return True
            time.sleep(0.02)
        return predicate()


class SniParser(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_real_openssl_hello_accepted(self):
        self.assertEqual(R.parse_client_hello_sni(real_hello()), HOST)

    def test_wrong_and_lookalike_names_rejected_by_policy_not_parser(self):
        for n in ('api.neuralwatt.com.evil.test', 'evil.api.neuralwatt.com', 'neuralwatt.com', 'api.neuralwatt.co'):
            self.assertNotEqual(R.parse_client_hello_sni(real_hello(n)), HOST)

    def test_uppercase_name_normalised(self):
        self.assertEqual(R.parse_client_hello_sni(build_hello(name=b'API.NeuralWatt.COM')), HOST)

    def test_missing_sni_and_ip_literal_rejected(self):
        for h in (build_hello(exts=[struct.pack('>HH', 10, 4) + b'\0\2\0\x1d']), real_hello('203.0.113.9')):
            with self.assertRaises(R.Reject):
                R.parse_client_hello_sni(h)

    def test_structural_attacks_rejected(self):
        bad = {
            'trailing dot': build_hello(name=HOST.encode() + b'.'),
            'nul in name': build_hello(name=HOST.encode() + b'\0evil'),
            'two names': build_hello(exts=[sni_ext(HOST.encode(), b'evil.test')]),
            'duplicate sni ext': build_hello(exts=[sni_ext(HOST.encode()), sni_ext(HOST.encode())]),
            'duplicate other ext': build_hello(exts=[sni_ext(HOST.encode()), struct.pack('>HH', 10, 4) + b'\0\2\0\x1d',
                                                     struct.pack('>HH', 10, 4) + b'\0\2\0\x1d']),
            'bytes after the record': build_hello() + b'\0',
            'ech': build_hello(exts=[sni_ext(HOST.encode()), struct.pack('>HH', 0xfe0d, 2) + b'\0\0']),
            'esni': build_hello(exts=[sni_ext(HOST.encode()), struct.pack('>HH', 0xffce, 0)]),
            'fractured over two records': build_hello(split_at=20),
            'trailing bytes in record': build_hello(trailing=b'\0'),
            'not a clienthello': build_hello(hs_type=2),
            'not a handshake record': build_hello(rec_type=0x17),
            'sslv2 style': b'\x80\x2e\x01\x03\x01' + b'\0' * 40,
            'plaintext http': b'CONNECT evil.test:443 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: evil.test\r\n\r\n',
            'oversize record': b'\x16\x03\x01\x40\x01' + b'\1' * 16385,
            'long session id': build_hello(sid=b'\0' * 33),
            'empty name': build_hello(name=b''),
        }
        for label, hello in bad.items():
            with self.subTest(label), self.assertRaises(R.Reject):
                R.parse_client_hello_sni(hello)

    def test_every_truncation_and_random_mutation_only_ever_rejects(self):
        good = real_hello()
        for n in range(len(good)):
            with self.assertRaises(R.Reject):
                R.parse_client_hello_sni(good[:n])
        rnd = secrets.SystemRandom(7)
        for _ in range(6000):
            b = bytearray(good)
            for _ in range(rnd.randint(1, 4)):
                b[rnd.randrange(len(b))] = rnd.randrange(256)
            try:
                R.parse_client_hello_sni(bytes(b))
            except R.Reject:
                pass  # any other exception type fails the test


class AddressPolicy(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_table(self):
        denied = ['127.0.0.1', '10.1.2.3', '172.16.0.1', '192.168.1.1', '169.254.169.254', '100.64.0.1', '100.64.0.1', '0.0.0.0',
                  '224.0.0.1', '255.255.255.255', '192.0.2.1', '198.18.0.1', '::1', '::', 'fe80::1', 'fe80::1%eth0', 'fd7a:115c:a1e0::1',
                  'fc00::1', '::ffff:127.0.0.1', '::ffff:93.184.216.34', '64:ff9b::7f00:1', '2002:7f00:1::', '2001:0::1', 'ff02::1',
                  '2001:db8::1', 'not-an-ip', '', '93.184.216.34/32', '1.2.3']
        for a in denied:
            with self.subTest(a):
                self.assertFalse(R.address_allowed(a))
        for a in ('93.184.216.34', '104.18.6.80', '2606:4700:20::681a:650'):
            with self.subTest(a):
                self.assertTrue(R.address_allowed(a))
        self.assertFalse(R.address_allowed(None))


class RelayBehaviour(Base):
    def test_positive_end_to_end_and_certificate_is_the_providers(self):
        up = MockUpstream()
        h = self.harness(upstream=up)
        out = h.tls()
        self.assertTrue(out.startswith(b'mock-ok '))
        self.assertEqual(h.calls, [(PUBLIC, 443)])  # the only connect: the validated address, port 443
        self.assertEqual(len(up.requests), 1)

    def test_relay_does_not_terminate_tls_client_verification_still_bites(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream())
        with self.assertRaises(ssl.SSLCertVerificationError):
            h.tls(cafile='ca2.pem')  # a client that does not trust the provider CA fails: the relay holds no cert of its own
        h2 = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(certtag='wrongname'))
        with self.assertRaises(ssl.SSLCertVerificationError):
            h2.tls()  # provider presents a certificate for another name: hostname verification still fails end to end

    def test_wrong_sni_closed_before_any_upstream_contact(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream())
        for hello in (real_hello('evil.example.test'), real_hello('api.neuralwatt.com.evil.test'), real_hello('203.0.113.9'),
                      build_hello(exts=[sni_ext(HOST.encode()), struct.pack('>HH', 0xfe0d, 0)]), build_hello(split_at=30),
                      b'CONNECT evil.test:443 HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n', b'\x00' * 64, os.urandom(300)):
            s = h.raw(hello)
            self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 3))
            s.close()
        self.assertEqual(h.calls, [])

    def test_dns_answers_private_or_mixed_refused_without_connect(self):
        for answers in (['127.0.0.1'], ['10.0.0.5'], ['100.64.0.1'], ['169.254.1.1'], ['fd7a:115c:a1e0::5'], ['::1'],
                        [PUBLIC, '10.0.0.5'], ['::ffff:10.0.0.5'], []):
            h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(), resolver=lambda host, port, a=answers: a)
            s = h.raw(real_hello())
            self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 3), answers)
            self.assertEqual(h.calls, [], answers)

    def test_resolver_hang_and_failure_are_bounded(self):
        gate = threading.Event()
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(), resolver=lambda host, port: gate.wait(30), connect_deadline=0.5)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        s = h.raw(real_hello())
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 3))
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 2.5)
        s2 = h.raw(real_hello())  # lookup still stuck: refused immediately, no thread pile-up
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s2, 1))
        gate.set()

        def boom(host, port):
            raise OSError('dns down')
        h2 = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(), resolver=boom)
        self.assertTrue(h2.closed_within(h2.raw(real_hello()), 3))

    def test_connect_failure_and_peername_mismatch_fail_closed(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=None)
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(h.raw(real_hello()), 3))
        up = MockUpstream()
        h2 = self.harness(upstream=up, connector=lambda a, p, t: socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1', up.port), t))
        self.assertTrue(h2.closed_within(h2.raw(real_hello()), 3))  # connector returned a socket to an address we did not validate

    def test_slowloris_and_silent_connect_hit_hello_deadline(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(), hello_deadline=0.8)
        s = h.raw()
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 3))
        hello = real_hello()
        s = h.raw()
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        try:
            for b in hello[:40]:
                s.sendall(bytes([b]))
                time.sleep(0.1)
        except OSError:
            pass
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 3))
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 3)
        self.assertEqual(h.calls, [])

    def test_concurrency_bound(self):
        up = MockUpstream(mode='silent')
        h = self.harness(upstream=up, max_concurrent=2)
        held = []
        for n in (1, 2):
            held.append(h.raw(real_hello()))
            self.assertTrue(self.wait_for(lambda n=n: len(h.calls) == n))  # one at a time: each slot really is in use before the next connection starts
        extra = h.raw(real_hello())
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(extra, 10))
        self.assertEqual(len(h.calls), 2)
        for s in held:
            s.close()

    def test_two_simultaneous_connections_with_a_slow_lookup_are_both_served(self):
        # rev6/rev6c refused the second connection with 'dns' while the first one's lookup was still in flight (found by stress; RED on the old relay).
        slow = lambda host, port: (time.sleep(0.4), [PUBLIC])[1]
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(mode='silent'), resolver=slow, max_concurrent=4)
        held = [h.raw(real_hello()), h.raw(real_hello())]
        self.assertTrue(self.wait_for(lambda: len(h.calls) == 2))  # both reached the upstream connect
        self.assertEqual(h.relay.counters['dns'], 0)
        for s in held:
            s.close()

    def test_a_stuck_resolver_under_the_default_four_slots_is_bounded_and_accumulates_nothing(self):
        # What the old non-blocking acquire protected and the bounded wait must NOT trade away (Hermes, rev6d review): with a resolver that never returns, under the DEFAULT
        # four slots, waiters are bounded by connect_deadline, the resolver is invoked once, the connection whose own lookup is stuck returns 'dns' inside the deadline,
        # nothing reaches the upstream, and no thread accumulates, across two waves of connections.
        self.assertEqual(R.MAX_CONCURRENT, 4)
        stuck, started = threading.Event(), []

        def resolver(host, port):
            started.append(1)
            stuck.wait(60)
            return [PUBLIC]
        deadline = 0.6
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(mode='silent'), resolver=resolver, connect_deadline=deadline)  # max_concurrent left at the default
        before = threading.active_count()
        for wave in (1, 2):
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            socks = [h.raw(real_hello()) for _ in range(6)]  # 4 can take a slot (one stuck lookup + three waiters); the other two are refused 'busy'
            ended = []
            for s in socks:
                self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 15))
                ended.append(time.monotonic() - t0)
            self.assertLess(max(ended), deadline + 5.0)  # bounded by the deadline plus scheduling slack, never by the resolver's 60 s
            self.assertEqual(len(started), 1)  # no second lookup thread while the first one lives
            self.assertTrue(self.wait_for(lambda: h.relay.counters['dns'] + h.relay.counters['busy'] == 6 * wave))
            self.assertGreaterEqual(h.relay.counters['dns'], 4 * wave)  # cumulative: at most two connections of each wave of six are refused 'busy', the rest are 'dns'
            self.assertEqual(h.calls, [])  # nothing ever reached the upstream
            self.assertTrue(self.wait_for(lambda: threading.active_count() <= before + 1))  # only the one stuck lookup thread outlives the wave
        stuck.set()
        self.assertTrue(self.wait_for(lambda: threading.active_count() <= before))  # and it goes away once the resolver returns

    def test_idle_total_and_byte_deadlines(self):
        up = MockUpstream(mode='silent')
        h = self.harness(upstream=up, idle_deadline=0.6)
        s = h.raw(real_hello())
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 4))
        up2 = MockUpstream(mode='firehose')
        h2 = self.harness(upstream=up2, total_deadline=1.0)
        s = h2.raw(real_hello())
        s.settimeout(5)
        got, t0 = 0, time.monotonic()
        try:
            ctx = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=TMP + '/ca.pem')
            t = ctx.wrap_socket(s, server_hostname=HOST)
            t.sendall(b'GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n')
            while t.recv(65536):
                got += 1
        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
            pass
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 4)
        up3 = MockUpstream(mode='firehose')
        h3 = self.harness(upstream=up3, max_bytes=100000)
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=TMP + '/ca.pem')
        t = ctx.wrap_socket(h3.raw(), server_hostname=HOST)
        t.sendall(b'GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n')
        total = 0
        try:
            while True:
                d = t.recv(65536)
                if not d:
                    break
                total += len(d)
        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
            pass
        self.assertLess(total, 400000)
        time.sleep(0.3)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(h3.relay.counters['bytes'], 1)

    # ---- upstream truncation: what the worker can and cannot see (EGRESS.md "Close and truncation semantics"). All deterministic: ----
    # ---- the upstream always reads the request first, so a close can only ever be a FIN (or an explicit RST), never a race. ----
    def view(self, h, strict=False):
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
        s.settimeout(10)
        s.connect(h.path)
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=TMP + '/ca.pem')
        t = ctx.wrap_socket(s, server_hostname=HOST, suppress_ragged_eofs=not strict)
        t.sendall(b'GET /canary HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\n\r\n')
        out = b''
        while True:
            d = t.recv(4096)
            if not d:
                return out
            out += d

    def wait_counter(self, h, code, secs=5):
        end = time.monotonic() + secs
        while time.monotonic() < end and not h.relay.counters[code]:
            time.sleep(0.02)
        return h.relay.counters[code]

    def test_truncation_without_close_notify_is_a_clean_empty_eof_to_a_lenient_client_and_nothing_is_invented(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(mode='close-after-request'))
        self.assertEqual(self.view(h), b'')  # documented limit: no exception, and no byte the upstream did not send
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.wait_counter(h, 'ok'), 1)

    def test_a_strict_client_sees_the_same_truncation_as_an_error_through_the_relay(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(mode='close-after-request'))
        with self.assertRaises(ssl.SSLError):
            self.view(h, strict=True)  # TLS is end to end, so the missing close_notify stays visible to a client that checks it

    def test_upstream_reset_ends_both_sides_is_counted_closed_and_delivers_nothing_extra(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream(mode='reset-after-request'))
        try:
            got = self.view(h)
        except (ssl.SSLError, OSError):
            got = b''
        self.assertEqual(got, b'')
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.wait_counter(h, 'closed'), 1)

    def test_relay_forwards_exactly_the_upstream_bytes_then_a_fin_even_when_the_last_record_is_incomplete(self):
