        payload = b'\x17\x03\x03\x00\x64' + b'abc'  # a record header that promises 100 bytes, only 3 delivered, then FIN
        h = self.harness(upstream=RawUpstream(payload, how='fin'))
        s = h.raw(real_hello())
        got = b''
        while True:
            d = s.recv(4096)
            if not d:
                break
            got += d
        self.assertEqual(got, payload)  # nothing dropped, nothing added, no repair of the half record
        s.close()  # the relay reports 'ok' only when BOTH directions have ended: the client's side is ours to close
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.wait_counter(h, 'ok'), 1)

    def test_relay_never_delivers_bytes_the_upstream_did_not_send_when_it_resets(self):
        payload = b'\x17\x03\x03\x00\x64' + b'abc'
        h = self.harness(upstream=RawUpstream(payload, how='rst'))
        s = h.raw(real_hello())
        got = b''
        try:
            while True:
                d = s.recv(4096)
                if not d:
                    break
                got += d
        except OSError:
            pass
        self.assertTrue(payload.startswith(got), got)  # whatever arrived is a prefix of what the upstream sent
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.wait_counter(h, 'closed'), 1)

    def test_fd_passing_is_not_accepted(self):
        up = MockUpstream()
        h = self.harness(upstream=up)
        base = len(os.listdir('/proc/self/fd'))
        for _ in range(20):
            s = h.raw()
            r, w = os.pipe()
            try:
                s.sendmsg([real_hello()], [(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SCM_RIGHTS, struct.pack('i', r))])
            except OSError:
                pass
            time.sleep(0.05)
            s.close()
            os.close(r)
            os.close(w)
        time.sleep(1.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(len(os.listdir('/proc/self/fd')), base + 3)

    def test_peer_uid_check_rejects_other_uid_even_with_open_permissions(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream())
        h.relay.peer_uid = os.getuid() + 12345
        os.chmod(h.path, 0o666)
        s = h.raw(real_hello())
        self.assertTrue(h.closed_within(s, 3))
        self.assertEqual(h.calls, [])
        self.assertGreaterEqual(h.relay.counters['peer'], 1)

    def test_logs_contain_only_fixed_codes(self):
        up = MockUpstream()
        h = self.harness(upstream=up)
        canary = 'CANARY-' + secrets.token_hex(8)
        h.tls(request=('GET /%s HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\nAuthorization: Bearer %s\r\n\r\n' % (canary, canary)).encode())
        s = h.raw(real_hello('secret-name-%s.example.test' % canary[-6:]))
        h.closed_within(s, 2)
        time.sleep(0.2)
        blob = '\n'.join(h.logs)
        self.assertTrue(h.logs)
        for line in h.logs:
            self.assertRegex(line, r'^event=[a-z]+ total=\d+$')
        for forbidden in (canary, 'secret-name', 'neuralwatt', PUBLIC, '16030', 'Bearer'):
            self.assertNotIn(forbidden, blob)

    def test_stale_socket_and_unsafe_directory_refused(self):
        h = self.harness(upstream=MockUpstream())
        again = R.Relay(h.path, peer_uid=os.getuid())
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            again.bind()  # a live relay owns the path
        d = tempfile.mkdtemp(dir=TMP)
        os.chmod(d, 0o755)
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            R.Relay(d + '/relay.sock', peer_uid=os.getuid()).bind()
        os.chmod(d, 0o700)
        open(d + '/relay.sock', 'w').close()
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            R.Relay(d + '/relay.sock', peer_uid=os.getuid()).bind()  # a regular file is never unlinked
        link_target = tempfile.mkdtemp(dir=TMP)
        os.chmod(link_target, 0o700)
        os.symlink(link_target, TMP + '/linkdir')
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            R.Relay(TMP + '/linkdir/relay.sock', peer_uid=os.getuid()).bind()


class SourceProperties(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        with open(os.path.join(MODDIR, 'fca_egress_relay.py')) as f:
            self.src = f.read()
        head, _, rest = self.src.partition('"""')  # drop the module docstring: it names what is absent
        body = rest.partition('"""')[2]
        self.code = '\n'.join(l for l in body.splitlines() if not l.strip().startswith('#'))

    def test_every_close_reason_is_documented_in_egress_md(self):
        doc = open(os.path.join(HERE, 'EGRESS.md')).read()
        for code in sorted(R.REASONS):
            self.assertIn('| `%s` |' % code, doc, code)
        row = [l for l in doc.splitlines() if l.startswith('| `dns` |')][0]
        self.assertIn('bounded wait', row)  # says when 'dns' fires after the change, not just that it exists

    def test_no_tls_termination_no_selector_no_config(self):
        for pat in (r'\bimport ssl\b', r'\bwrap_socket\b', r'\bSSLContext\b', r'os\.environ', r'os\.getenv', r'argparse', r'sys\.argv\[1',
                    r'\bSOCKS\b', r'\bsocks\b', r'recvmsg', r'SCM_RIGHTS', r'AF_INET.*bind', r'\.bind\(\(', r'verify_mode', r'check_hostname',
                    r'CERT_NONE', r'shell=True', r'open\('):
            self.assertIsNone(re.search(pat, self.code), pat)

    def test_only_unix_listener_and_fixed_constants(self):
        self.assertIn("UPSTREAM_HOST = 'api.neuralwatt.com'", self.src)
        self.assertIn('UPSTREAM_PORT = 443', self.src)
        self.assertEqual(self.src.count('socket.AF_UNIX'), 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.src.count('.listen('), 1)

    def test_main_refuses_arguments(self):
        self.assertEqual(R.main(['relay', '--upstream', 'evil.test:443']), 2)
        self.assertEqual(R.main(['relay', 'x']), 2)

    def test_harden_refuses_forbidden_identities(self):
        if os.geteuid() == 1000:
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
                R._harden()  # the test runner is gille (1000): must be refused as a relay identity


def tearDownModule():
    shutil.rmtree(TMP, ignore_errors=True)


make_pki()
if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main(verbosity=1, warnings='ignore')
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/test_egress_sandbox.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3"
"""Real-namespace, real-UID integration test for the FCA egress relay (SOURCE candidate). Needs bwrap and passwordless sudo for
setpriv/install/setfacl (all scratch state is under /tmp and removed). Stand-in identities are bare numeric uids with NO passwd entry:
relay=61999, other=61998; the worker is the real uid 2012 with NO supplementary groups (the post-cortex-removal state).
Synthetic local TLS only; the relay's resolver/connector are injected here only; nothing leaves this host. Usage:
test_egress_sandbox.py [DIR_WITH_MODULES]"""
import faulthandler
import importlib.util
import json
import os
import pwd
import select
import shutil
import socket
import ssl
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
import warnings

warnings.simplefilter('ignore', ResourceWarning)
# A stall must name itself: after FCA_TEST_WATCHDOG seconds (default 300) every thread's traceback is printed and the process exits non-zero.
faulthandler.dump_traceback_later(int(os.environ.get('FCA_TEST_WATCHDOG', '300')), exit=True)
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
MODDIR = sys.argv.pop(1) if len(sys.argv) > 1 and os.path.isdir(sys.argv[1]) else HERE
RELAY_UID, OTHER_UID, WORKER = 61999, 61998, 2012
NODE = '/home/agent-deepseek/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime/node/bin/node'
WORK = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-egress-sbx-')
os.chmod(WORK, 0o755)
LANE, SBX = WORK + '/lane', WORK + '/sbx'
SOCKDIR = WORK + '/sock'
procs, relay_logs = [], []

def _sudo_ok(*argv):
    try:
        return subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n'] + list(argv), capture_output=True, timeout=30).returncode == 0
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return False


node_present = _sudo_ok('test', '-x', NODE)


def honey_preconditions():
    """Why this suite cannot run here, or [] when it can. It needs the real Honey identity: bwrap, passwordless sudo, and uid 2012 = agent-deepseek."""
    problems = []
    if shutil.which('bwrap') is None:
        problems.append('bwrap is not installed')
    if not _sudo_ok('true'):
        problems.append('passwordless sudo is not available')
    try:
        if pwd.getpwuid(WORKER).pw_name != 'agent-deepseek':
            problems.append('uid %d is not agent-deepseek' % WORKER)
    except KeyError:
        problems.append('uid %d does not exist on this host' % WORKER)
    return problems


def sudo_as(uid, *argv, groups=None):
    g = ['--clear-groups'] if groups is None else ['--groups', groups]
    return ['sudo', '-n', 'setpriv', '--reuid=%d' % uid, '--regid=%d' % uid] + g + list(argv)


def host_tailnet_address():
    """The host's own tailnet IPv4 address, read at run time with `tailscale ip -4`. Never written into the repo or printed."""
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(['tailscale', 'ip', '-4'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None
    lines = out.stdout.split()
    return lines[0] if out.returncode == 0 and lines else None


def run(argv, **kw):
    kw.setdefault('capture_output', True)
    kw.setdefault('text', True)
    kw.setdefault('timeout', 60)
    return subprocess.run(argv, **kw)


def start_relay(sockdir):
    run(['sudo', '-n', 'install', '-d', '-o', str(RELAY_UID), '-g', str(RELAY_UID), '-m', '0700', sockdir], check=True)
    run(['sudo', '-n', 'setfacl', '-m', 'u:%d:--x' % WORKER, sockdir], check=True)
    p = subprocess.Popen(sudo_as(RELAY_UID, '/usr/bin/python3', '-B', '-I', LANE + '/relay_runner.py', LANE, sockdir, PORT),
                         stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
    procs.append(p)
    lines = []

    def pump():
        for line in p.stderr:
            lines.append(line.strip())
    threading.Thread(target=pump, daemon=True).start()
    if not select.select([p.stdout], [], [], 60)[0]:
        raise AssertionError('relay did not report ready within 60 s; stderr so far: %r' % (lines,))
    ready = p.stdout.readline().strip()
    assert ready == 'relay-ready', (ready, lines)
    return p, lines


def bwrap(sockdir, command, env=None, with_node=False):
    args = ['/usr/bin/bwrap', '--unshare-user', '--unshare-pid', '--unshare-net', '--unshare-ipc', '--unshare-uts', '--unshare-cgroup',
            '--uid', '2012', '--gid', '2012', '--cap-add', 'CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE',
            '--ro-bind', '/usr', '/usr', '--ro-bind', '/bin', '/bin', '--ro-bind', '/lib', '/lib', '--ro-bind', '/lib64', '/lib64',
            '--ro-bind', SBX + '/hosts', '/etc/hosts', '--ro-bind', sockdir, '/run/fca-egress', '--ro-bind', SBX, '/opt/test',
            '--tmpfs', '/tmp', '--proc', '/proc', '--clearenv', '--setenv', 'PATH', '/usr/bin:/bin',
            '--setenv', 'HOME', '/tmp/h']
    # NOT "--dev /dev": with it the kernel denies the in-namespace bind of 443 even though CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE is held (reproduced
    # 2026-11-06, bwrap 0.11.0, kernel 7.2.0-rc2). The four harmless device nodes are bound individually instead.
    for node in ('null', 'zero', 'urandom', 'random'):
        args += ['--dev-bind', '/dev/' + node, '/dev/' + node]
    if with_node:
        args += ['--dir', '/opt/node', '--dir', '/opt/node/bin', '--ro-bind', NODE, '/opt/node/bin/node']
    for k, v in (env or {}).items():
        args += ['--setenv', k, v]
    return sudo_as(WORKER, *(args + command))


def through_shim(sockdir, mode, **kw):
    return run(bwrap(sockdir, ['/usr/bin/python3', '-I', '/opt/test/fca_egress_shim.py', '--', '/usr/bin/python3', '-I',
                               '/opt/test/sandbox_client.py', mode], **kw))


def real_hello(name):
    ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
    ctx.check_hostname = False
    ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE
    inb, outb = ssl.MemoryBIO(), ssl.MemoryBIO()
    try:
        ctx.wrap_bio(inb, outb, server_hostname=name).do_handshake()
    except ssl.SSLWantReadError:
        pass
    return outb.read()


def connect_unix(path, send=None):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
    s.settimeout(3)
    try:
        s.connect(path)
        if send:
            s.sendall(send)
            return 'connected-then-' + ('closed' if s.recv(1) == b'' else 'data')
        return 'connected'
    except ConnectionResetError:
        return 'connected-then-closed'  # the relay closed with our bytes unread
    except OSError as e:
        return type(e).__name__
    finally:
        s.close()


def setUpModule():
    global PORT, CANARY, ABSTRACT, relay_proc, relay_log
    problems = honey_preconditions()
    if problems:
        raise unittest.SkipTest('Honey-only suite, skipped here: ' + '; '.join(problems))
    os.makedirs(LANE)
    os.makedirs(SBX)
    for f in ('fca_egress_relay.py', 'relay_runner.py', 'sandbox_mock_upstream.py'):
        shutil.copy(os.path.join(MODDIR, f) if f.startswith('fca_') else os.path.join(HERE, f), LANE)
    for f in ('fca_egress_shim.py', 'sandbox_client.py', 'hosts'):
        shutil.copy(os.path.join(MODDIR, f) if f.startswith('fca_') else os.path.join(HERE, f), SBX)
    for d in (LANE, SBX):
        for f in os.listdir(d):
            os.chmod(os.path.join(d, f), 0o755 if f.endswith('.py') else 0o644)
    key = WORK + '/keys'
    os.makedirs(key, mode=0o700)
    sh = lambda *a: subprocess.run(a, check=True, capture_output=True)
    sh('openssl', 'req', '-x509', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048', '-nodes', '-keyout', key + '/ca.key', '-out', SBX + '/ca.pem', '-subj', '/CN=fca-test-ca',
       '-days', '2', '-addext', 'basicConstraints=critical,CA:TRUE', '-addext', 'keyUsage=critical,keyCertSign,cRLSign')
    sh('openssl', 'req', '-x509', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048', '-nodes', '-keyout', key + '/ca2.key', '-out', SBX + '/ca2.pem', '-subj', '/CN=unrelated', '-days', '2')
    sh('openssl', 'req', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048', '-nodes', '-keyout', key + '/leaf.key', '-out', key + '/leaf.csr', '-subj', '/CN=api.neuralwatt.com')
    open(key + '/leaf.ext', 'w').write('subjectAltName=DNS:api.neuralwatt.com\n')
    sh('openssl', 'x509', '-req', '-in', key + '/leaf.csr', '-CA', SBX + '/ca.pem', '-CAkey', key + '/ca.key', '-CAcreateserial', '-out', key + '/leaf.pem',
       '-days', '2', '-extfile', key + '/leaf.ext')
    for f in os.listdir(SBX):
        os.chmod(os.path.join(SBX, f), 0o755 if f.endswith('.py') else 0o644)
    up = subprocess.Popen(['/usr/bin/python3', '-I', LANE + '/sandbox_mock_upstream.py', key + '/leaf.pem', key + '/leaf.key', WORK + '/port'])
    procs.append(up)
    for _ in range(50):
        if os.path.exists(WORK + '/port') and open(WORK + '/port').read():
            break
        time.sleep(0.1)
    PORT = open(WORK + '/port').read().strip()
    CANARY = socket.socket()
    CANARY.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0))
    CANARY.listen(4)
    ABSTRACT = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
    ABSTRACT.bind('\0fca-egress-canary-%d' % os.getpid())
    ABSTRACT.listen(4)
    relay_proc, relay_log = start_relay(SOCKDIR)


def tearDownModule():
    for p in procs:
        run(['sudo', '-n', 'kill', '-9', str(p.pid)], check=False)
        try:
            p.kill()
        except OSError:
            pass
    run(['sudo', '-n', 'pkill', '-9', '-u', str(RELAY_UID)], check=False)
    run(['sudo', '-n', 'rm', '-rf', WORK], check=False)


class Sandbox(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_01_the_operator_is_not_admitted_to_the_relay_socket(self):
        self.assertEqual(connect_unix(SOCKDIR + '/relay.sock'), 'PermissionError')  # gille (uid 1000, in cortex): 0700 dir, ACL only for 2012

    def test_02_direct_network_attempts_stay_blocked_in_the_namespace(self):
        host_lo = socket.create_connection(CANARY.getsockname(), 2)  # control: the canary really listens on the host
        host_lo.close()
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)
        s.connect('\0fca-egress-canary-%d' % os.getpid())
        s.close()
        env = {'CANARY_PORT': str(CANARY.getsockname()[1]), 'ABSTRACT_NAME': 'fca-egress-canary-%d' % os.getpid()}
        tailnet = host_tailnet_address()  # read at run time; the address is never committed or printed
        tail_canary = None
        if tailnet:
            tail_canary = socket.socket()
            tail_canary.bind((tailnet, 0))
            tail_canary.listen(4)
            socket.create_connection(tail_canary.getsockname(), 2).close()  # control: the host really listens on its tailnet address
            env['TAILNET_IP'] = tailnet
            env['TAILNET_CANARY_PORT'] = str(tail_canary.getsockname()[1])
        try:
            out = run(bwrap(SOCKDIR, ['/usr/bin/python3', '-I', '/opt/test/sandbox_client.py', 'isolation'], env=env))
        finally:
            if tail_canary:
                tail_canary.close()
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        res = json.loads(out.stdout)
        for k in ('host_loopback_canary', 'lan_gateway_80', 'public_v4_443', 'public_v6_443', 'dns_udp_resolver',
                  'host_abstract_socket', 'other_unix_path'):
            self.assertNotEqual(res[k], 'connected', (k, res))
            print('  blocked %-22s %s' % (k, res[k]))
        for k in ('tailnet_canary', 'tailnet_22', 'tailnet_3003'):
            with self.subTest(k):
                if not tailnet:
                    self.assertEqual(res[k], 'unset')
                    self.skipTest('SKIPPED %s: this host has no tailnet address (nothing to probe)' % k)
                self.assertNotIn(res[k], ('connected', 'unset'), (k, res))
                print('  blocked %-22s %s' % (k, res[k]))
        self.assertEqual(res['resolve_example_com'], 'gaierror')
        self.assertEqual(res['resolve_provider'], ['127.0.0.1'])  # hosts file only: the name never leaves the namespace
        self.assertEqual(res['resolve_provider_alt'], 'gaierror')
        self.assertEqual(res['visible_mounts_run'], ['fca-egress'])
        self.assertFalse(res['home_visible'])

    def test_the_probe_can_see_a_leak_when_the_namespace_is_absent(self):
        # Negative control for test_02: a probe that can only ever say "blocked" is indistinguishable from a probe with a bug.
        # The SAME client and variables, NO namespace, a real host listener: it must report 'connected'.
        addr = host_tailnet_address() or '127.0.0.1'  # loopback binds anywhere, so the control rarely has to skip
        listener = socket.socket()
        try:
            listener.bind((addr, 0))
            listener.listen(4)
        except OSError as e:
            listener.close()
            self.skipTest('SKIPPED: no bindable address for the control listener (%s)' % type(e).__name__)
        try:
            env = {'PATH': '/usr/bin:/bin', 'TAILNET_IP': addr, 'TAILNET_CANARY_PORT': str(listener.getsockname()[1]),
                   'CANARY_PORT': '1', 'ABSTRACT_NAME': 'fca-egress-no-such-name-%d' % os.getpid()}
            out = run(['/usr/bin/python3', '-I', os.path.join(HERE, 'sandbox_client.py'), 'isolation'], env=env)
        finally:
            listener.close()
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        res = json.loads(out.stdout)
        self.assertEqual(res['tailnet_canary'], 'connected', res)  # the very probe test_02 relies on, with nothing in its way
        print('  control: tailnet_canary connected with no namespace (the probe can say "connected")')

    def test_03_shim_binds_443_then_holds_no_capabilities(self):
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'caps')
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        res = json.loads(out.stdout)
        self.assertEqual((res['uid'], res['gid']), (2012, 2012))
        self.assertNotIn(2000, res['groups'])
        self.assertNotIn(1000, res['groups'])
        for k in ('CapEff', 'CapPrm', 'CapInh', 'CapAmb'):
            self.assertEqual(int(res[k], 16), 0, res)
        self.assertEqual(res['NoNewPrivs'], '1')
        self.assertEqual(res['listening_443'], 'connected')

    def test_04_positive_transfer_through_the_one_allowed_socket(self):
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'positive')
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(out.stdout)['response'], 'mock-ok ')
        self.assertIn('event=ok total=1', '\n'.join(relay_log))

    def test_05_negative_cases_through_the_real_path(self):
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'negative')
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        res = json.loads(out.stdout)
        for k, v in res.items():
            self.assertEqual(v, 'closed', (k, res))
        time.sleep(0.3)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(sum('event=sni' in l or 'event=hello' in l for l in relay_log), 5)

    @unittest.skipUnless(node_present, 'DSH runtime Node not staged')
    def test_06_real_dsh_node_client_verifies_the_providers_certificate_end_to_end(self):
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'node-positive', with_node=True)
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        lines = dict(l.split(':', 1) for l in out.stdout.splitlines())
        self.assertEqual(lines['trusted-ca'], 'mock-ok ')
        self.assertTrue(lines['default-roots'].startswith('ERR:'), lines)  # real Node with real roots rejects the synthetic CA
        self.assertTrue(lines['unrelated-ca'].startswith('ERR:'), lines)
        print('  node:', lines)

    @unittest.skipUnless(node_present, 'DSH runtime Node not staged')
    def test_06b_real_node_sees_truncation_through_http_framing_not_through_tls(self):
        # What the worker can and cannot see when the upstream dies mid-response (EGRESS.md "Close and truncation semantics"). Measured with the
        # real DSH Node through shim, socket and relay. The relay is a byte pump and cannot tell an orderly close from a truncation on a record boundary.
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'node-truncation', with_node=True)
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        res = dict(l.split(':', 1) for l in out.stdout.splitlines())
        for k in ('content-length-fin', 'content-length-notify', 'chunked-fin'):
            self.assertTrue(res[k].startswith('INCOMPLETE:'), (k, res))  # a short body or a missing chunk terminator is detected by framing
        for k in ('close-delimited-fin', 'close-delimited-notify'):
            self.assertTrue(res[k].startswith('COMPLETE:'), (k, res))  # a close-delimited body cannot be told from a complete one: do not use it
        print('  node truncation:', res)

    def test_07_socket_acl_admits_only_the_worker_uid(self):
        path = SOCKDIR + '/relay.sock'
        probe = ['/usr/bin/python3', '-I', '-c', 'import socket,sys\ns=socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)\ntry:\n s.connect(sys.argv[1]);print("connected")\n'
                 'except OSError as e: print(type(e).__name__)', path]
        self.assertEqual(run(probe).stdout.strip(), 'PermissionError')  # gille (uid 1000, in cortex)
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(OTHER_UID, *probe)).stdout.strip(), 'PermissionError')
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(WORKER, *probe)).stdout.strip(), 'connected')
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(WORKER, *probe, groups='2000')).stdout.strip(), 'connected')  # uid, not cortex, is what admits

    def test_08_peer_credential_check_holds_even_if_permissions_are_loosened(self):
        d = WORK + '/sock2'
        p, lines = start_relay(d)
        run(['sudo', '-n', 'setpriv', '--reuid=%d' % RELAY_UID, '--regid=%d' % RELAY_UID, '--clear-groups', '/usr/bin/chmod', '0777', d,
             d + '/relay.sock'], check=True)
        self.assertEqual(connect_unix(d + '/relay.sock', real_hello('api.neuralwatt.com')), 'connected-then-closed')
        time.sleep(0.3)
        self.assertIn('event=peer total=1', '\n'.join(lines))
        self.assertNotIn('event=ok', '\n'.join(lines))
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(WORKER, '/usr/bin/python3', '-I', '-c', 'import socket,sys\ns=socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)\ns.connect(sys.argv[1])\n'
                                    's.sendall(bytes.fromhex(sys.argv[2]))\nprint("data" if s.recv(1) else "closed")', d + '/relay.sock',
                                    real_hello('api.neuralwatt.com').hex())).stdout.strip(), 'data')  # the real worker uid is still admitted

    def test_09_relay_refuses_forbidden_identities(self):
        code = ('import importlib.util,sys\nspec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("r",sys.argv[1]+"/fca_egress_relay.py")\n'
                'm=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)\ntry:\n m._harden();print("accepted")\n'
                'except SystemExit as e: print("refused")')
        cmd = ['/usr/bin/python3', '-B', '-I', '-c', code, LANE]
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(RELAY_UID, *cmd)).stdout.strip(), 'accepted')
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(RELAY_UID, *cmd, groups='2000')).stdout.strip(), 'refused')   # cortex membership carried in
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(RELAY_UID, *cmd, groups='61999,2012')).stdout.strip(), 'refused')  # any supplementary group
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(WORKER, *cmd)).stdout.strip(), 'refused')                    # not the worker itself
        self.assertEqual(run(sudo_as(1000, *cmd)).stdout.strip(), 'refused')                      # not gille
        self.assertEqual(run(['sudo', '-n'] + cmd).stdout.strip(), 'refused')                     # not root

    def test_10_relay_death_and_socket_absence_fail_closed(self):
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        run(['sudo', '-n', 'pkill', '-9', '-f', 'relay_runner.py %s %s ' % (LANE, SOCKDIR)], check=False)
        time.sleep(0.5)
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'positive')
        self.assertNotEqual(out.returncode, 0)  # TLS handshake fails: the shim closed the connection, nothing fell back
        self.assertNotIn('mock-ok', out.stdout)
        run(['sudo', '-n', 'rm', '-f', SOCKDIR + '/relay.sock'], check=True)
        out = through_shim(SOCKDIR, 'positive')
        self.assertNotEqual(out.returncode, 0)
        self.assertNotIn('mock-ok', out.stdout)
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 40)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main(verbosity=2, warnings='ignore')
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/relay_runner.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3 -I"
"""Runs the real relay under a distinct unprivileged uid with an injected resolver/connector (test fixture only; the production
entry point is fca_egress_relay.main). Usage: relay_runner.py MODULE_DIR SOCKET_DIR MOCK_PORT"""
import importlib.util, socket, sys
moddir, sockdir, port = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('fca_egress_relay', moddir + '/fca_egress_relay.py')
R = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(R)
R._harden()
class Up:
    def __init__(self, s, a): self._s, self._a = s, a
    def getpeername(self): return (self._a, 443)
    def fileno(self): return self._s.fileno()
    def __getattr__(self, n): return getattr(self._s, n)
relay = R.Relay(sockdir + '/relay.sock', peer_uid=2012, resolver=lambda h, p: ['93.184.216.34'],
                connector=lambda a, p, t: Up(socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1', port), t), a))
relay.bind()
print('relay-ready', flush=True)
relay.serve_forever()
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/sandbox_client.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3 -I"
"""Runs INSIDE the bwrap namespace as uid 2012 (test fixture). Usage: sandbox_client.py isolation|caps|positive|negative|node-positive"""
import json, os, socket, ssl, subprocess, sys

def attempt(fn):
    try:
        fn(); return 'connected'
    except OSError as e:
        return type(e).__name__ + ':' + str(e.errno)
    except Exception as e:
        return type(e).__name__

def tcp(host, port, family=socket.AF_INET):
    s = socket.socket(family); s.settimeout(2); s.connect((host, port)); s.close()

def udp(host, port):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM); s.settimeout(2); s.connect((host, port)); s.send(b'x'); s.close()

def abstract(name):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX); s.settimeout(2); s.connect('\0' + name); s.close()

def path_sock(p):
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX); s.settimeout(2); s.connect(p); s.close()

def resolve(name):
    try:
        return sorted({ai[4][0] for ai in socket.getaddrinfo(name, 443, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM)})
    except OSError as e:
        return 'gaierror'

def tls_fetch(sni, ca='/opt/test/ca.pem', host='127.0.0.1'):
    ctx = ssl.create_default_context(cafile=ca)
    s = socket.create_connection((host, 443), 5)
    t = ctx.wrap_socket(s, server_hostname=sni)
    t.sendall(b'GET /v1/models HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: api.neuralwatt.com\r\n\r\n')
    out = b''
    while True:
        d = t.recv(4096)
        if not d: break
        out += d
    return out.decode().partition('\r\n\r\n')[2]

def probe_tailnet(port):
    ip = os.environ.get('TAILNET_IP')  # the host's own tailnet address, passed by the runner at run time
    return attempt(lambda: tcp(ip, port)) if ip and port else 'unset'

mode = sys.argv[1]
if mode == 'isolation':
    r = {
        'host_loopback_canary': attempt(lambda: tcp('127.0.0.1', int(os.environ['CANARY_PORT']))),
        'tailnet_canary': probe_tailnet(int(os.environ.get('TAILNET_CANARY_PORT', '0'))),
        'tailnet_22': probe_tailnet(22),
        'tailnet_3003': probe_tailnet(3003),
        'lan_gateway_80': attempt(lambda: tcp('192.168.1.1', 80)),
        'public_v4_443': attempt(lambda: tcp('104.18.6.80', 443)),
        'public_v6_443': attempt(lambda: tcp('2606:4700:20::681a:650', 443, socket.AF_INET6)),
        'dns_udp_resolver': attempt(lambda: udp('1.1.1.1', 53)),
        'host_abstract_socket': attempt(lambda: abstract(os.environ['ABSTRACT_NAME'])),
        'other_unix_path': attempt(lambda: path_sock('/run/fca-egress/not-the-relay.sock')),
        'resolve_example_com': resolve('example.com'),
        'resolve_provider': resolve('api.neuralwatt.com'),
        'resolve_provider_alt': resolve('api.neuralwatt.com.evil.test'),
        'visible_mounts_run': sorted(os.listdir('/run')),
        'home_visible': os.path.exists('/home'),
    }
    print(json.dumps(r, sort_keys=True))
elif mode == 'caps':
    st = dict(l.split(':\t', 1) for l in open('/proc/self/status').read().splitlines() if ':\t' in l)
    shim_parent = open('/proc/%d/status' % os.getppid()).read()
    print(json.dumps({'uid': os.getuid(), 'gid': os.getgid(), 'groups': sorted(os.getgroups()), 'CapEff': st['CapEff'], 'CapPrm': st['CapPrm'],
                      'CapInh': st['CapInh'], 'CapAmb': st['CapAmb'], 'NoNewPrivs': st['NoNewPrivs'],
                      'listening_443': attempt(lambda: tcp('127.0.0.1', 443))}, sort_keys=True))
elif mode == 'positive':
    print(json.dumps({'response': tls_fetch('api.neuralwatt.com')[:8]}))
elif mode == 'negative':
    r = {}
    def wrong(sni):
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context(); ctx.check_hostname = False; ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE
        s = socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1', 443), 5)
        try:
            ctx.wrap_socket(s, server_hostname=sni)
            return 'HANDSHAKE-COMPLETED'
        except (OSError, ssl.SSLError) as e:
            return 'closed'
    r['wrong_sni'] = wrong('evil.example.test')
    r['lookalike_sni'] = wrong('api.neuralwatt.com.evil.test')
    r['ip_literal_sni'] = wrong('203.0.113.9')
    def plain(data):
        s = socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1', 443), 5); s.settimeout(3)
        try:
            s.sendall(data); return 'closed' if s.recv(1) == b'' else 'GOT-DATA'
        except OSError:
            return 'closed'
    r['connect_request'] = plain(b'CONNECT evil.test:443 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: evil.test\r\n\r\n')
    r['garbage'] = plain(os.urandom(200))
    print(json.dumps(r, sort_keys=True))
elif mode == 'node-truncation':
    js = r'''
const https = require('https'), fs = require('fs');
const ca = fs.readFileSync('/opt/test/ca.pem');
function go(path, label) { return new Promise(res => {
  let got = 0, err = '';
  const rq = https.request({host:'api.neuralwatt.com', path, method:'GET', timeout:5000, ca}, r => {
    r.on('data', d => got += d.length); r.on('error', e => { err = e.code || e.message; });
    r.on('close', () => res(label + ':' + (r.complete ? 'COMPLETE' : 'INCOMPLETE') + ':bytes=' + got + (err ? ':err=' + err : ''))); });
  rq.on('error', e => res(label + ':REQUEST-ERR:' + (e.code || e.message))); rq.on('timeout', () => rq.destroy()); rq.end(); }); }
(async () => {
  for (const [p, l] of [['/trunc-cl-fin','content-length-fin'], ['/trunc-cl-notify','content-length-notify'], ['/trunc-chunked-fin','chunked-fin'],
                        ['/delimited-fin','close-delimited-fin'], ['/delimited-notify','close-delimited-notify']]) console.log(await go(p, l));
})();
'''
    print(subprocess.run(['/opt/node/bin/node', '-e', js], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60).stdout.strip())
elif mode == 'node-positive':
    js = r'''
const https = require('https'), fs = require('fs');
function go(opts, label) { return new Promise(res => {
  const rq = https.request(Object.assign({host:'api.neuralwatt.com', path:'/v1/models', method:'GET', timeout:5000}, opts), r => {
    let b=''; r.on('data', d => b+=d); r.on('end', () => res(label+':'+b.slice(0,8))); });
  rq.on('error', e => res(label+':ERR:'+(e.code||e.message))); rq.on('timeout', () => { rq.destroy(); }); rq.end(); }); }
(async () => {
  console.log(await go({ca: fs.readFileSync('/opt/test/ca.pem')}, 'trusted-ca'));
  console.log(await go({}, 'default-roots'));
  console.log(await go({ca: fs.readFileSync('/opt/test/ca2.pem')}, 'unrelated-ca'));
})();
'''
    print(subprocess.run(['/opt/node/bin/node', '-e', js], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30).stdout.strip())
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/sandbox_mock_upstream.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3 -I"
"""Synthetic TLS upstream for the sandbox integration test (test fixture only). Usage: sandbox_mock_upstream.py CERT KEY PORTFILE"""
import hashlib, socket, ssl, sys, threading
cert, key, portfile = sys.argv[1:4]
ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_SERVER)
ctx.load_cert_chain(cert, key)
srv = socket.socket(); srv.bind(('127.0.0.1', 0)); srv.listen(16)
with open(portfile, 'w') as f:
    f.write(str(srv.getsockname()[1]))
def one(c):
    try:
        t = ctx.wrap_socket(c, server_side=True)
        buf = b''
        while b'\r\n\r\n' not in buf:
            d = t.recv(4096)
            if not d:
                return
            buf += d
        parts = buf.split(b'\r\n', 1)[0].split(b' ')
        path = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else b'/'
        # Truncation fixtures (test_06b). Every one reads the whole request first, so a close is a FIN and never a RST.
        if path.startswith(b'/trunc-') or path.startswith(b'/delimited-'):
            if path.startswith(b'/trunc-cl'):
                t.sendall(b'HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 100\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\npartial-body')
            elif path.startswith(b'/trunc-chunked'):
                t.sendall(b'HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n5\r\nhello\r\n')
            else:
                t.sendall(b'HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\npartial-body')
            if path.endswith(b'-notify'):
                t.unwrap()  # orderly TLS shutdown: close_notify is sent
            t.close()  # otherwise: TCP FIN with no close_notify
            return
        body = b'mock-ok ' + hashlib.sha256(buf).hexdigest().encode()
        t.sendall(b'HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: text/plain\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: %d\r\n\r\n' % len(body) + body)
        t.close()
    except (OSError, ssl.SSLError):
        pass
while True:
    c, _ = srv.accept()
    threading.Thread(target=one, args=(c,), daemon=True).start()
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/mutation-check.sh :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash"
# Mutation proof: each sabotaged copy of the relay MUST fail the named suite. Disposable space only; the candidate is not modified.
set -uo pipefail
HERE="$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)"
TMP="$(mktemp -d)"; trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
fail=0
mutate() { # name suite sed-expression. A KILL needs a real unittest failure (named below); a crash, stall or skip is an infrastructure problem and fails the run.
  local name="$1" suite="$2" expr="$3" d="$TMP/$1-$2" out rc names
  if [ "$suite" = test_egress_sandbox.py ] && [ "${HONEY_LIVE:-1}" != 1 ]; then echo "skipped (Honey-only, HONEY_LIVE=0): $name [$suite]"; return; fi
  mkdir -p "$d"; cp "$HERE"/fca_egress_*.py "$d"/
  sed -i "$expr" "$d/fca_egress_relay.py"
  if cmp -s "$HERE/fca_egress_relay.py" "$d/fca_egress_relay.py"; then echo "MUTATION NOT APPLIED: $name"; fail=1; return; fi
  out="$(timeout 300 python3 "$HERE/$suite" "$d" 2>&1)"; rc=$?
  if [ "$rc" = 0 ]; then echo "SURVIVED (tests too weak, or the suite skipped): $name [$suite]"; fail=1
  elif [ "$rc" = 124 ]; then echo "TIMEOUT (infrastructure, NOT a kill): $name [$suite]"; fail=1
  elif printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -qE '^FAILED \('; then
    names="$(printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -E '^(FAIL|ERROR):' | sed 's/^[A-Z]*: //; s/ (.*//' | head -3 | tr '\n' ' ')"
    echo "killed: $name [$suite] by: $names"
  else echo "INFRASTRUCTURE ERROR (not a kill): $name [$suite]"; printf '%s\n' "$out" | tail -8 | sed 's/^/    | /'; fail=1; fi
}
mutate sni-check-removed          test_egress_relay.py   "s/if parse_client_hello_sni(record) != UPSTREAM_HOST:/if False:/"
mutate address-policy-open        test_egress_relay.py   "s/^    if not isinstance(text, str) or '%' in text:/    return True\n    if not isinstance(text, str) or '%' in text:/"
mutate one-bad-answer-tolerated   test_egress_relay.py   "s/                raise Reject('dnsaddr')  # one bad answer.*/                continue/"
mutate ech-allowed                test_egress_relay.py   "s/if etype in seen or etype in BANNED_EXTENSIONS:/if etype in seen:/"
# (dropping the 'len(record) != 5 + length' clause is an equivalent mutant: the exact-consumption checks reject the same inputs)
mutate fractured-hello-allowed    test_egress_relay.py   "s/ or len(record) != 5 + length:/:/; s/    if not r.done():/    if False:/"
mutate peername-unchecked         test_egress_relay.py   "s/if ipaddress.ip_address(peer) != ipaddress.ip_address(address):/if False:/"
mutate hello-deadline-unbounded   test_egress_relay.py   "s/deadline = time.monotonic() + self.hello_deadline/deadline = time.monotonic() + 5000/"
mutate concurrency-unbounded      test_egress_relay.py   "s/if not self.slots.acquire(blocking=False):/if False:/"
mutate peer-uid-unchecked         test_egress_relay.py   "s/if self.peer_uid is not None and struct.unpack('3i', cred)\[1\] != self.peer_uid:/if False:/"
mutate sni-duplicate-allowed      test_egress_relay.py   "s/if etype in seen or etype in BANNED_EXTENSIONS:/if etype in BANNED_EXTENSIONS:/"
mutate argv-accepted              test_egress_relay.py   "s/if len(argv) != 1:/if False:/"
mutate upstream-fin-not-forwarded   test_egress_relay.py   "s/                        peer\[s\].shutdown(socket.SHUT_WR)/                        pass/"
mutate resolver-lock-nonblocking    test_egress_relay.py   "s/self.resolving.acquire(timeout=self.connect_deadline)/self.resolving.acquire(blocking=False)/"
mutate resolver-lock-removed        test_egress_relay.py   "s/self.resolving = threading.Lock()/self.resolving = threading.BoundedSemaphore(1000)/"
mutate last-byte-dropped            test_egress_relay.py   "s/                    peer\[s\].sendall(chunk)/                    peer[s].sendall(chunk[:-1])/"
mutate supplementary-groups-ok    test_egress_sandbox.py "s/if set(os.getgroups()) - {os.getegid()}:/if False:/"
mutate cortex-not-forbidden       test_egress_sandbox.py "s/FORBIDDEN_IDS = frozenset({0, 1000, 2000, 2012})/FORBIDDEN_IDS = frozenset({0, 1000, 2012})/; s/if set(os.getgroups()) - {os.getegid()}:/if False:/"
mutate peer-uid-unchecked         test_egress_sandbox.py "s/if self.peer_uid is not None and struct.unpack('3i', cred)\[1\] != self.peer_uid:/if False:/"
exit $fail
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/egress/test-identity-nocortex.sh :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash"
# Proof that the worker needs no cortex membership: run the REAL tangled launcher as uid 2012 with NO supplementary groups
# (exactly the state after `gpasswd -d agent-deepseek cortex`) without changing any user or group. The lane is deployed to a
# worker-readable system path (a copy in /tmp here); from the sealed operator home it is unreachable without cortex, which is why
# the deployed lane must not live under /home/gille. Production launcher pin stays REVIEW_REQUIRED; only the /tmp copy is pinned.
set -euo pipefail
LANE_SRC="${1:-/home/gille/DotCortex/all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh}"
STAGE=/home/agent-deepseek/.local/share/fca-dsh-runtime
TMP="$(mktemp -d /tmp/fca-lane-nocortex.XXXXXX)"; trap 'sudo -n rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
chmod 755 "$TMP"; cp -a "$LANE_SRC" "$TMP/lane"; chmod -R a+rX "$TMP/lane"
pin="$(sudo -n sha256sum "$STAGE/dsh/REVIEWED-CLOSURE.sha256" | cut -d' ' -f1)"
sed -i "s/^REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256=REVIEW_REQUIRED/REVIEWED_CLOSURE_MANIFEST_SHA256=$pin/" "$TMP/lane/bin/honey-fca-dsh"
as_worker() { local groups="$1" g=(--clear-groups); shift; [ -z "$groups" ] || g=(--groups "$groups")
  sudo -n setpriv --reuid=2012 --regid=2012 "${g[@]}" /usr/bin/env -i HOME=/home/agent-deepseek PATH=/usr/bin:/bin "$@"; }
echo "--- identity seen by the worker with no supplementary groups"
as_worker '' /usr/bin/id
[ "$(as_worker '' /usr/bin/id -G)" = 2012 ]
echo "--- cortex-owned files inside the worker home (none means nothing is stranded by removal)"
[ -z "$(sudo -n find /home/agent-deepseek -group cortex -print -quit)" ] && echo none
echo "--- offline probe and dsh-config from the worker-readable deployed copy, NO groups"
as_worker '' "$TMP/lane/bin/honey-fca-dsh" probe
as_worker '' "$TMP/lane/bin/honey-fca-dsh" dsh-config >"$TMP/cfg.out" 2>"$TMP/cfg.err"; echo "dsh-config exit=$? lines=$(wc -l <"$TMP/cfg.out") stderr_bytes=$(wc -c <"$TMP/cfg.err")"
[ ! -s "$TMP/cfg.err" ]
echo "--- same result WITH cortex (current state), so removal changes nothing for the lane"
a="$(as_worker '' "$TMP/lane/bin/honey-fca-dsh" probe)"; b="$(as_worker 2000 "$TMP/lane/bin/honey-fca-dsh" probe)"; [ "$a" = "$b" ] && echo identical
echo "--- the sealed operator home is NOT reachable without cortex (deploy the lane outside /home/gille)"
if as_worker '' /usr/bin/ls /home/gille/DotCortex >/dev/null 2>&1; then echo 'UNEXPECTED: reachable without cortex'; exit 1; else echo 'refused without cortex (EACCES)'; fi
as_worker 2000 /usr/bin/ls /home/gille/DotCortex >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo 'reachable with cortex: this is the access removal takes away'
#+END_SRC

** Relay service unit and installer (rev14 SOURCE candidate; not installed)

The egress relay of the previous section has no way to start. This adds the two missing pieces and nothing else: =openrc/honey-fca-egress=, the OpenRC body (it recreates the tmpfs socket directory on every start and refuses an unsafe identity or a relay directory that is not root-owned and read-only), and =openrc/honey-fca-egress-install=, which places ONLY the relay and the unit, and only when the relay's sha256 equals the hash recorded in the review. The installer starts nothing and enables nothing by default: =enable= is a separate verb that links =/etc/rc2.d/S04honey-fca-egress=, because this host boots sysvinit and =rc-update= is not evidence of boot durability. Nothing below is provisioned, installed, started or enabled. Tests: =openrc/test_honey_fca_egress.py= (38 tests: 34 run anywhere, and four RealOpenRC tests that REFUSE (fail, naming the hazard) unless HONEY_DISPOSABLE_OPENRC_HOST states the host is disposable, and are skipped by name where there is no openrc-run; the root-owned checks need passwordless sudo and are SKIPPED by name without it) and =openrc/mutation-check-boot.sh= (25 mutants: 22 killed by the tests that run anywhere; 3 can only be proven on a disposable OpenRC host and are reported UNPROVEN, exit 3, never as killed).

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/openrc/honey-fca-egress :comments no :shebang "#!/sbin/openrc-run"
# Honey FCA DSH egress relay (generated by agents-bots-honey.org; source of truth: DotCortex).
# A fixed-upstream TLS-passthrough relay: api.neuralwatt.com:443 and nothing else (egress/EGRESS.md).
# It runs as its own identity, fca-egress, with no groups. The worker (agent-deepseek, uid 2012) reaches it
# only through /run/fca-dsh-egress/relay.sock. /run is tmpfs: this unit recreates the directory on every start.

name="honey-fca-egress"
description="FCA DSH fixed-upstream egress relay (api.neuralwatt.com:443 only)"

: "${relay_root:=/opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress}"
relay_user=fca-egress
worker_user=agent-deepseek
worker_uid=2012
run_dir=/run/fca-dsh-egress
log_file=/var/log/honey-fca-egress.log

command="/usr/bin/python3"
# The relay takes no arguments and reads no configuration (it refuses any), so none are added here.
command_args="-I ${relay_root}/fca_egress_relay.py"
command_user="${relay_user}:${relay_user}"
command_background=true
pidfile="/run/honey-fca-egress.pid"
output_log="${log_file}"
error_log="${log_file}"

depend() {
    use net dns
    after net
}

start_pre() {
    [ "$(id -u "${relay_user}" 2>/dev/null)" ] || { eerror "no ${relay_user} identity; refusing"; return 1; }
    [ "$(id -u "${relay_user}")" -ne 0 ] || { eerror "${relay_user} is uid 0; refusing"; return 1; }
    # The relay refuses ANY supplementary group; refuse before it has to.
    [ "$(id -G "${relay_user}")" = "$(id -g "${relay_user}")" ] || { eerror "${relay_user} has supplementary groups; refusing"; return 1; }
    [ "$(id -u "${worker_user}" 2>/dev/null)" = "${worker_uid}" ] || { eerror "${worker_user} is not uid ${worker_uid}; the relay admits only that uid"; return 1; }
    [ -f "${relay_root}/fca_egress_relay.py" ] || { eerror "missing ${relay_root}/fca_egress_relay.py"; return 1; }
    # What the relay runs must be root's and unwritable by anyone else, or the relay is not the reviewed one.
    if [ -n "$(find "${relay_root}" \( ! -user root -o -perm /022 \) -print -quit 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
        eerror "${relay_root} is not root-owned and read-only; refusing"
        return 1
    fi
    checkpath -d -m 0700 -o "${relay_user}:${relay_user}" "${run_dir}" || return 1
    setfacl -m "u:${worker_uid}:--x" "${run_dir}" || return 1   # traverse only; the relay grants rw on the socket itself
    checkpath -f -m 0640 -o "${relay_user}:${relay_user}" "${log_file}"
}

start_post() {
    # Not half-alive: the relay must have bound its socket, or this start fails loudly (the relay needs no tailnet; it
    # listens on a Unix socket only and refuses tailnet addresses as destinations; network trouble shows up per connection).
    local i=0
    while [ "${i}" -lt 20 ]; do
        [ -S "${run_dir}/relay.sock" ] && return 0
        sleep 0.5
        i=$((i + 1))
    done
    eerror "the relay did not create ${run_dir}/relay.sock within 10 s; stopping it and failing the start"
    stop >/dev/null 2>&1
    return 1
}

# OpenRC's default stop adds --exec ${command}, and command is /usr/bin/python3. With the pidfile gone, start-stop-daemon then matches on the
# executable alone and SIGTERMs EVERY OpenRC-started python3 process on the host (reproduced 2026-11-12: it took out identity-proxy and
# other services). So this unit stops by pidfile ALONE, never passes --exec, and does nothing when there is no live pid of its own.
stop() {
    local pid
    pid="$(cat "${pidfile}" 2>/dev/null)"
    [ -n "${pid}" ] || return 0
    if [ ! -d "/proc/${pid}" ]; then
        rm -f "${pidfile}"
        return 0
    fi
    ebegin "Stopping ${name:-${RC_SVCNAME}}"
    start-stop-daemon --stop --quiet --retry TERM/5/KILL/3 --user "${relay_user}" --pidfile "${pidfile}"
    eend $? "Failed to stop ${name:-${RC_SVCNAME}}"
}

stop_post() {
    rm -f "${run_dir}/relay.sock"
}
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/openrc/honey-fca-egress-install :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash"
# honey-fca-egress-install (generated by agents-bots-honey.org): put the REVIEWED relay and its OpenRC unit in place.
# A human-released provisioning step. It starts nothing; "enable" is a separate, explicit verb.
set -euo pipefail
umask 022

PREFIX="${HONEY_FCA_EGRESS_PREFIX:-}"   # rehearsals only: a scratch root for /opt, /etc and /var
RELAY_DIR="$PREFIX/opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress"
INIT="$PREFIX/etc/init.d/honey-fca-egress"
BOOT_LINK="$PREFIX/etc/rc2.d/S04honey-fca-egress"
RELAY_NAME=fca_egress_relay.py

usage() {
    cat <<'USAGE'
Usage: honey-fca-egress-install [--dry-run] --expect-relay-sha256 HEX install
       honey-fca-egress-install [--dry-run] enable
       honey-fca-egress-install [--dry-run] disable
       honey-fca-egress-install status
       honey-fca-egress-install -h|--help

install  copy fca_egress_relay.py (ONLY the relay) to /opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/ (root:root, 0644) and the OpenRC body to
         /etc/init.d/honey-fca-egress (root:root, 0755), after checking the relay in THIS tree has exactly the sha256
         you pass (the hash recorded in the review). A changed file is backed up as <file>.bak-<timestamp>.
enable   link the unit into runlevel 2 (/etc/rc2.d/S04honey-fca-egress). This host boots sysvinit: rc-update is not
         evidence of boot durability, the symlink is.
disable  remove that link. The unit stays installed.
status   report what is installed, whether it matches this tree, the identities, and the boot link. Read-only.

Run as root (not needed for --dry-run or status). Nothing is started or stopped by this tool: start it with
  rc-service honey-fca-egress start
--dry-run   print what would change; change nothing.
USAGE
}

DRY=0 VERB="" EXPECT=""
while [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; do
    case "$1" in
        -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
        --dry-run) DRY=1 ;;
        --expect-relay-sha256) shift; [ "$#" -gt 0 ] || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; EXPECT="$1" ;;
        install|enable|disable|status) [ -z "$VERB" ] || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; VERB="$1" ;;
        *) usage >&2; exit 2 ;;
    esac
    shift
done
[ -n "$VERB" ] || { usage >&2; exit 2; }

lane="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "$(readlink -f -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")")/.." && pwd)"
src_relay="$lane/egress/$RELAY_NAME"
src_body="$lane/openrc/honey-fca-egress"
run() { if [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ]; then echo "would: $*"; else "$@"; fi; }
fail() { echo "honey-fca-egress-install: $*" >&2; exit 1; }
need_root() { [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ] || fail "run as root"; }
sha() { sha256sum "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; }

do_install() {
    local ts problems=() got dst
    ts="$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
    [ -r "$src_relay" ] || problems+=("missing $src_relay")
    [ -r "$src_body" ] || problems+=("missing $src_body")
    case "$EXPECT" in
        "") problems+=("--expect-relay-sha256 is required: pass the hash recorded in the review") ;;
        *[!0-9a-f]*) problems+=("--expect-relay-sha256 is not a lowercase hex sha256") ;;
        *) if [ "${#EXPECT}" -ne 64 ]; then problems+=("--expect-relay-sha256 is not 64 hex characters")
           elif [ -r "$src_relay" ]; then
               got="$(sha "$src_relay")"
               [ "$got" = "$EXPECT" ] || problems+=("relay hash mismatch: this tree has $got, expected $EXPECT")
           fi ;;
    esac
    if [ "${#problems[@]}" -gt 0 ]; then
        printf 'honey-fca-egress-install: %s\n' "${problems[@]}" >&2
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || exit 1
        echo "honey-fca-egress-install: dry-run continues; a real run would refuse" >&2
    fi
    need_root
    run install -d -m 0755 -o root -g root "$RELAY_DIR" "$(dirname "$INIT")"
    for pair in "$src_relay:$RELAY_DIR/$RELAY_NAME:0644" "$src_body:$INIT:0755"; do
        IFS=: read -r s d m <<<"$pair"
        if [ -e "$d" ] && cmp -s "$s" "$d"; then echo "unchanged $d"; continue; fi
        [ -e "$d" ] && run cp -p "$d" "$d.bak-$ts"
        run install -m "$m" -o root -g root "$s" "$d"
        [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || echo "installed $d"
    done
    echo "not started and not enabled: 'rc-service honey-fca-egress start' and 'honey-fca-egress-install enable' are separate steps"
}

do_enable() {
    [ -e "$INIT" ] || [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || fail "$INIT is not installed; run install first"
    need_root
    if [ -L "$BOOT_LINK" ] && [ "$(readlink "$BOOT_LINK")" = ../init.d/honey-fca-egress ]; then echo "already enabled $BOOT_LINK"; return 0; fi
    [ ! -e "$BOOT_LINK" ] || [ -L "$BOOT_LINK" ] || fail "$BOOT_LINK exists and is not a symlink; refusing"
    run install -d -m 0755 "$(dirname "$BOOT_LINK")"
    run ln -sfn ../init.d/honey-fca-egress "$BOOT_LINK"
    [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || echo "enabled $BOOT_LINK"
}

do_disable() {
    need_root
    if [ -L "$BOOT_LINK" ]; then run rm -f "$BOOT_LINK"; [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ] || echo "disabled $BOOT_LINK"; else echo "not enabled"; fi
}

do_status() {
    local f
    for f in "$RELAY_DIR/$RELAY_NAME:$src_relay" "$INIT:$src_body"; do
        if [ ! -e "${f%%:*}" ]; then echo "missing    ${f%%:*}"
        elif cmp -s "${f%%:*}" "${f#*:}"; then echo "matches    ${f%%:*}"
        else echo "DIFFERS    ${f%%:*} (not the reviewed bytes of this tree)"; fi
    done
    if [ -L "$BOOT_LINK" ] && [ "$(readlink "$BOOT_LINK")" = ../init.d/honey-fca-egress ]; then echo "boot link  present ($BOOT_LINK)"; else echo "boot link  ABSENT (the unit will not start at boot)"; fi
    id -u fca-egress >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "identity   fca-egress uid $(id -u fca-egress), groups: $(id -G fca-egress)" || echo "identity   fca-egress MISSING"
    [ -S "$PREFIX/run/fca-dsh-egress/relay.sock" ] && echo "socket     present" || echo "socket     absent (relay not running)"
}

case "$VERB" in
    install) do_install ;;
    enable) do_enable ;;
    disable) do_disable ;;
    status) do_status ;;
esac
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/openrc/test_honey_fca_egress.py :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/python3"
"""Tests for the relay's OpenRC unit and its installer. Run from the tangled tree: python3 test_honey_fca_egress.py

The unit is exercised without starting anything: its start_pre runs in a harness shell with stubbed identity and
OpenRC helpers (every refusal path), and the installer runs against a scratch PREFIX. The root-owned checks need
passwordless sudo; without it those tests are SKIPPED by name, never passed.

The RealOpenRC class runs the unit under the real openrc-run and is GATED: it FAILS, naming the hazard, unless HONEY_DISPOSABLE_OPENRC_HOST
carries the exact statement in DISPOSABLE_VALUE, and it fails even then on a host with production init scripts (see rehearsal_gate). On a host
with no openrc-run it is a named skip. Do not run it on Honey: it is followed by OpenRC-managed python3 services crashing there (2026-11-12)."""
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
LANE = os.path.dirname(HERE)
BODY = os.path.join(HERE, 'honey-fca-egress')
INSTALL = os.path.join(HERE, 'honey-fca-egress-install')
RELAY = os.path.join(LANE, 'egress', 'fca_egress_relay.py')
EGRESS_MD = os.path.join(LANE, 'egress', 'EGRESS.md')


def read(path):
    with open(path) as f:
        return f.read()


def sudo_ok():
    return subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'true'], capture_output=True).returncode == 0


def sh(script, env=None, timeout=30):
    return subprocess.run(['sh', '-c', script], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, env=env)


def sha(path):
    return subprocess.run(['sha256sum', path], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.split()[0]


def need_sudo(test):
    def wrapper(self):
        if not sudo_ok():
            self.skipTest('SKIPPED %s: passwordless sudo is needed to make root-owned fixtures' % test.__name__)
        return test(self)
    wrapper.__name__ = test.__name__
    return wrapper


class UnitBody(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_body_parses_in_sh_and_bash(self):
        for shell in ('sh', 'bash'):
            r = subprocess.run([shell, '-n', BODY], capture_output=True, text=True)
            self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, (shell, r.stderr))

    def test_the_unit_runs_exactly_the_relay_as_the_unprivileged_identity(self):
        r = sh('. "%s"; printf "%%s\\n" "$command" "$command_args" "$command_user" "$command_background" "$pidfile" "$run_dir"' % BODY)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr)
        command, args, user, background, pidfile, run_dir = r.stdout.splitlines()
        self.assertEqual(command, '/usr/bin/python3')
        self.assertEqual(args, '-I /opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py')  # isolated mode, the relay, and NO other argument
        self.assertEqual(user, 'fca-egress:fca-egress')
        self.assertEqual(background, 'true')
        self.assertEqual(pidfile, '/run/honey-fca-egress.pid')
        self.assertEqual(run_dir, '/run/fca-dsh-egress')
        self.assertFalse(re.search(r'command_user="?root', read(BODY)))

    def test_the_unit_names_nothing_the_relay_must_not_read(self):
        text = read(BODY)
        for forbidden in ('/home/gille', 'agent-deepseek/', 'cortex', 'env_file', 'export '):
            self.assertNotIn(forbidden, text)

    def test_egress_md_documents_the_unit_and_installer(self):
        text = read(EGRESS_MD)
        for name in ('honey-fca-egress', 'honey-fca-egress-install', '--expect-relay-sha256', 'S04honey-fca-egress', 'start_post', 'command_user'):
            self.assertIn(name, text)


HARNESS = r'''
eerror() { echo "E: $*" >&2; }
einfo() { :; }
checkpath() { echo "checkpath $*" >> "$LOG"; }
setfacl() { echo "setfacl $*" >> "$LOG"; }
id() {
    case "$1:$2" in
        -u:fca-egress) [ "$T_NO_IDENTITY" = 1 ] && return 1; echo "${T_RELAY_UID:-985}" ;;
        -G:fca-egress) echo "${T_RELAY_GROUPS:-985}" ;;
        -g:fca-egress) echo 985 ;;
        -u:agent-deepseek) echo "${T_WORKER_UID:-2012}" ;;
        *) command id "$@" ;;
    esac
}
relay_root="$T_ROOT"
. "$BODY"
relay_root="$T_ROOT"
start_pre
echo "rc=$?"
'''


class StartPre(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-unit-')
        self.log = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'calls.log')
        with open(self.log, 'w'):
            pass
        self.root = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'egress')

    def tearDown(self):
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'rm', '-rf', self.tmp], capture_output=True)

    def make_root(self, root_owned=True, mode='0755', relay=True):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        if relay:
            with open(os.path.join(self.root, 'fca_egress_relay.py'), 'w') as f:
                f.write('# fixture\n')
        if root_owned:
            subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'chown', '-R', 'root:root', self.root], check=True)
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'chmod', '-R', mode if mode else '0755', self.root], check=True)

    def run_pre(self, **env_extra):
        env = dict(os.environ, LOG=self.log, BODY=BODY, T_ROOT=self.root, **env_extra)
        r = sh(HARNESS, env=env)
        calls = read(self.log).splitlines()
        return r.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1], r.stderr.strip(), calls

    @need_sudo
    def test_a_good_environment_creates_the_socket_dir_and_acl_and_nothing_else(self):
        self.make_root()
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre()
        self.assertEqual(rc, 'rc=0', err)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ['checkpath -d -m 0700 -o fca-egress:fca-egress /run/fca-dsh-egress',
                                 'setfacl -m u:2012:--x /run/fca-dsh-egress',
                                 'checkpath -f -m 0640 -o fca-egress:fca-egress /var/log/honey-fca-egress.log'])

    def test_missing_relay_identity_is_refused_before_anything_is_created(self):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre(T_NO_IDENTITY='1')
        self.assertEqual(rc, 'rc=1')
        self.assertIn('no fca-egress identity', err)
        self.assertEqual(calls, [])

    def test_uid_zero_relay_identity_is_refused(self):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre(T_RELAY_UID='0')
        self.assertEqual((rc, calls), ('rc=1', []))
        self.assertIn('uid 0', err)

    def test_a_relay_identity_with_supplementary_groups_is_refused(self):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre(T_RELAY_GROUPS='985 2000')
        self.assertEqual((rc, calls), ('rc=1', []))
        self.assertIn('supplementary groups', err)

    def test_a_worker_that_is_not_uid_2012_is_refused(self):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre(T_WORKER_UID='2013')
        self.assertEqual((rc, calls), ('rc=1', []))
        self.assertIn('not uid 2012', err)

    def test_a_missing_relay_file_is_refused(self):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre()
        self.assertEqual((rc, calls), ('rc=1', []))
        self.assertIn('missing', err)

    def test_a_relay_directory_not_owned_by_root_is_refused(self):
        os.makedirs(self.root)
        with open(os.path.join(self.root, 'fca_egress_relay.py'), 'w') as f:
            f.write('# owned by the test user\n')
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre()
        self.assertEqual((rc, calls), ('rc=1', []))
        self.assertIn('not root-owned and read-only', err)

    @need_sudo
    def test_a_group_writable_relay_file_is_refused_even_when_root_owned(self):
        self.make_root()
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'chmod', 'g+w', os.path.join(self.root, 'fca_egress_relay.py')], check=True)
        rc, err, calls = self.run_pre()
        self.assertEqual((rc, calls), ('rc=1', []))
        self.assertIn('not root-owned and read-only', err)


OPENRC_RUN = '/sbin/openrc-run'
RC_BIN = '/lib/rc/bin'

# The RealOpenRC tests run the unit under the REAL openrc-run. On 2026-11-12 they were followed, on Honey, by OpenRC-managed python3
# services crashing (identity-proxy, which Aura's every chat depends on, and others; the mechanism is NOT confirmed). They therefore
# refuse to run unless the operator states that the host is disposable, and refuse even then if it carries production services.
# A refusal is a FAILURE on purpose: a skip that looks like a pass is how a hazard ships a second time.
DISPOSABLE_VAR = 'HONEY_DISPOSABLE_OPENRC_HOST'
DISPOSABLE_VALUE = 'yes-this-host-may-lose-every-python3-service'
PRODUCTION_MARKERS = ('/etc/init.d/identity-proxy', '/etc/init.d/gille-llama-swap', '/etc/init.d/hermes-gateway')


def rehearsal_gate(env, exists, sudo, identities):
    """('skip' | 'refuse' | 'run', message). Pure: no command is run here. Order matters: without openrc-run nothing can happen, so
    that is a named skip; with it, the operator's explicit statement comes before anything else."""
    if not exists(OPENRC_RUN):
        return 'skip', 'SKIPPED: there is no %s on this host; the RealOpenRC tests need a DISPOSABLE OpenRC host' % OPENRC_RUN
    if env.get(DISPOSABLE_VAR) != DISPOSABLE_VALUE:
        return 'refuse', ('REFUSED: the RealOpenRC tests run %s on THIS host, and on 2026-11-12 they were followed by OpenRC-managed python3 '
                          'services (identity-proxy and others) crashing on Honey. Run them only on a DISPOSABLE OpenRC host and say so with '
                          '%s=%s. This is a failure on purpose: a skip would look like a pass.' % (OPENRC_RUN, DISPOSABLE_VAR, DISPOSABLE_VALUE))
    marked = [m for m in PRODUCTION_MARKERS if exists(m)]
    if marked:
        return 'refuse', ('REFUSED: %s is set but this host carries production services (%s). The variable does not override that; '
                          'use a host that has none.' % (DISPOSABLE_VAR, ', '.join(marked)))
    if not (sudo() and identities()):
        return 'skip', 'SKIPPED: needs passwordless sudo and the fca-egress and agent-deepseek identities'
    return 'run', ''


def have_identities():
    return all(subprocess.run(['id', '-u', u], capture_output=True).returncode == 0 for u in ('fca-egress', 'agent-deepseek'))


class RealOpenRC(unittest.TestCase):
    """The unit under the real OpenRC. Rehearsals use a COPY with four path variables pointed at a scratch directory and a stand-in
    for the relay (a different service name), so nothing of the real service, /run/fca-dsh-egress or /etc/init.d is touched."""

    def setUp(self):
        verdict, why = rehearsal_gate(os.environ, os.path.exists, sudo_ok, have_identities)
        if verdict == 'skip':
            self.skipTest(why)
        if verdict == 'refuse':
            self.fail(why)
        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-rehearsal-')
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'chown', 'root:root', self.tmp], check=True)
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'chmod', '0755', self.tmp], check=True)
        self.relay_dir = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'relay')
        self.run_dir = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'run')
        self.pidfile = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'unit.pid')
        self.unit = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'honey-fca-egress-rehearsal')
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'install', '-d', '-m', '0755', '-o', 'root', '-g', 'root', self.relay_dir], check=True)
        text = read(BODY)
        for old, new in (('relay_root:=/opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress', 'relay_root:=%s' % self.relay_dir),
                         ('run_dir=/run/fca-dsh-egress', 'run_dir=%s' % self.run_dir),
                         ('log_file=/var/log/honey-fca-egress.log', 'log_file=%s/unit.log' % self.tmp),
                         ('pidfile="/run/honey-fca-egress.pid"', 'pidfile="%s"' % self.pidfile)):
            self.assertEqual(text.count(old), 1, old)
            text = text.replace(old, new)
        staged = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), os.path.basename(self.tmp) + '.unit')
        with open(staged, 'w') as f:
            f.write(text)
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'install', '-m', '0755', '-o', 'root', '-g', 'root', staged, self.unit], check=True)
        os.unlink(staged)

    def tearDown(self):
        if not hasattr(self, 'tmp'):
            return
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', OPENRC_RUN, self.unit, 'stop'], capture_output=True)
        if os.path.exists(self.pidfile):
            pid = read(self.pidfile).strip()
            subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'kill', '-KILL', pid], capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'rm', '-rf', self.tmp], capture_output=True)

    def stand_in(self, binds):
        body = ('import socket, time\n'
                + ('s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX)\ns.bind(%r)\n' % (self.run_dir + '/relay.sock') if binds else '')
                + 'time.sleep(60)\n')
        staged = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), os.path.basename(self.tmp) + '.relay')
        with open(staged, 'w') as f:
            f.write(body)
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'install', '-m', '0644', '-o', 'root', '-g', 'root', staged,
                        os.path.join(self.relay_dir, 'fca_egress_relay.py')], check=True)
        os.unlink(staged)

    def dir_state(self):
        """The socket directory's ACL entries (owner rwx, the worker traverse-only, nothing for group or other) and its owner. stat's mode
        is not used: with a named ACL entry its group bits show the mask, so 0700 plus u:2012:--x reads as 710."""
        acl = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'getfacl', '-p', '-c', self.run_dir], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
        owner = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'stat', '-c', '%U:%G', self.run_dir], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
        return sorted(acl), owner

    def rc(self, verb):
        return subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', OPENRC_RUN, self.unit, verb], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90)

    def owner_of(self, pid):
        return subprocess.run(['ps', '-o', 'user=', '-p', pid], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()

    def test_openrc_accepts_the_real_unit_file(self):
        r = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', OPENRC_RUN, BODY, 'describe'], capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr)
        self.assertIn('fixed-upstream egress relay', r.stdout)
        r = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', OPENRC_RUN, BODY, 'status'], capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertIn(r.returncode, (0, 3), r.stdout + r.stderr)  # started or stopped, but parsed and answered
        self.assertIn('status:', r.stdout)

    def test_a_second_start_is_a_no_op_and_the_relay_never_runs_as_root(self):
        self.stand_in(binds=True)
        first = self.rc('start')
        self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
        pid = read(self.pidfile).strip()
        self.assertEqual(self.owner_of(pid), 'fca-egress')
        second = self.rc('start')
        self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 0, second.stdout + second.stderr)
        self.assertIn('already', second.stdout + second.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(read(self.pidfile).strip(), pid)
        self.assertEqual(self.dir_state(), (['group::---', 'mask::--x', 'other::---', 'user::rwx', 'user:agent-deepseek:--x'], 'fca-egress:fca-egress'))
        stopped = self.rc('stop')
        self.assertEqual(stopped.returncode, 0, stopped.stdout + stopped.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'test', '-e', self.run_dir + '/relay.sock']).returncode, 1)  # the socket is gone
        self.assertEqual(subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'test', '-f', self.relay_dir + '/fca_egress_relay.py']).returncode, 0)  # the code is not
        self.assertEqual(subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'test', '-d', self.run_dir]).returncode, 0)  # nor is the directory

    def test_a_relay_that_never_binds_its_socket_fails_the_start_loudly_and_is_not_left_running(self):
        self.stand_in(binds=False)
        r = self.rc('start')
        self.assertNotEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        self.assertIn('did not create', r.stdout + r.stderr)
        status = self.rc('status')
        self.assertNotEqual(status.returncode, 0, status.stdout)  # not reported as started
        leftover = subprocess.run(['pgrep', '-u', 'fca-egress', '-f', 'fca_egress_relay.py'], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
        self.assertEqual(leftover, [])

    def test_start_pre_twice_is_idempotent_and_corrects_a_loosened_directory(self):
        self.stand_in(binds=True)
        script = ('eerror() { echo "E: $*" >&2; }; PATH="%s:$PATH"; . "%s"; relay_root="%s"; run_dir="%s"; log_file="%s/unit.log"; '
                  'start_pre; echo first=$?; chmod 0755 "$run_dir"; start_pre; echo second=$?'
                  % (RC_BIN, BODY, self.relay_dir, self.run_dir, self.tmp))
        r = subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'sh', '-c', script], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
        self.assertEqual([l for l in r.stdout.split() if l.startswith(('first=', 'second='))], ['first=0', 'second=0'], r.stdout + r.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(self.dir_state(), (['group::---', 'mask::--x', 'other::---', 'user::rwx', 'user:agent-deepseek:--x'], 'fca-egress:fca-egress'))


STOP_HARNESS = r'''
ebegin() { :; }
eend() { return "${1:-0}"; }
eerror() { echo "E: $*" >&2; }
. "$BODY"
pidfile="$T_PID"
stop
echo "rc=$?"
'''


class StopSafety(unittest.TestCase):
    """The unit's own stop(). OpenRC's default stop passes --exec /usr/bin/python3, which with no pidfile SIGTERMs every OpenRC-started python3
    process on the host (reproduced 2026-11-12). These tests run stop() in a stubbed shell: no real start-stop-daemon, no real signal."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-stop-')
        self.log = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'calls.log')
        self.pid = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'unit.pid')
        open(self.log, 'w').close()
        self.bin = os.path.join(self.tmp, 'bin')
        os.makedirs(self.bin)
        stub = os.path.join(self.bin, 'start-stop-daemon')   # a hyphenated name cannot be a shell function in dash: a stub on PATH
        with open(stub, 'w') as f:
            f.write('#!/bin/sh\necho "ssd $*" >> "$LOG"\n')
        os.chmod(stub, 0o755)

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    def run_stop(self):
        r = sh(STOP_HARNESS, env=dict(os.environ, LOG=self.log, BODY=BODY, T_PID=self.pid, PATH=self.bin + ':' + os.environ['PATH']))
        self.assertIn('rc=0', r.stdout, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        return read(self.log).splitlines()

    def test_the_unit_never_passes_exec_to_start_stop_daemon(self):
        code = '\n'.join(l for l in read(BODY).splitlines() if not l.lstrip().startswith('#'))
        self.assertNotIn('--exec', code)
        self.assertRegex(code, r'(?m)^stop\(\) \{')

    def test_no_pidfile_means_nothing_is_signalled(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.run_stop(), [])

    def test_an_empty_pidfile_means_nothing_is_signalled(self):
        open(self.pid, 'w').close()
        self.assertEqual(self.run_stop(), [])

    def test_a_pidfile_of_a_dead_process_is_cleared_and_nothing_is_signalled(self):
        p = subprocess.Popen(['true'])
        p.wait()
        with open(self.pid, 'w') as f:
            f.write('%d\n' % p.pid)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_stop(), [])
        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(self.pid))

    def test_a_live_pid_is_stopped_by_pidfile_and_user_only(self):
        p = subprocess.Popen(['sleep', '30'])
        try:
            with open(self.pid, 'w') as f:
                f.write('%d\n' % p.pid)
            calls = self.run_stop()
        finally:
            p.kill()
            p.wait()
        self.assertEqual(len(calls), 1, calls)
        self.assertEqual(calls[0], 'ssd --stop --quiet --retry TERM/5/KILL/3 --user fca-egress --pidfile %s' % self.pid)

    def test_a_failed_start_stops_through_the_same_safe_stop(self):
        text = '\n'.join(l for l in read(BODY).splitlines() if not l.lstrip().startswith('#'))
        post = text[text.index('start_post()'):text.index('stop() {')]
        self.assertRegex(post, r'(?m)^    stop >/dev/null 2>&1$')
        self.assertNotIn('start-stop-daemon', post)


class RehearsalGate(unittest.TestCase):
    """The gate in front of the RealOpenRC tests, tested as pure logic: none of these runs openrc-run."""
    VAR = {DISPOSABLE_VAR: DISPOSABLE_VALUE}

    def gate(self, env, present=(OPENRC_RUN,), sudo=True, identities=True):
        return rehearsal_gate(env, lambda p: p in present, lambda: sudo, lambda: identities)

    def test_no_openrc_on_the_host_is_a_named_skip(self):
        verdict, msg = self.gate({}, present=())
        self.assertEqual(verdict, 'skip')
        self.assertIn('SKIPPED', msg)

    def test_openrc_present_and_no_statement_is_a_refusal_that_names_the_hazard(self):
        verdict, msg = self.gate({})
        self.assertEqual(verdict, 'refuse')
        for word in ('REFUSED', 'identity-proxy', 'DISPOSABLE', DISPOSABLE_VAR, 'failure on purpose'):
            self.assertIn(word, msg)

    def test_a_loose_or_wrong_statement_does_not_count(self):
        for value in ('', '1', 'yes', 'true', DISPOSABLE_VALUE.upper(), DISPOSABLE_VALUE + ' '):
            self.assertEqual(self.gate({DISPOSABLE_VAR: value})[0], 'refuse', value)

    def test_the_statement_is_overridden_by_any_production_service_on_the_host(self):
        for marker in PRODUCTION_MARKERS:
            verdict, msg = self.gate(self.VAR, present=(OPENRC_RUN, marker))
            self.assertEqual(verdict, 'refuse', marker)
            self.assertIn(marker, msg)

    def test_only_the_exact_statement_on_a_clean_host_runs(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.gate(self.VAR), ('run', ''))
        self.assertEqual(self.gate(self.VAR, sudo=False)[0], 'skip')
        self.assertEqual(self.gate(self.VAR, identities=False)[0], 'skip')

    def test_the_real_tests_ask_the_gate_before_anything_else_in_setup(self):
        src = read(__file__)
        setup = src[src.index('class RealOpenRC'):]
        setup = setup[setup.index('def setUp'):setup.index('def tearDown')]
        self.assertIn('rehearsal_gate(', setup)
        self.assertLess(setup.index('rehearsal_gate('), setup.index('mkdtemp'))
        self.assertLess(setup.index('rehearsal_gate('), setup.index('subprocess'))


class Installer(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.prefix = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='fca-prefix-')
        self.good = sha(RELAY)

    def tearDown(self):
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'rm', '-rf', self.prefix], capture_output=True)

    def run_inst(self, *args, root=True, **env):
        env = dict(os.environ, HONEY_FCA_EGRESS_PREFIX=self.prefix, **env)
        cmd = ([ 'sudo', '-n', '--preserve-env=HONEY_FCA_EGRESS_PREFIX'] if root else []) + [INSTALL] + list(args)
        return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, timeout=60)

    def written(self):
        out = []
        for d, _, files in os.walk(self.prefix):
            out += [os.path.relpath(os.path.join(d, f), self.prefix) for f in files]
        return sorted(out)

    def test_install_without_the_expected_hash_is_refused_and_writes_nothing(self):
        r = self.run_inst('--dry-run', 'install', root=False)  # dry run continues but says a real run would refuse
        self.assertIn('--expect-relay-sha256 is required', r.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(self.written(), [])
        self.assertEqual(self.run_inst('install', root=False).returncode, 1)

    def test_a_wrong_hash_is_refused_and_writes_nothing(self):
        r = self.run_inst('--dry-run', '--expect-relay-sha256', '0' * 64, 'install', root=False)
        self.assertIn('relay hash mismatch', r.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(self.written(), [])

    def test_a_malformed_hash_is_refused(self):
        for bad in ('abc', 'G' * 64, self.good.upper()):
            r = self.run_inst('--dry-run', '--expect-relay-sha256', bad, 'install', root=False)
            self.assertIn('--expect-relay-sha256', r.stderr, bad)
            self.assertEqual(self.written(), [])

    def test_dry_run_with_the_right_hash_prints_the_plan_and_writes_nothing(self):
        r = self.run_inst('--dry-run', '--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install', root=False)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr)
        self.assertIn('would: install -m 0644 -o root -g root', r.stdout)
        self.assertEqual(self.written(), [])

    @need_sudo
    def test_install_places_only_the_relay_and_the_unit_with_root_ownership(self):
        r = self.run_inst('--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install')
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(self.written(), ['etc/init.d/honey-fca-egress', 'opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py'])
        for rel, mode in (('opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py', '644'), ('etc/init.d/honey-fca-egress', '755'),
                          ('opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress', '755')):
            st = subprocess.run(['stat', '-c', '%a %U:%G', os.path.join(self.prefix, rel)], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
            self.assertEqual(st, [mode, 'root:root'], rel)
        self.assertEqual(sha(os.path.join(self.prefix, 'opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py')), self.good)
        self.assertIn('not started and not enabled', r.stdout)

    @need_sudo
    def test_a_second_install_changes_nothing_and_a_drifted_file_is_backed_up(self):
        self.run_inst('--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install')
        again = self.run_inst('--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install')
        self.assertEqual(again.stdout.count('unchanged'), 2, again.stdout)
