        target = os.path.join(self.prefix, 'etc/init.d/honey-fca-egress')
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'sh', '-c', 'echo drift >> "%s"' % target], check=True)
        r = self.run_inst('--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install')
        self.assertIn('installed %s' % target, r.stdout)
        self.assertEqual(len([f for f in self.written() if '.bak-' in f]), 1)

    @need_sudo
    def test_enable_creates_the_runlevel_link_and_disable_removes_it(self):
        self.run_inst('--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install')
        status = self.run_inst('status', root=False).stdout
        self.assertIn('boot link  ABSENT', status)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_inst('enable').returncode, 0)
        link = os.path.join(self.prefix, 'etc/rc2.d/S04honey-fca-egress')
        self.assertEqual(os.readlink(link), '../init.d/honey-fca-egress')
        self.assertIn('boot link  present', self.run_inst('status', root=False).stdout)
        self.assertIn('already enabled', self.run_inst('enable').stdout)
        self.assertEqual(self.run_inst('disable').returncode, 0)
        self.assertFalse(os.path.lexists(link))

    def test_enable_refuses_when_the_unit_is_not_installed(self):
        if not sudo_ok():
            self.skipTest('SKIPPED: passwordless sudo is needed to run the installer as root')
        r = self.run_inst('enable')
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 1)
        self.assertIn('is not installed', r.stderr)

    @need_sudo
    def test_status_reports_a_drifted_unit_as_different_from_this_tree(self):
        self.run_inst('--expect-relay-sha256', self.good, 'install')
        subprocess.run(['sudo', '-n', 'sh', '-c', 'echo drift >> "%s"' % os.path.join(self.prefix, 'opt/honey-fca-dsh/egress/fca_egress_relay.py')], check=True)
        self.assertIn('DIFFERS', self.run_inst('status', root=False).stdout)

    def test_the_installer_never_starts_or_stops_anything(self):
        text = re.sub(r"cat <<'USAGE'.*?\nUSAGE\n", '', read(INSTALL), flags=re.S)  # the usage and echo text may NAME commands
        called = re.findall(r'^\s*(?:run\s+)?(rc-service|rc-update|service|start-stop-daemon|kill|pkill|systemctl)\b', text, flags=re.M)
        self.assertEqual(called, [])


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-fca-dsh/openrc/mutation-check-boot.sh :comments no :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash"
# Mutation proof for the relay's OpenRC unit and installer: each sabotaged copy MUST fail the suite, by a NAMED unittest failure.
# A pass, a stall, a skip or a REFUSAL is never a kill. A mutant only a RealOpenRC test can kill is reported UNPROVEN (exit 3) unless those tests
# ran on a declared-disposable OpenRC host; it is never counted as killed. Exit 0 = every mutant killed, 1 = a survivor or an error, 3 = unproven only.
# Disposable space only; the candidate is not modified. Do NOT set HONEY_DISPOSABLE_OPENRC_HOST on a host that carries services.
set -uo pipefail
HERE="$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd -P)"
LANE="$(dirname "$HERE")"
TMP="$(mktemp -d)"; trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
fail=0; killed=0; unproven=0
mutate() { # name file sed-expression
  local name="$1" file="$2" expr="$3" d="$TMP/$1" out rc names
  mkdir -p "$d"; cp -r "$LANE/egress" "$LANE/openrc" "$d"/
  sed -i "$expr" "$d/openrc/$file"
  if cmp -s "$HERE/$file" "$d/openrc/$file"; then echo "MUTATION NOT APPLIED: $name"; fail=1; return; fi
  out="$(timeout 300 python3 "$d/openrc/test_honey_fca_egress.py" 2>&1)"; rc=$?
  if [ "$rc" = 124 ]; then echo "TIMEOUT (infrastructure, NOT a kill): $name [$file]"; fail=1; return; fi
  if ! printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -qE '^(FAILED \(|OK)'; then
    echo "INFRASTRUCTURE ERROR (not a kill): $name [$file]"; printf '%s\n' "$out" | tail -8 | sed 's/^/    | /'; fail=1; return; fi
  # Evidence of a kill = a named failure OTHER than the RealOpenRC tests when those REFUSED (they refuse on any host that has not been declared
  # disposable, and a refusal fails every mutant alike, so it proves nothing). A skip proves nothing either.
  names="$(printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -E '^(FAIL|ERROR):' )"
  if printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -q 'REFUSED:'; then names="$(printf '%s\n' "$names" | grep -v 'RealOpenRC')"; fi
  names="$(printf '%s\n' "$names" | sed 's/^[A-Z]*: //; s/ (.*//' | sed '/^$/d' | head -3 | tr '\n' ' ')"
  if [ -n "$names" ]; then killed=$((killed+1)); echo "killed: $name [$file] by: $names"
  elif [ "$rc" != 0 ] || printf '%s\n' "$out" | grep -qE 'skipped=|REFUSED:'; then
    unproven=$((unproven+1)); echo "UNPROVEN (only a RealOpenRC test could kill it, and those did not run here; needs a disposable OpenRC host): $name [$file]"
  else echo "SURVIVED (tests too weak): $name [$file]"; fail=1; fi
}
B=honey-fca-egress I=honey-fca-egress-install
mutate runs-as-root              $B "s/^command_user=.*/command_user=\"root:root\"/"
mutate extra-relay-argument      $B "s|^command_args=\"-I \(.*\)\"|command_args=\"-I \1 --upstream example.com\"|"
mutate no-uid0-check             $B "s/\[ \"\$(id -u \"\${relay_user}\")\" -ne 0 \] ||/true ||/"
mutate no-supplementary-check    $B "s/\[ \"\$(id -G \"\${relay_user}\")\" = \"\$(id -g \"\${relay_user}\")\" \] ||/true ||/"
mutate no-worker-uid-check       $B "s/\[ \"\$(id -u \"\${worker_user}\" 2>\/dev\/null)\" = \"\${worker_uid}\" \] ||/true ||/"
mutate no-relay-file-check       $B "s/\[ -f \"\${relay_root}\/fca_egress_relay.py\" \] ||/true ||/"
mutate no-root-owned-check       $B "s/\\\\( ! -user root -o -perm \/022 \\\\)/-name no-such-file/"
mutate socket-dir-world-open     $B "s/checkpath -d -m 0700/checkpath -d -m 0755/"
mutate acl-grants-write          $B "s/u:\${worker_uid}:--x/u:\${worker_uid}:rwx/"
mutate hash-not-compared         $I "s/\[ \"\$got\" = \"\$EXPECT\" \] ||/true ||/"
mutate hash-optional             $I "s/^        \"\") problems+=(.*/        \"\") ;;/"
mutate installer-starts-service  $I "s/^    echo \"not started and not enabled.*/&\n    run rc-service honey-fca-egress start/"
mutate enable-links-wrong-unit   $I "s|ln -sfn ../init.d/honey-fca-egress|ln -sfn ../init.d/something-else|"
mutate no-backup-of-drifted-file $I "s/\[ -e \"\$d\" \] \&\& run cp -p \"\$d\" \"\$d.bak-\$ts\"/true/"
mutate start-post-never-fails        $B "/^    stop >\/dev\/null 2>&1\$/{n;s/return 1/return 0/}"
mutate start-post-leaves-relay-up    $B "/^    stop >\/dev\/null 2>&1\$/d"
mutate start-post-skips-socket-wait  $B "s/\[ -S \"\${run_dir}\/relay.sock\" \] \&\& return 0/true \&\& return 0/"
mutate stop-post-removes-run-dir     $B "s|rm -f \"\${run_dir}/relay.sock\"|rm -rf \"\${run_dir}\"|"
mutate no-acl-for-worker             $B "/setfacl -m/d"
mutate log-world-readable            $B "s/checkpath -f -m 0640/checkpath -f -m 0644/"
mutate stop-passes-exec              $B "s|--user \"\${relay_user}\" --pidfile|--user \"\${relay_user}\" --exec /usr/bin/python3 --pidfile|"
mutate stop-without-liveness-guard   $B "s|if \[ ! -d \"/proc/\${pid}\" \]; then|if false; then|"
mutate stop-without-empty-guard      $B "/\[ -n \"\${pid}\" \] || return 0/d"
mutate stop-without-user-filter      $B "s| --user \"\${relay_user}\" --pidfile| --pidfile|"
mutate start-post-uses-raw-ssd       $B "s|^    stop >/dev/null 2>&1\$|    start-stop-daemon --stop --quiet --pidfile \"\${pidfile}\" >/dev/null 2>\&1|"
echo "mutants: $killed killed, $unproven UNPROVEN, survivors/errors counted in the exit status"
[ "$fail" = 0 ] || exit 1
[ "$unproven" = 0 ] || exit 3
exit 0
#+END_SRC

* Ductor voice service lane (Badger and Flying Fox)

The Ductor voice watcher is a host service, not a stowed dotfile. Its implementation is canonical in [[file:agents-voice.org][agents-voice.org]]; this host module owns only the OpenRC body, private configuration template, and narrow installer. The installer copies exactly the provider-aware watcher and service body, backs up each replaced file, never starts or restarts a service, and never contains a bot token, owner chat ID, host address, or account-specific home path. The live engine root and Ductor homes are supplied by the machine-local =/etc/conf.d= file.

#+BEGIN_SRC sh :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-voice/openrc/honey-voice :tangle-mode (identity #o755)
#!/sbin/openrc-run
# Generated by agents-bots-honey.org. Configure in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}.

: "${honey_voice_user:?set honey_voice_user in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}}"
: "${honey_voice_group:=${honey_voice_user}}"
: "${honey_voice_engine_root:?set honey_voice_engine_root in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}}"
: "${honey_voice_ductor_home:?set honey_voice_ductor_home in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}}"
: "${honey_voice_rollout_home:?set honey_voice_rollout_home in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}}"
: "${honey_voice_bot:?set honey_voice_bot in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}}"
: "${honey_voice_provider:?set honey_voice_provider in /etc/conf.d/${RC_SVCNAME}}"

user_home="$(getent passwd "${honey_voice_user}" | cut -d: -f6)"
: "${user_home:?cannot resolve honey_voice_user home}"
name="${RC_SVCNAME}"
description="Piper voice notes (${honey_voice_bot}/${honey_voice_provider})"
command="${honey_voice_python:-/usr/bin/python3}"
command_args="${honey_voice_engine_root}/bin/provider-rollout-watcher.py"
command_user="${honey_voice_user}:${honey_voice_group}"
command_background=true
pidfile="/run/${RC_SVCNAME}.pid"
output_log="/var/log/${RC_SVCNAME}.log"
error_log="/var/log/${RC_SVCNAME}.log"
directory="${user_home}"

export HOME="${user_home}"
export PATH="${PATH}:${user_home}/.local/bin"
export PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
export HONEY_VOICE_BOT="${honey_voice_bot}"
export HONEY_VOICE_PROVIDER="${honey_voice_provider}"
export HONEY_VOICE_DUCTOR_HOME="${honey_voice_ductor_home}"
export HONEY_VOICE_ROLLOUT_HOME="${honey_voice_rollout_home}"
[ -n "${honey_voice_read_as:-}" ] && export HONEY_VOICE_READ_AS="${honey_voice_read_as}"

depend() {
    use net dns
    case "${RC_SVCNAME}" in
        honey-voice-badger) after net honey-badger-opus ;;
        honey-voice-fox) after net honey-codex ;;
        *) after net ;;
    esac
}

start_pre() {
    checkpath -f -m 0640 -o "${honey_voice_user}:${honey_voice_group}" "${output_log}"
}
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC text :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-voice/honey-voice.conf.template :padline no
# Copy to /etc/conf.d/honey-voice-{badger,fox}; keep this file machine-local.
honey_voice_user=
honey_voice_group=
honey_voice_engine_root=
honey_voice_ductor_home=
honey_voice_rollout_home=
honey_voice_bot=
honey_voice_provider=
honey_voice_read_as=
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-voice/openrc/honey-voice-install :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash" :tangle-mode (identity #o755)
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
umask 022

LANE="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
ENGINE_ROOT="${HONEY_VOICE_ENGINE_ROOT:-}"
DRY=0
VERB=check
for arg in "$@"; do
    case "$arg" in
        --dry-run) DRY=1 ;;
        install|check) VERB="$arg" ;;
        -h|--help) printf '%s\n' 'honey-voice-install [--dry-run] [check|install]' ; exit 0 ;;
        *) printf 'honey-voice-install: unknown argument: %s\n' "$arg" >&2; exit 2 ;;
    esac
done
[ -n "$ENGINE_ROOT" ] || { echo 'set HONEY_VOICE_ENGINE_ROOT to the existing private honey-voice engine' >&2; exit 2; }
SRC_WATCHER="$LANE/bin/provider-rollout-watcher.py"
SRC_OPENRC="$LANE/openrc/honey-voice"
for path in "$SRC_WATCHER" "$SRC_OPENRC"; do [ -r "$path" ] || { echo "missing $path" >&2; exit 1; }; done
targets=(
    "$SRC_WATCHER|$ENGINE_ROOT/bin/provider-rollout-watcher.py"
    "$SRC_OPENRC|/etc/init.d/honey-voice-badger"
)
for pair in "${targets[@]}"; do
    src="${pair%%|*}"; dst="${pair#*|}"
    if cmp -s "$src" "$dst" 2>/dev/null; then echo "unchanged $dst"; continue; fi
    if [ "$VERB" = check ]; then echo "drift $dst"; continue; fi
    if [ "$DRY" -eq 1 ]; then echo "would: backup/install $dst"; continue; fi
    [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ] || { echo 'install requires root' >&2; exit 1; }
    ts="$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"
    [ -e "$dst" ] && cp -p -- "$dst" "$dst.bak-$ts"
    install -D -m 0755 -o root -g root -- "$src" "$dst"
    echo "installed $dst"
done
echo 'No service was started, restarted, or enabled.'
#+END_SRC

The fresh-host path is deliberately explicit: tangle =agents-voice.org= and =agents-bots-honey.org=, set =HONEY_VOICE_ENGINE_ROOT=...= to the existing private Piper engine, run =honey-voice-install --dry-run check=, then =--dry-run install= and review the exact four targets. Create the two machine-local =/etc/conf.d= files from =honey-voice.conf.template=, set the Badger and Fox provider/home values, verify with =honey-voice-install check= and =sh -n=, then add only the intended services to the actual OpenRC runlevel and start them one at a time. Test with a no-send render, a synthetic fixture watcher run, and one real foreground response; never replay rollout history. Rollback is the timestamped per-file backup, followed by the service status check; the installer itself never bounces a working bot.

** Switchover (not applied; each step is Mètsàtron's word, on Honey as =gille=)

Nothing in this module has changed Honey. Until the switchover the installed =/etc/init.d/honey-*= scripts run =~agent-claude/DotCortex/honey/.local/bin/honey-<bot>=, so *do not pull =agent-claude='s checkout before step 5*: a pull deletes those launchers (the running bots survive, but their next start fails). Rollback at any step: copy the =.bak-<timestamp>= script back over =/etc/init.d/honey-<bot>= and restart that bot.

1. *Dry-run the clone* from any up-to-date checkout (for example =~/DotCortex= after it has pulled the merge): =~/DotCortex/all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/openrc/honey-claude-install --dry-run sync=.
2. *Clone* (root, once): the same with =sudo= and without =--dry-run=. =/usr/local/share/dotcortex= already exists (the Ductor seams). Check: =sudo find /usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex/all \( ! -user root -o -perm /022 \) ! -type l -print -quit= prints nothing.
3. *Dry-run the install* from the system checkout: =/usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex/all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/openrc/honey-claude-install --dry-run= must report no problems and three =would: install= lines.
4. *Install*: the same with =sudo= and without =--dry-run=. The old scripts are backed up; nothing restarts.
5. *Bounce one bot at a time* in a quiet window, Shoukichi first: =sudo rc-service honey-haiku restart=, then =rc-service honey-haiku status=, =su -l agent-claude -c 'tmux attach -t honey-haiku'= (UI renders; =Ctrl-b d=) and a Telegram round trip. Then Sasuke, then Bunta.
6. *Working directories*: the identity and boundary =CLAUDE.md= copies in =~agent-claude/honey-work/<bot>/= are not touched by the move. Run =honey-claude-install --dry-run workdirs=, then without =--dry-run=: it copies only on a difference and backs up what it replaces.
7. *Afterwards*: =agent-claude='s own checkout no longer runs the bots. It still feeds =agent-guix-apply= and =agent-cli-install=, so it stays, and it may be pulled again.
8. *Updates from then on*: =sudo /usr/local/share/dotcortex/DotCortex/all/.local/share/dotcortex/honey-claude/openrc/honey-claude-install sync= (dry-run first), re-run steps 3-4 if the body changed, run =workdirs= if a =CLAUDE.<bot>.md= changed, and restart the bots whose lane files changed (a changed =CLAUDE.md= loads only at a session start).

* Identity and boundary law for the bots' working directories

Each bot starts in =~/honey-work/<bot>= (in =agent-claude='s home). Its =CLAUDE.md= is a copy of =CLAUDE.<bot>.md=, installed by =honey-claude-install workdirs= (below): the identity first (the kikin brain layout: =# CLAUDE.md - <Name>=, =## Identity=, =## Telegram=), then the Honey boundary law, then the shared reach-and-context block, so the identity and the boundary are the first things every session loads. They carry no IP addresses and no names of clients, and no secrets, chat ids or user ids (this is a public repository: placeholders only).
